namespace Infragistics.Documents.Core
{
	internal static class StringUtilities
	{
		internal static readonly bool IsNls;

		public static StringComparison StringComparisonInvariantCultureIgnoreCase => StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase;

		public static StringComparer StringComparerInvariantCultureIgnoreCase => StringComparer.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase;

		public static StringComparison StringComparisonInvariantCulture => StringComparison.InvariantCulture;

		public static StringComparer StringComparerInvariantCulture => StringComparer.InvariantCulture;

		public static StringComparer StringComparerOrdinal => StringComparer.Ordinal;

		public static StringComparer StringComparerOrdinalIgnoreCase => StringComparer.OrdinalIgnoreCase;

		static StringUtilities()
		{
			string text = "This\r\nThat";
			IsNls = text.IndexOf("\n") > 0;
		}

		public static int StringCompare(string s1, string s2, CultureInfo cultureInfo, CompareOptions compareOptions)
		{
			return string.Compare(s1, s2, cultureInfo, compareOptions);
		}

		public static char ToLower(char ch, CultureInfo cultureInfo)
		{
			return char.ToLower(ch, cultureInfo);
		}

		public static char ToUpper(char ch, CultureInfo cultureInfo)
		{
			return char.ToUpper(ch, cultureInfo);
		}
	}
}
namespace Infragistics.Win
{
	[ToolboxItem(false)]
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public class DropDownListBox : ListBox
	{
		private bool wasItemClicked;

		private bool populating;

		public bool WasItemClicked => wasItemClicked;

		public DropDownListBox(object[] items, object defaultItem, bool sorted)
		{
			base.BorderStyle = BorderStyle.None;
			base.Visible = true;
			base.Sorted = sorted;
			base.IntegralHeight = false;
			Populate(items, defaultItem);
		}

		public DropDownListBox(object[] items, object defaultItem)
			: this(items, defaultItem, sorted: true)
		{
		}

		protected override bool IsInputKey(Keys keyData)
		{
			if (keyData == Keys.Return)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return base.IsInputKey(keyData);
		}

		protected override void OnClick(EventArgs e)
		{
			if (!populating)
			{
				wasItemClicked = true;
			}
			base.OnClick(e);
		}

		protected override void OnKeyDown(KeyEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.KeyCode == Keys.Return)
			{
				wasItemClicked = true;
				OnClick(EventArgs.Empty);
			}
			else
			{
				base.OnKeyDown(e);
			}
		}

		protected override void OnHandleCreated(EventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnHandleCreated(e);
			if (base.PreferredHeight < base.Height)
			{
				base.Height = base.PreferredHeight;
			}
		}

		private void Populate(object[] items, object defaultItem)
		{
			if (items != null)
			{
				populating = true;
				foreach (object item in items)
				{
					base.Items.Add(item);
				}
				if (defaultItem != null)
				{
					SelectedIndex = base.Items.IndexOf(defaultItem);
				}
				populating = false;
			}
		}
	}
}
namespace Infragistics.Win.CalcEngine
{
	internal static class MathUtilities
	{
		private static readonly string NonScientificFormatCode = "0." + new string('#', 324);

		private static double[] powersOf10 = new double[16]
		{
			1.0, 10.0, 100.0, 1000.0, 10000.0, 100000.0, 1000000.0, 10000000.0, 100000000.0, 1000000000.0,
			10000000000.0, 100000000000.0, 1000000000000.0, 10000000000000.0, 100000000000000.0, 1000000000000000.0
		};

		public static CultureInfo CurrentCulture => Thread.CurrentThread.CurrentCulture;

		public static string DecimalToStringNonScientific(decimal value, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return value.ToString(culture);
		}

		public static string DoubleToStringNonScientific(double value, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			return value.ToString(NonScientificFormatCode, culture);
		}

		public static bool DecimalTryParse(string text, IFormatProvider provider, out decimal value)
		{
			if (UltraCalcValue.UseExcelValueCompatibility)
			{
				if (!decimal.TryParse(text, NumberStyles.Float | NumberStyles.AllowParentheses | NumberStyles.AllowThousands | NumberStyles.AllowCurrencySymbol, provider, out value))
				{
					return false;
				}
				return EnforceExcelGroupingRules(text, provider, ref value);
			}
			return decimal.TryParse(text, NumberStyles.Float | NumberStyles.AllowThousands, provider, out value);
		}

		public static bool DoubleTryParse(string text, IFormatProvider provider, out double value)
		{
			if (UltraCalcValue.UseExcelValueCompatibility)
			{
				if (DoubleTryParseFast(text, out value))
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (!double.TryParse(text, NumberStyles.Float | NumberStyles.AllowParentheses | NumberStyles.AllowThousands | NumberStyles.AllowCurrencySymbol, provider, out value))
				{
					return false;
				}
				return EnforceExcelGroupingRules(text, provider, ref value);
			}
			return double.TryParse(text, NumberStyles.Float | NumberStyles.AllowThousands, provider, out value);
		}

		private static bool DoubleTryParseFast(string text, out double value)
		{
			value = 0.0;
			int length = text.Length;
			if (length == 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < length; i++)
			{
				char c = text[i];
				if (c < '0' || '9' < c)
				{
					return false;
				}
				value *= 10.0;
				value += c - 48;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private static bool EnforceExcelGroupingRules<T>(string text, IFormatProvider provider, ref T value)
		{
			text = text.Trim();
			CompareInfo compareInfo = ((!(provider is CultureInfo cultureInfo)) ? CurrentCulture.CompareInfo : cultureInfo.CompareInfo);
			NumberFormatInfo numberFormatInfo = ((NumberFormatInfo)provider.GetFormat(typeof(NumberFormatInfo))) ?? CurrentCulture.NumberFormat;
			string text2 = text;
			int num = compareInfo.IndexOf(text, numberFormatInfo.NumberDecimalSeparator, CompareOptions.IgnoreCase);
			if (0 <= num)
			{
				text2 = text.Substring(0, num);
			}
			else
			{
				int num2 = compareInfo.IndexOf(text, "E", CompareOptions.IgnoreCase);
				if (0 <= num2)
				{
					text2 = text.Substring(0, num2);
				}
			}
			for (int num3 = text2.Length - 1; num3 >= 0; num3--)
			{
				bool flag = false;
				if (compareInfo.Compare(text2, num3, numberFormatInfo.NumberGroupSeparator.Length, numberFormatInfo.NumberGroupSeparator, 0, numberFormatInfo.NumberGroupSeparator.Length, CompareOptions.IgnoreCase) == 0)
				{
					flag = true;
				}
				else
				{
					char c = text2[num3];
					if (char.IsDigit(c))
					{
						flag = true;
					}
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					text2 = text2.Substring(0, num3 + 1);
					break;
				}
			}
			string[] array = text2.Split(new string[1] { numberFormatInfo.NumberGroupSeparator }, StringSplitOptions.None);
			int num4 = numberFormatInfo.NumberGroupSizes[0];
			for (int i = 1; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				if (array[i].Length < num4)
				{
					value = default(T);
					return false;
				}
			}
			return true;
		}

		public static int FloorOfLog10(double value)
		{
			return (int)Math.Floor(Math.Log10(value));
		}

		public static float MidpointRoundingAwayFromZero(float value)
		{
			return (float)MidpointRoundingAwayFromZero(value, 0);
		}

		public static double MidpointRoundingAwayFromZero(double value)
		{
			return MidpointRoundingAwayFromZero(value, 0);
		}

		public static double MidpointRoundingAwayFromZero(double value, int digits)
		{
			return MidpointRoundingAwayFromZeroHelper(value, digits, shouldRoundShiftedValue: true);
		}

		private static double MidpointRoundingAwayFromZeroHelper(double value, int digits, bool shouldRoundShiftedValue)
		{
			if (double.IsNaN(value) || double.IsInfinity(value))
			{
				return value;
			}
			double num = ((digits >= 0 && digits <= 15) ? powersOf10[digits] : Math.Pow(10.0, digits));
			int num2 = Math.Sign(value);
			double num3 = value * num;
			if (shouldRoundShiftedValue)
			{
				num3 = RoundToExcelDisplayValue(num3);
			}
			double value2 = num3 % 1.0;
			num3 = Truncate(num3);
			if (Math.Abs(value2) >= 0.5)
			{
				num3 += (double)Math.Sign(value2);
			}
			return num3 / num;
		}

		public static double RoundToExcelDisplayValue(double value)
		{
			if (value == 0.0)
			{
				return value;
			}
			int num = 14 - FloorOfLog10(Math.Abs(value));
			if (num > 0)
			{
				return MidpointRoundingAwayFromZeroHelper(value, num, shouldRoundShiftedValue: false);
			}
			return value;
		}

		public static double Truncate(double value)
		{
			return Math.Truncate(value);
		}

		internal static double Truncate(double value, int digits)
		{
			if (double.IsNaN(value) || double.IsInfinity(value))
			{
				return value;
			}
			double num = ((digits >= 0 && digits <= 15) ? powersOf10[digits] : Math.Pow(10.0, digits));
			int num2 = Math.Sign(value);
			double value2 = value * num;
			value2 = Truncate(value2);
			return value2 / num;
		}
	}
	public class UltraCalcValueException : UltraCalcException
	{
		public UltraCalcValueException()
		{
		}

		public UltraCalcValueException(string message)
			: base(message)
		{
		}

		public UltraCalcValueException(string message, Exception innerException)
			: base(message, innerException)
		{
		}
	}

	public struct UltraCalcFormulaStates
	{
		private int formulaStates;

		public bool RecalcVisible
		{
			get
			{
				return (formulaStates & 1) != 0;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value)
				{
					formulaStates |= 1;
				}
				else
				{
					formulaStates &= -2;
				}
			}
		}

		public bool RecalcDeferred
		{
			get
			{
				return (formulaStates & 2) != 0;
			}
			set
			{
				if (value)
				{
					formulaStates |= 2;
				}
				else
				{
					formulaStates &= -3;
				}
			}
		}

		public void Reset()
		{
			formulaStates = 0;
		}
	}
	public class UltraCalcReferenceError : IUltraCalcReference

		private string reference;

	public class UltraCalcException : Exception
	{
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		public static readonly object LocationPlaceholder = new object();

		private int column;

		private int line;

		private object[] messageParams;

		private string messageRaw;

		internal int Column => column;

		internal string ErrorLocationRaw => Utilities.GetString("Error_Location");

		internal bool HasDynamicMessage => messageRaw != null;

		internal int Line => line;

		internal object[] MessageParams => messageParams;

		internal string MessageRaw => messageRaw;

		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		public UltraCalcException(string messageRaw, int line, int column, object[] messageParams)
			: base(ConstructMessage(messageRaw, line, column, messageParams))
		{
			this.messageRaw = messageRaw;
			this.line = line;
			this.column = column;
			this.messageParams = messageParams;
		}

		private static string ConstructMessage(string messageRaw, int line, int column, object[] messageParams)
		{
			object[] array = new object[messageParams.Length];
			for (int i = 0; i < messageParams.Length; i++)
			{
				object obj = messageParams[i];
				if (obj == LocationPlaceholder)
				{
					array[i] = Utilities.GetString("Error_Location", line, column);
				}
				else
				{
					array[i] = obj;
				}
			}
			return string.Format(messageRaw, array);
		}

		public UltraCalcException()
		{
		}

		public UltraCalcException(string message)
			: base(message)
		{
		}

		public UltraCalcException(string message, Exception innerException)
			: base(message, innerException)
		{
		}
	}
	public class UltraCalcValue : IComparable, IConvertible
	{
		private const double OffsetFor1904DateSystem = 1462.0;

		private const double MinOaDate = -657435.0;

		private const double MaxOaDate = 2958466.0;

		internal static readonly UltraCalcValue Empty = new UltraCalcValue();

		private object value;

		private static bool useExcelFormulaCompatibility = false;

		public static bool UseExcelValueCompatibility = false;

		public static bool UseExcelFunctionCompatibility = false;

		public bool IsReference
		{
			get
			{
				if (!(value is IUltraCalcReference))
				{
					return false;
				}
				UltraCalcErrorValue errorValue;
				return ToReference(willUseValueInCalculations: false, out errorValue) != null;
			}
		}

		public bool IsError => IsErrorHelper(GetResolvedValue(willUseValueInCalculations: false));

		public bool IsNull
		{
			get
			{
				object resolvedValue = GetResolvedValue(willUseValueInCalculations: false);
				return IsNullHelper(resolvedValue);
			}
		}

		public bool IsDBNull
		{
			get
			{
				object resolvedValue = GetResolvedValue(willUseValueInCalculations: false);
				return resolvedValue == DBNull.Value;
			}
		}

		public bool IsString
		{
			get
			{
				object resolvedValue = GetResolvedValue(willUseValueInCalculations: false);
				return IsStringHelper(resolvedValue);
			}
		}

		public bool IsBoolean => IsBooleanHelper(GetResolvedValue(willUseValueInCalculations: false));

		public bool IsDateTime
		{
			get
			{
				object resolvedValue = GetResolvedValue(willUseValueInCalculations: false);
				if (resolvedValue is IConvertible)
				{
					return TypeCode.DateTime == ((IConvertible)resolvedValue).GetTypeCode();
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public object Value => value;

		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		public static bool UseExcelFormulaCompatibility
		{
			get
			{
				return useExcelFormulaCompatibility;
			}
			set
			{
				useExcelFormulaCompatibility = value;
				UseExcelValueCompatibility = value;
				UseExcelFunctionCompatibility = value;
			}
		}

		internal CultureInfo Culture
		{
			get
			{
				if (UseExcelValueCompatibility)
				{
					return Utilities.CurrentCulture;
				}
				return CultureInfo.InvariantCulture;
			}
		}

		private IUltraCalcReference ToReference(bool willUseValueInCalculations, out UltraCalcErrorValue errorValue)
		{
			errorValue = null;
			return ToReferenceHelper();
		}

		public UltraCalcValue(object value)
		{
			Initialize(value);
		}

		internal void Initialize(object value)
		{
			if (value is Exception)
			{
				ExceptionHandler((Exception)value);
			}
			else
			{
				this.value = value;
			}
		}

		public UltraCalcValue()
			: this(null)
		{
		}

		public int ToInt()
		{
			return ToInt(Culture);
		}

		public int ToInt(IFormatProvider provider)
		{
			return (int)ToDecimal(provider);
		}

		private void ExceptionHandler(Exception e)
		{
			if (e is UltraCalcErrorException)
			{
				value = ((UltraCalcErrorException)e).Value;
			}
			else if (e is DivideByZeroException)
			{
				value = new UltraCalcErrorValue(UltraCalcErrorCode.Div, e.Message, e);
			}
			else if (e is ArithmeticException)
			{
				value = new UltraCalcErrorValue(UltraCalcErrorCode.Num, e.Message, e);
			}
			else if (e is InvalidCastException)
			{
				value = new UltraCalcErrorValue(UltraCalcErrorCode.Num, e.Message, e);
			}
			else if (e is FormatException)
			{
				value = new UltraCalcErrorValue(UltraCalcErrorCode.Value, e.Message, e);
			}
			else if (e is UltraCalcValueException)
			{
				value = new UltraCalcErrorValue(UltraCalcErrorCode.Value, e.Message, e);
			}
			else if (e is UltraCalcNumberException)
			{
				value = new UltraCalcErrorValue(UltraCalcErrorCode.Num, e.Message, e);
			}
			else
			{
				value = new UltraCalcErrorValue(UltraCalcErrorCode.Value, e.Message, e);
			}
		}

		public IUltraCalcReference ToReference()
		{
			UltraCalcErrorValue errorValue;
			IUltraCalcReference ultraCalcReference = ToReference(willUseValueInCalculations: true, out errorValue);
			if (ultraCalcReference != null)
			{
				return ultraCalcReference;
			}
			return CreateReferenceError();
		}

		internal IUltraCalcReference ToReference(bool willUseValueInCalculations)
		{
			UltraCalcErrorValue errorValue;
			return ToReference(willUseValueInCalculations, out errorValue);
		}

		private IUltraCalcReference ToReferenceHelper()
		{
			if (value is UCReference)
			{
				return ((UCReference)value).Reference;
			}
			if (value is IUltraCalcReference)
			{
				return (IUltraCalcReference)value;
			}
			if (value is UltraCalcErrorValue)
			{
				((UltraCalcErrorValue)value).RaiseException();
			}
			return CreateReferenceError();
		}

		private static IUltraCalcReference CreateReferenceError()
		{
			return new UltraCalcReferenceError(SR.GetString("Value_UCErrorCode_Reference"), SR.GetString("Error_InvalidReference"));
		}

		public UltraCalcErrorValue ToErrorValue()
		{
			object resolvedValue = GetResolvedValue();
			return resolvedValue as UltraCalcErrorValue;
		}

		public override string ToString()
		{
			return ToString(Culture);
		}

		internal static bool IsErrorHelper(object resolvedValue)
		{
			return resolvedValue is UltraCalcErrorValue;
		}

		internal static bool IsNullHelper(object resolvedValue)
		{
			if (Utilities.IsNullResolved(resolvedValue))
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (UseExcelValueCompatibility)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (resolvedValue is string text)
			{
				return text.Length == 0;
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal static bool IsStringHelper(object resolvedValue)
		{
			if (resolvedValue is string)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (resolvedValue is IConvertible)
			{
				return TypeCode.String == ((IConvertible)resolvedValue).GetTypeCode();
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal static bool IsBooleanHelper(object resolvedValue)
		{
			if (resolvedValue is IConvertible)
			{
				return TypeCode.Boolean == ((IConvertible)resolvedValue).GetTypeCode();
			}
			return false;
		}

		public static bool AreValuesEqual(UltraCalcValue x, UltraCalcValue y)
		{
			if (x == null)
			{
				return y == null;
			}
			if (y == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			object resolvedValue = x.GetResolvedValue();
			object resolvedValue2 = y.GetResolvedValue();
			if (UseExcelValueCompatibility && x.IsString && y.IsString)
			{
				string s = resolvedValue.ToString();
				string s2 = resolvedValue2.ToString();
				if (StringUtilities.StringCompare(s, s2, x.Culture, CompareOptions.IgnoreCase) == 0)
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			if (resolvedValue is IComparable && resolvedValue2 is IComparable && resolvedValue.GetType() == resolvedValue2.GetType())
			{
				return ((IComparable)resolvedValue).CompareTo(resolvedValue2) == 0;
			}
			if (UseExcelValueCompatibility)
			{
				if (resolvedValue == null)
				{
					if (resolvedValue2 == null || (resolvedValue2 is string && (string)resolvedValue2 == string.Empty) || (resolvedValue2 is bool && !(bool)resolvedValue2))
					{
						return true;
					}
				}
				else if (resolvedValue2 == null && ((resolvedValue is string && (string)resolvedValue == string.Empty) || (resolvedValue is bool && !(bool)resolvedValue)))
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (x.IsBoolean != y.IsBoolean)
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (x.IsString != y.IsString)
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
			if (x.ToDouble(out var result) && y.ToDouble(out var result2))
			{
				return result == result2;
			}
			return string.Equals(x.ToString(), y.ToString());
		}

		int IComparable.CompareTo(object value)
		{
			return CompareTo((UltraCalcValue)value);
		}

		public int CompareTo(UltraCalcValue value)
		{
			return CompareTo(this, value);
		}

		public static int CompareTo(UltraCalcValue x, UltraCalcValue y)
		{
			if (x == y)
			{
				return 0;
			}
			if (x == null)
			{
				return -1;
			}
			if (y == null)
			{
				return 1;
			}
			return CompareToHelper(x, x.GetResolvedValue(), y, y.GetResolvedValue());
		}

		internal static int CompareToHelper(UltraCalcValue x, object resolvedXValue, UltraCalcValue y, object resolvedYValue)
		{
			CultureInfo culture = x.Culture;
			if (UseExcelValueCompatibility)
			{
				bool flag = IsBooleanHelper(resolvedXValue);
				bool flag2 = IsBooleanHelper(resolvedYValue);
				if (flag && !flag2)
				{
					return 1;
				}
				if (flag2 && !flag)
				{
					return -1;
				}
				bool flag3 = IsStringHelper(resolvedXValue);
				bool flag4 = IsStringHelper(resolvedYValue);
				if (flag3 && !flag4)
				{
					return 1;
				}
				if (flag4 && !flag3)
				{
					return -1;
				}
				if (flag3 && flag4)
				{
					string strA = x.ToStringHelper(resolvedXValue, culture);
					string strB = y.ToStringHelper(resolvedYValue, culture);
					return string.Compare(strA, strB);
				}
			}
			if (x.ToDoubleHelper(resolvedXValue, out var result, culture, out var exception) && y.ToDoubleHelper(resolvedYValue, out var result2, culture, out exception))
			{
				result = MathUtilities.RoundToExcelDisplayValue(result);
				result2 = MathUtilities.RoundToExcelDisplayValue(result2);
				return result.CompareTo(result2);
			}
			if (resolvedXValue != null && resolvedYValue != null && resolvedXValue.GetType() == resolvedYValue.GetType() && resolvedXValue is IComparable comparable)
			{
				return comparable.CompareTo(resolvedYValue);
			}
			string strA2 = x.ToStringHelper(resolvedXValue, culture);
			string strB2 = y.ToStringHelper(resolvedYValue, culture);
			return string.Compare(strA2, strB2);
		}

		public bool IsSameValue(UltraCalcValue value)
		{
			return AreValuesEqual(this, value);
		}

		internal bool IsTrue(IFormatProvider provider)
		{
			object resolvedValue = GetResolvedValue(willUseValueInCalculations: true, raiseExceptionOnError: false);
			if (resolvedValue is bool)
			{
				return (bool)resolvedValue;
			}
			if (IsErrorHelper(resolvedValue) || IsNullHelper(resolvedValue))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (IsStringHelper(resolvedValue))
			{
				string text = ToStringHelper(resolvedValue, provider);
				if (bool.TryParse(text, out var result))
				{
					return result;
				}
			}
			if (ToDoubleHelper(resolvedValue, out var result2, provider, out var _))
			{
				return result2 != 0.0;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public object GetResolvedValue()
		{
			return GetResolvedValue(willUseValueInCalculations: true, raiseExceptionOnError: false);
		}

		public double ToDouble()
		{
			return ToDouble(Culture);
		}

		public double ToDouble(IFormatProvider provider)
		{
			if (ToDouble(out var result, provider, out var exception))
			{
				return result;
			}
			throw exception ?? new InvalidCastException();
		}

		public bool ToDouble(out double result)
		{
			Exception exception;
			return ToDouble(out result, Culture, out exception);
		}

		private bool ToDouble(out double result, IFormatProvider provider, out Exception exception)
		{
			object resolvedValue = GetResolvedValue();
			return ToDoubleHelper(resolvedValue, out result, provider, out exception);
		}

		internal bool ToDoubleHelper(object resolvedValue, out double result, IFormatProvider provider, out Exception exception)
		{
			exception = null;
			if (resolvedValue is double)
			{
				result = (double)resolvedValue;
				return true;
			}
			if (IsNullHelper(resolvedValue))
			{
				result = 0.0;
				return true;
			}
			if (resolvedValue is string)
			{
				if (MathUtilities.DoubleTryParse((string)resolvedValue, provider, out result))
				{
					return true;
				}
				exception = new FormatException();
				return false;
			}
			if (UseExcelValueCompatibility && resolvedValue is DateTime)
			{
				double? num = DateTimeToExcelDate((DateTime)resolvedValue);
				if (!num.HasValue)
				{
					exception = new ArgumentOutOfRangeException();
					result = 0.0;
					return false;
				}
				result = num.Value;
				return true;
			}
			try
			{
				if (!UseExcelValueCompatibility && resolvedValue is DateTime)
				{
					result = 0.0;
					return false;
				}
				if (resolvedValue is IConvertible convertible)
				{
					result = convertible.ToDouble(provider);
					return true;
				}
				result = 0.0;
				return false;
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				exception = ex;
				result = 0.0;
				return false;
			}
		}

		public DateTime ToDateTime()
		{
			return ToDateTime(Culture);
		}

		public DateTime ToDateTime(IFormatProvider provider)
		{
			if (!ToDateTimeHelper(provider, out var dateTime, useTryParseForString: false))
			{
				throw new InvalidCastException();
			}
			return dateTime;
		}

		internal bool ToDateTimeHelper(IFormatProvider provider, out DateTime dateTime, bool useTryParseForString = true, bool allowZero = false)
		{
			object resolvedValue = GetResolvedValue(willUseValueInCalculations: true, raiseExceptionOnError: true);
			if (resolvedValue is DateTime)
			{
				dateTime = (DateTime)resolvedValue;
				return true;
			}
			if (UseExcelValueCompatibility && ToDouble(out var result, provider, out var _))
			{
				DateTime? dateTime2 = ExcelDateToDateTime(result, shouldCorrect1900Dates: true, allowZero && result >= 0.0 && result < 1.0);
				if (!dateTime2.HasValue)
				{
					dateTime = DateTime.MinValue;
					return false;
				}
				dateTime = dateTime2.Value;
				return true;
			}
			if (resolvedValue is long)
			{
				try
				{
					dateTime = new DateTime((long)resolvedValue);
					return true;
				}
				catch (ArgumentOutOfRangeException)
				{
					dateTime = DateTime.MinValue;
					return false;
				}
			}
			if (useTryParseForString && resolvedValue is string)
			{
				return DateTime.TryParse((string)resolvedValue, provider, DateTimeStyles.None, out dateTime);
			}
			if (resolvedValue is IConvertible)
			{
				dateTime = ((IConvertible)resolvedValue).ToDateTime(provider);
				return true;
			}
			dateTime = DateTime.MinValue;
			return false;
		}

		public float ToSingle()
		{
			return ToSingle(Culture);
		}

		public float ToSingle(IFormatProvider provider)
		{
			return (float)ToDouble(provider);
		}

		public bool ToBoolean()
		{
			return ToBoolean(Culture);
		}

		public bool ToBoolean(IFormatProvider provider)
		{
			object resolvedValue = GetResolvedValue(willUseValueInCalculations: true, raiseExceptionOnError: true);
			if (resolvedValue is bool)
			{
				return (bool)resolvedValue;
			}
			if (IsNullHelper(resolvedValue))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (resolvedValue is string text)
			{
				if (bool.TryParse(text, out var result))
				{
					return result;
				}
				if (UseExcelValueCompatibility)
				{
					throw new FormatException();
				}
			}
			return ToDouble(provider) != 0.0;
		}

		public int ToInt32()
		{
			return ToInt32(Culture);
		}

		public int ToInt32(IFormatProvider provider)
		{
			return (int)ToDecimal(provider);
		}

		public short ToInt16()
		{
			return ToInt16(Culture);
		}

		public short ToInt16(IFormatProvider provider)
		{
			return (short)ToDecimal(provider);
		}

		public string ToString(IFormatProvider provider)
		{
			object resolvedValue = GetResolvedValue();
			return ToStringHelper(resolvedValue, provider);
		}

		internal string ToStringHelper(object resolvedValue, IFormatProvider provider)
		{
			if (UseExcelValueCompatibility)
			{
				if (resolvedValue is bool flag)
				{
					return flag.ToString(provider).ToUpper();
				}
				if (resolvedValue is DateTime && ToDoubleHelper(resolvedValue, out var result, provider, out var _))
				{
					return result.ToString(provider);
				}
			}
			if (resolvedValue is IConvertible)
			{
				return ((IConvertible)resolvedValue).ToString(provider);
			}
			if (resolvedValue is UltraCalcErrorValue)
			{
				return ((UltraCalcErrorValue)resolvedValue).ToString();
			}
			if (resolvedValue is UltraCalcErrorException)
			{
				return ((UltraCalcErrorException)resolvedValue).ToString();
			}
			if (resolvedValue == null)
			{
				return "";
			}
			return resolvedValue.ToString();
		}

		public byte ToByte()
		{
			return ToByte(Culture);
		}

		public byte ToByte(IFormatProvider provider)
		{
			return (byte)ToDecimal(provider);
		}

		public char ToChar()
		{
			return ToChar(Culture);
		}

		public char ToChar(IFormatProvider provider)
		{
			object resolvedValue = GetResolvedValue(willUseValueInCalculations: true, raiseExceptionOnError: true);
			if (resolvedValue is IConvertible convertible)
			{
				return convertible.ToChar(provider);
			}
			throw new InvalidCastException();
		}

		public long ToInt64()
		{
			return ToInt64(Culture);
		}

		public long ToInt64(IFormatProvider provider)
		{
			if (!UseExcelValueCompatibility && GetResolvedValue(willUseValueInCalculations: true, raiseExceptionOnError: true) is DateTime dateTime)
			{
				return dateTime.Ticks;
			}
			return (long)ToDecimal(provider);
		}

		public decimal ToDecimal()
		{
			return ToDecimal(Culture);
		}

		public bool ToDecimal(out decimal result)
		{
			Exception exception;
			return ToDecimalHelper(out result, Culture, out exception);
		}

		public decimal ToDecimal(IFormatProvider provider)
		{
			if (ToDecimalHelper(out var result, provider, out var exception))
			{
				return result;
			}
			if (exception == null)
			{
				throw new InvalidCastException();
			}
			throw exception;
		}

		private bool ToDecimalHelper(out decimal result, IFormatProvider provider, out Exception exception)
		{
			exception = null;
			object resolvedValue = GetResolvedValue(willUseValueInCalculations: true, raiseExceptionOnError: true);
			if (IsNullHelper(resolvedValue))
			{
				result = default(decimal);
				return true;
			}
			if (UseExcelValueCompatibility && resolvedValue is DateTime)
			{
				double? num = DateTimeToExcelDate((DateTime)resolvedValue);
				if (!num.HasValue)
				{
					exception = new ArgumentOutOfRangeException();
					result = default(decimal);
					return false;
				}
				result = (decimal)num.Value;
				return true;
			}
			if (resolvedValue is IConvertible)
			{
				try
				{
					result = ((IConvertible)resolvedValue).ToDecimal(provider);
					return true;
				}
				catch (FormatException ex)
				{
					if (UseExcelValueCompatibility && resolvedValue is string text && MathUtilities.DecimalTryParse(text, provider, out var num2))
					{
						result = num2;
						return true;
					}
					exception = ex;
					result = default(decimal);
					return false;
				}
			}
			result = default(decimal);
			return false;
		}

		public static double? DateTimeToExcelDate(DateTime dateValue)
		{
			return DateTimeToExcelDate(dateValue, shouldCorrect1900Dates: true);
		}

		public static double? DateTimeToExcelDate(DateTime dateValue, bool shouldCorrect1900Dates)
		{
			return DateTimeToExcelDate(dateValue, shouldCorrect1900Dates);
		}

		internal static double? DateTimeToExcelDate(DateTime dateValue, bool shouldCorrect1900Dates, bool adjustFor1904System)
		{
			if (dateValue.Ticks == 0L)
			{
				return null;
			}
			double num;
			try
			{
				num = dateValue.ToOADate();
			}
			catch
			{
				return null;
			}
			bool flag = shouldCorrect1900Dates;
			bool flag2 = false;
			if (flag && dateValue < new DateTime(1900, 3, 1))
			{
				num -= 1.0;
			}
			if (!flag2 && num == 0.0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (num < 0.0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return num;
		}

		public static DateTime? ExcelDateToDateTime(double excelDate)
		{
			return ExcelDateToDateTime(excelDate, shouldCorrect1900Dates: true);
		}

		public static DateTime? ExcelDateToDateTime(double excelDate, bool shouldCorrect1900Dates)
		{
			return ExcelDateToDateTime(excelDate, shouldCorrect1900Dates, allowDateBeforeMinimum: false);
		}

		internal static DateTime? ExcelDateToDateTime(double excelDate, bool shouldCorrect1900Dates, bool allowDateBeforeMinimum)
		{
			bool flag = shouldCorrect1900Dates;
			if (excelDate >= 2958466.0 || excelDate <= -657435.0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			DateTime dateTime;
			try
			{
				dateTime = Utilities.DateTimeFromOADate(excelDate);
			}
			catch
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (flag && dateTime < new DateTime(1900, 3, 1) && excelDate >= 0.0)
			{
				dateTime = dateTime.AddDays(1.0);
			}
			if (!allowDateBeforeMinimum && dateTime < new DateTime(1900, 1, 1))
			{
				return null;
			}
			return dateTime;
		}

		public static TimeSpan ExcelDateToTimeOfDay(double excelDate, bool useDayInfo = false)
		{
			if (!useDayInfo && excelDate >= 0.0)
			{
				excelDate %= 1.0;
			}
			try
			{
				return TimeSpan.FromDays(excelDate);
			}
			catch
			{
				return TimeSpan.Zero;
			}
		}

		public static double TimeOfDayToExcelDate(TimeSpan timeValue, bool useDayInfo = false)
		{
			double num = timeValue.TotalDays;
			if (!useDayInfo && num >= 1.0)
			{
				num %= 1.0;
			}
			return num;
		}

		internal object GetResolvedValue(bool willUseValueInCalculations)
		{
			return GetResolvedValue(willUseValueInCalculations, raiseExceptionOnError: false);
		}

		internal object GetResolvedValue(bool willUseValueInCalculations, bool raiseExceptionOnError)
		{
			return GetResolvedValue(this, willUseValueInCalculations, raiseExceptionOnError);
		}

		private object GetResolvedValue(UltraCalcValue originalValue, bool willUseValueInCalculations, bool raiseExceptionOnError)
		{
			if (value is IUltraCalcReference)
			{
				UltraCalcErrorValue errorValue;
				IUltraCalcReference ultraCalcReference = ToReference(willUseValueInCalculations, out errorValue);
				if (ultraCalcReference == null)
				{
					return errorValue;
				}
				return ultraCalcReference.Value?.GetResolvedValue(originalValue, willUseValueInCalculations, raiseExceptionOnError);
			}
			object obj = value;
			if (raiseExceptionOnError && obj is UltraCalcErrorValue ultraCalcErrorValue)
			{
				ultraCalcErrorValue.RaiseException();
			}
			return obj;
		}

		ulong IConvertible.ToUInt64(IFormatProvider provider)
		{
			return (ulong)ToDecimal(provider);
		}

		sbyte IConvertible.ToSByte(IFormatProvider provider)
		{
			return (sbyte)ToDecimal(provider);
		}

		ushort IConvertible.ToUInt16(IFormatProvider provider)
		{
			return (ushort)ToDecimal(provider);
		}

		uint IConvertible.ToUInt32(IFormatProvider provider)
		{
			return (uint)ToDecimal(provider);
		}

		public TypeCode GetTypeCode()
		{
			object resolvedValue = GetResolvedValue(willUseValueInCalculations: false);
			if (resolvedValue == null || !(resolvedValue is IConvertible))
			{
				return TypeCode.Empty;
			}
			return ((IConvertible)resolvedValue).GetTypeCode();
		}

		public object ToType(Type conversionType, IFormatProvider provider)
		{
			object resolvedValue = GetResolvedValue(willUseValueInCalculations: true, raiseExceptionOnError: true);
			if (resolvedValue is IConvertible)
			{
				return ((IConvertible)resolvedValue).ToType(conversionType, provider);
			}
			throw new InvalidCastException();
		}
	}

		public const RegexOptions RegexOptionsCompiled = RegexOptions.Compiled;

		public static DateTime DateTimeFromOADate(double d)
		{
			return DateTime.FromOADate(d);
		}

		public static void DebugAssert(bool condition, string message)
		{
		}

		public static void DebugFail(string message)
		{
		}

		public static int GetBits(int value, int firstBitIndex, int lastBitIndex)
		{
			int num = lastBitIndex - firstBitIndex + 1;
			int num2 = (1 << num) - 1;
			return (value >> firstBitIndex) & num2;
		}

		public static string GetString(string name)
		{
			return SR.GetString(name);
		}

		public static string GetString(string name, params object[] args)
		{
			return SR.GetString(name, args);
		}

		public static bool IsNullResolved(object value)
		{
			if (value != null)
			{
				return value == DBNull.Value;
			}
			return true;
		}

		public static int StringCompare(string s1, string s2, CultureInfo cultureInfo, CompareOptions compareOptions)
		{
			return string.Compare(s1, s2, cultureInfo, compareOptions);
		}

	public enum UltraCalcErrorCode
	{
		Reference,
		Value,
		Div,
		NA,
		Num,
		Circularity
	}
	public class UltraCalcErrorException : UltraCalcException
	{
		private UltraCalcErrorValue value;

		public UltraCalcErrorValue Value => value;

		public UltraCalcErrorException(UltraCalcErrorValue errorValue)
		{
			value = errorValue;
		}

		public override string ToString()
		{
			return value.ToString();
		}
	}
	public class UltraCalcErrorValue
	{
		private UltraCalcErrorCode errorCode;

		private string errorMessage;

		private object errorValue;

		public UltraCalcErrorCode Code => errorCode;

		public string Message => errorMessage;

		public object ErrorValue => errorValue;

		public UltraCalcErrorValue(UltraCalcErrorCode code)
		{
			errorCode = code;
			switch (code)
			{
			case UltraCalcErrorCode.Div:
				errorMessage = Utilities.GetString("Error_UCErrorCode_Div");
				break;
			case UltraCalcErrorCode.NA:
				errorMessage = Utilities.GetString("Error_UCErrorCode_NA");
				break;
			case UltraCalcErrorCode.Num:
				errorMessage = Utilities.GetString("Error_UCErrorCode_Num");
				break;
			case UltraCalcErrorCode.Reference:
				errorMessage = Utilities.GetString("Error_UCErrorCode_Reference");
				break;
			case UltraCalcErrorCode.Value:
				errorMessage = Utilities.GetString("Error_UCErrorCode_Value");
				break;
			default:
				errorMessage = Utilities.GetString("Error_UCErrorCode_Unknown");
				break;
			}
		}

		public UltraCalcErrorValue(UltraCalcErrorCode code, string message)
		{
			errorCode = code;
			errorMessage = message;
		}

		public UltraCalcErrorValue(UltraCalcErrorCode code, string message, object value)
		{
			errorCode = code;
			errorMessage = message;
			errorValue = value;
		}

		public override string ToString()
		{
			if (errorCode == UltraCalcErrorCode.Div)
			{
				return Utilities.GetString("Value_UCErrorCode_Div");
			}
			if (errorCode == UltraCalcErrorCode.Num)
			{
				return Utilities.GetString("Value_UCErrorCode_Num");
			}
			if (errorCode == UltraCalcErrorCode.NA)
			{
				return Utilities.GetString("Value_UCErrorCode_NA");
			}
			if (errorCode == UltraCalcErrorCode.Value)
			{
				return Utilities.GetString("Value_UCErrorCode_Value");
			}
			if (errorCode == UltraCalcErrorCode.Reference)
			{
				return Utilities.GetString("Value_UCErrorCode_Reference");
			}
			return Utilities.GetString("Value_UCErrorCode_Unknown");
		}

		public void RaiseException()
		{
			if (errorValue is UltraCalcException)
			{
				throw (UltraCalcException)errorValue;
			}
			throw new UltraCalcErrorException(this);
		}
	}

		{
			get

				if (displayName != null)

				return null;
			}
		}

	public abstract class RefBase : IUltraCalcReference
	{
		private UltraCalcFormulaStates formulaStates;

		private RefParser rp;

		public virtual object Context => null;

		public abstract RefBase BaseParent { get; }

		public virtual bool IsAnchored => true;

		public virtual bool IsRange => false;

		public virtual RefBase WrappedReference => this;

		public virtual RefParser ParsedReference
		{
			get
			{
				if (rp == null)
				{
					rp = CreateParsedReference();
				}
				return rp;
			}
			set
			{
				rp = value;
			}
		}

		public virtual RefParser RelativeReference
		{
			get
			{
				return ParsedReference;
			}
			set
			{
				throw new UltraCalcException(SR.GetString("Error_AnchoredReference", GetType().ToString() + " " + ElementName));
			}
		}

		public virtual string AbsoluteName => BaseParent.AbsoluteName + "/" + ElementName;

		public virtual string NormalizedAbsoluteName => ParsedReference.ToString();

		public abstract string ElementName { get; }

		public virtual UltraCalcValue Value
		{
			get
			{
				return new UltraCalcValue(new UltraCalcErrorValue(UltraCalcErrorCode.Value));
			}
			set
			{
				throw new UltraCalcException(SR.GetString("Error_ReadOnlyReference", GetType().ToString() + " " + ElementName));
			}
		}

		public virtual IUltraCalcFormula Formula => null;

		public virtual bool RecalcDeferred
		{
			get
			{
				return formulaStates.RecalcDeferred;
			}
			set
			{
				formulaStates.RecalcDeferred = value;
			}
		}

		public virtual bool RecalcVisible
		{
			get
			{
				return formulaStates.RecalcVisible;
			}
			set
			{
				formulaStates.RecalcVisible = value;
			}
		}

		public virtual bool HasRelativeIndex => false;

		public virtual bool HasAbsoluteIndex => false;

		public virtual bool HasScopeAll => false;

		public virtual IUltraCalcReferenceCollection References => null;

		public virtual bool IsEnumerable => false;

		public virtual IUltraCalcReference Parent => BaseParent;

		public virtual bool IsDataReference => false;

		public virtual bool IsDisposedReference => false;

		public RefBase()
		{
			formulaStates = default(UltraCalcFormulaStates);
		}

		protected virtual RefBase FindRoot()
		{
			return BaseParent.FindRoot();
		}

		public abstract IUltraCalcReference FindItem(string name);

		public abstract IUltraCalcReference FindAll(string name);

		public abstract IUltraCalcReference FindSummaryItem(string name);

		public abstract IUltraCalcReference FindItem(string name, string index);

		public abstract IUltraCalcReference FindItem(string name, int index, bool isRelative);

		public virtual RefBase FindParent()
		{
			return BaseParent;
		}

		public virtual IUltraCalcReferenceCollection ScopedReferences(RefParser scopeRP)
		{
			throw new UltraCalcException(SR.GetString("Error_ReferenceNotEumerable", GetType().ToString() + " " + ElementName));
		}

		protected virtual IUltraCalcReference BuildReference(RefParser buildRP, bool forceDataRef)
		{
			IEnumerator enumerator = buildRP.GetEnumerator();
			return RefLoop(this, enumerator, forceDataRef);
		}

		protected IUltraCalcReference RefLoop(RefBase start, IEnumerator tupleEnum, bool forceDataRef)
		{
			RefTuple refTuple = null;
			IUltraCalcReference ultraCalcReference = start;
			while (tupleEnum.MoveNext())
			{
				RefBase refBase = ultraCalcReference as RefBase;
				refTuple = (RefTuple)tupleEnum.Current;
				switch (refTuple.Type)
				{
				case RefTuple.RefType.Parent:
					if (refTuple.Scope != RefTuple.RefScope.Any)
					{
						return new UltraCalcReferenceError(null, SR.GetString("Error_UnexpectedScope", GetType().ToString() + " " + ElementName, refTuple.Scope));
					}
					ultraCalcReference = refBase.FindParent();
					break;
				case RefTuple.RefType.ExplicitThis:
					return new UltraCalcReferenceError(null, SR.GetString("Error_ExplicitThis", GetType().ToString() + " " + ElementName));
				case RefTuple.RefType.Identifier:
					switch (refTuple.Scope)
					{
					case RefTuple.RefScope.Any:
						ultraCalcReference = refBase.FindItem(refTuple.Name);
						break;
					case RefTuple.RefScope.All:
						if (refTuple.Last)
						{
							ultraCalcReference = refBase.FindAll(refTuple.Name);
							break;
						}
						return new UltraCalcReferenceError(null, SR.GetString("Error_ScopeAllNotLast", GetType().ToString() + " " + ElementName, refTuple.Name));
					case RefTuple.RefScope.SummaryValue:
						ultraCalcReference = refBase.FindSummaryItem(refTuple.Name);
						break;
					case RefTuple.RefScope.Identifier:
						ultraCalcReference = refBase.FindItem(refTuple.Name, refTuple.ScopeID);
						break;
					case RefTuple.RefScope.Index:
						ultraCalcReference = refBase.FindItem(refTuple.Name, refTuple.ScopeIndex, isRelative: false);
						break;
					case RefTuple.RefScope.RelativeIndex:
						ultraCalcReference = refBase.FindItem(refTuple.Name, refTuple.ScopeIndex, isRelative: true);
						break;
					default:
						return new UltraCalcReferenceError(null, SR.GetString("Error_UnknownScope", GetType().ToString() + " " + ElementName, refTuple.Scope));
					}
					break;
				default:
					return new UltraCalcReferenceError(null, SR.GetString("Error_UnknownRefType", GetType().ToString() + " " + ElementName, refTuple.Type));
				}
				if (forceDataRef && !ultraCalcReference.IsDataReference && !(ultraCalcReference is UltraCalcReferenceError))
				{
					ultraCalcReference = refBase.FindItem(refTuple.Name, 0, isRelative: false);
				}
				if (ultraCalcReference is UltraCalcReferenceError)
				{
					return ultraCalcReference;
				}
			}
			return ultraCalcReference;
		}

		public virtual void MarkRelativeIndices(IUltraCalcReference inReference)
		{
			if (inReference is RefBase)
			{
				ParsedReference.MarkRelativeIndices(((RefBase)inReference).ParsedReference, forceLastTuple: false);
			}
		}

		public virtual IUltraCalcReference CreateReference(string inReference)
		{
			try
			{
				RefParser refParser = new RefParser(inReference);
				if (refParser.TupleCount == 0)
				{
					throw new UltraCalcException(SR.GetString("Error_RefMalformed", GetType().ToString() + " " + ElementName, inReference));
				}
				if (refParser.IsFullyQualified || refParser.IsRoot)
				{
					RefBase refBase = FindRoot();
					if (refBase != this)
					{
						return refBase.CreateReference(inReference);
					}
				}
				IUltraCalcReference ultraCalcReference = BuildReference(refParser, forceDataRef: false);
				if (ultraCalcReference is UltraCalcReferenceError)
				{
					return new UltraCalcReferenceError(inReference, ((UltraCalcReferenceError)ultraCalcReference).Message);
				}
				if (ultraCalcReference == null)
				{
					return new UltraCalcReferenceError(inReference);
				}
				RefBase refBase2 = ultraCalcReference as RefBase;
				if (refBase2.IsAnchored)
				{
					refBase2 = new RefUnAnchored(refBase2);
				}
				refBase2.RelativeReference = refParser;
				return refBase2;
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				return new UltraCalcReferenceError(inReference, ex.Message);
			}
		}

		public virtual IUltraCalcReference CreateRange(string fromReference, string toReference)
		{
			try
			{
				IUltraCalcReference ultraCalcReference = CreateReference(fromReference);
				if (ultraCalcReference is UltraCalcReferenceError)
				{
					return ultraCalcReference;
				}
				if (!(ultraCalcReference is RefBase))
				{
					return ultraCalcReference.CreateRange(fromReference, toReference);
				}
				IUltraCalcReference ultraCalcReference2 = CreateReference(toReference);
				if (ultraCalcReference2 is UltraCalcReferenceError)
				{
					return ultraCalcReference2;
				}
				if (!(ultraCalcReference2 is RefBase))
				{
					return new UltraCalcReferenceError(toReference, SR.GetString("Error_ExternalRange"));
				}
				return new RefRange((RefBase)ultraCalcReference, (RefBase)ultraCalcReference2);
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				return new UltraCalcReferenceError(fromReference + ":" + toReference, ex.Message);
			}
		}

		public virtual IUltraCalcReference ResolveReference(IUltraCalcReference inReference, ResolveReferenceType referenceType)
		{
			try
			{
				if (!(inReference is RefBase))
				{
					return inReference;
				}
				if (((RefBase)inReference).IsRange)
				{
					if (inReference is RefRange)
					{
						IUltraCalcReference ultraCalcReference = ResolveReference(((RefRange)inReference).FromRef, referenceType);
						IUltraCalcReference ultraCalcReference2 = ResolveReference(((RefRange)inReference).ToRef, referenceType);
						if (!(ultraCalcReference is RefBase))
						{
							return new UltraCalcReferenceError(inReference.ToString(), SR.GetString("Error_RangeFromRefBase", ultraCalcReference.ToString()));
						}
						if (!(ultraCalcReference2 is RefBase))
						{
							return new UltraCalcReferenceError(inReference.ToString(), SR.GetString("Error_RangeFromRefBase", ultraCalcReference2.ToString()));
						}
						return new RefRange((RefBase)ultraCalcReference, (RefBase)ultraCalcReference2);
					}
					return new UltraCalcReferenceError(inReference.ToString(), SR.GetString("Error_UnknownRange"));
				}
				RefParser refParser = new RefParser();
				refParser.Merge(ParsedReference, ((RefBase)inReference).ParsedReference, respectMarked: true);
				bool forceDataRef = IsDataReference && refParser.HasSummaryScope;
				IUltraCalcReference ultraCalcReference3 = null;
				ultraCalcReference3 = ((!refParser.IsFullyQualified && !refParser.IsRoot) ? BuildReference(refParser, forceDataRef) : FindRoot().BuildReference(refParser, forceDataRef));
				if (ultraCalcReference3 is UltraCalcReferenceError)
				{
					return new UltraCalcReferenceError(inReference.ToString(), ((UltraCalcReferenceError)ultraCalcReference3).Message);
				}
				if (ultraCalcReference3 == null)
				{
					return new UltraCalcReferenceError(inReference.ToString());
				}
				RefBase refBase = ultraCalcReference3 as RefBase;
				if (refBase.IsAnchored)
				{
					refBase = new RefUnAnchored(refBase);
				}
				refBase.RelativeReference = refParser;
				return refBase;
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				return new UltraCalcReferenceError(inReference.ToString(), ex.Message);
			}
		}

		public virtual bool ContainsReference(IUltraCalcReference inReference)
		{
			if (!(inReference is RefBase) && !(inReference is UCReference))
			{
				return false;
			}
			RefParser refParser = null;
			if (inReference is UCReference)
			{
				if (RefParser.AreStringsEqual(FindRoot().ElementName, ((UCReference)inReference).RootName, ignoreCase: true))
				{
					IUltraCalcReference reference = ((UCReference)inReference).Reference;
					if (reference is RefBase)
					{
						refParser = ((RefBase)reference).ParsedReference;
					}
					else if (reference is UCReference)
					{
						refParser = new RefParser(reference.AbsoluteName);
					}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				refParser = ((RefBase)inReference).ParsedReference;
			}
			if (refParser != null)
			{
				RefParser parsedReference = ParsedReference;
				return parsedReference.Contains(refParser);
			}
			return false;
		}

		public virtual bool IsSubsetReference(IUltraCalcReference inReference)
		{
			if (!(inReference is RefBase) && !(inReference is UCReference))
			{
				return false;
			}
			RefParser refParser = null;
			if (inReference is UCReference)
			{
				if (RefParser.AreStringsEqual(FindRoot().ElementName, ((UCReference)inReference).RootName, ignoreCase: true))
				{
					IUltraCalcReference reference = ((UCReference)inReference).Reference;
					if (reference is RefBase)
					{
						refParser = ((RefBase)reference).ParsedReference;
					}
					else if (reference is UCReference)
					{
						refParser = new RefParser(reference.AbsoluteName);
					}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				refParser = ((RefBase)inReference).ParsedReference;
			}
			if (refParser != null)
			{
				RefParser parsedReference = ParsedReference;
				return parsedReference.IsSubset(refParser);
			}
			return false;
		}

		public virtual bool IsSiblingReference(IUltraCalcReference reference)
		{
			return false;
		}

		public override string ToString()
		{
			return GetType().ToString() + "-" + AbsoluteName;
		}

		public virtual IUltraCalcReference[] GetChildReferences(ChildReferenceType referenceType)
		{
			return null;
		}

		protected virtual RefParser CreateParsedReference()
		{
			return new RefParser(AbsoluteName);
		}

		public override int GetHashCode()
		{
			return NormalizedAbsoluteName.GetHashCode();
		}

		public override bool Equals(object obj)
		{
			if (obj is IUltraCalcReference ultraCalcReference)
			{
				return ultraCalcReference.NormalizedAbsoluteName.Equals(NormalizedAbsoluteName);
			}
			return false;
		}
	}
	public class RefRange : RefBase, IUltraCalcReferenceCollection, IEnumerable
	{
		protected class RefRangeEnumerator : IEnumerator
		{
			private RefBase current;

			private RefRange range;

			public object Current
			{
				get
				{
					if (current == null)
					{
						throw new UltraCalcException(SR.GetString("Error_NoCurrentRangeElement"));
					}
					return current;
				}
			}

			public RefRangeEnumerator(RefRange range)
			{
				this.range = range;
			}

			public bool MoveNext()
			{
				if (current == null)
				{
					current = range.NormalizedFromBase;
					return current != null;
				}
				RefParser refParser = new RefParser(current.ParsedReference);
				int num = refParser.TupleCount - 1;
				if (refParser.LastTuple.Scope != RefTuple.RefScope.RelativeIndex && num > 0)
				{
					num--;
				}
				if (refParser[num].IncrementScope() && range.Contains(refParser))
				{
					IUltraCalcReference ultraCalcReference = current.CreateReference(refParser.ToString());
					if (ultraCalcReference is RefBase)
					{
						current = (RefBase)ultraCalcReference;
						return true;
					}
				}
				current = null;
				return false;
			}

			public void Reset()
			{
				current = null;
			}
		}

		protected RefBase from;

		protected RefBase to;

		protected RefBase normalizedFrom;

		protected RefBase normalizedTo;

		private bool isNormalized;

		public RefBase FromBase => from;

		public RefBase NormalizedFromBase
		{
			get
			{
				VerifyNormalized();
				return normalizedFrom;
			}
		}

		public IUltraCalcReference FromRef => from;

		public IUltraCalcReference NormalizedFromRef
		{
			get
			{
				VerifyNormalized();
				return normalizedFrom;
			}
		}

		public RefBase ToBase => to;

		public RefBase NormalizedToBase
		{
			get
			{
				VerifyNormalized();
				return normalizedTo;
			}
		}

		public IUltraCalcReference ToRef => to;

		public IUltraCalcReference NormalizedToRef
		{
			get
			{
				VerifyNormalized();
				return normalizedTo;
			}
		}

		public override bool IsRange => true;

		public override string ElementName
		{
			get
			{
				string text = ((from != null) ? from.ElementName : "???");
				string text2 = ((to != null) ? to.ElementName : "???");
				return text + " : " + text2;
			}
		}

		public override RefBase BaseParent => from;

		public override bool IsDataReference => true;

		public override string AbsoluteName => from.AbsoluteName + " : " + to.AbsoluteName;

		public override string NormalizedAbsoluteName => from.NormalizedAbsoluteName + " : " + to.NormalizedAbsoluteName;

		public override bool HasRelativeIndex => true;

		public override IUltraCalcReferenceCollection References => this;

		public override bool IsEnumerable => true;

		public RefRange(RefBase from, RefBase to)
		{
			if (from == null)
			{
				throw new UltraCalcException(SR.GetString("Error_RangeFromNull"));
			}
			if (to == null)
			{
				throw new UltraCalcException(SR.GetString("Error_RangeToNull"));
			}
			this.from = from;
			this.to = to;
			Validate();
		}

		private void VerifyNormalized()
		{
			if (!isNormalized)
			{
				RefParser parsedReference = from.ParsedReference;
				RefParser parsedReference2 = to.ParsedReference;
				try
				{
					Normalize(parsedReference, parsedReference2);
				}
				catch
				{
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual void Validate()
		{
			RefParser parsedReference = from.ParsedReference;
			RefParser parsedReference2 = to.ParsedReference;
			bool flag = parsedReference.HasScopeAll | parsedReference2.HasScopeAll;
			if (parsedReference.TupleCount != parsedReference2.TupleCount)
			{
				throw new UltraCalcException(SR.GetString("Error_RangeValidateCount", parsedReference.TupleCount, parsedReference2.TupleCount));
			}
			int num = 0;
			foreach (RefTuple item in parsedReference)
			{
				RefTuple refTuple2 = parsedReference2[num];
				if (item.Last)
				{
					if (!item.IsSameIdentifier(refTuple2))
					{
						throw new UltraCalcException(SR.GetString("Error_RangeLast", item.ToString(), refTuple2.ToString()));
					}
					break;
				}
				if (parsedReference2[num + 1].Last || flag)
				{
					if (!item.IsSameIdentifier(refTuple2))
					{
						throw new UltraCalcException(SR.GetString("Error_RangeTuple", item.ToString(), refTuple2.ToString()));
					}
				}
				else if (!item.IsSameTuple(refTuple2))
				{
					throw new UltraCalcException(SR.GetString("Error_RangeTuple", item.ToString(), refTuple2.ToString()));
				}
				num++;
			}
		}

		private void Normalize(RefParser fromRP, RefParser toRP)
		{
			RefParser outRP = null;
			NormalizeOne(fromRP, ref outRP);
			RefParser outRP2 = null;
			NormalizeOne(toRP, ref outRP2);
			IUltraCalcReference ultraCalcReference = CreateReference(outRP.ToString());
			if (!(ultraCalcReference is RefBase))
			{
				throw new UltraCalcException(SR.GetString("Error_RangeFromRefBase", ultraCalcReference.ToString()));
			}
			IUltraCalcReference ultraCalcReference2 = CreateReference(outRP2.ToString());
			if (!(ultraCalcReference2 is RefBase))
			{
				throw new UltraCalcException(SR.GetString("Error_RangeFromRefBase", ultraCalcReference2.ToString()));
			}
			if (fromRP.HasScopeAll || toRP.HasScopeAll)
			{
				if ((fromRP.HasScopeAll && toRP.HasScopeAll) || toRP.HasScopeAll)
				{
					normalizedFrom = (RefBase)ultraCalcReference;
					normalizedTo = (RefBase)ultraCalcReference2;
				}
				else
				{
					normalizedFrom = (RefBase)ultraCalcReference2;
					normalizedTo = (RefBase)ultraCalcReference;
				}
			}
			else if (fromRP.TupleCount <= 1)
			{
				normalizedFrom = (RefBase)ultraCalcReference;
				normalizedTo = (RefBase)ultraCalcReference2;
			}
			else
			{
				int num = fromRP.TupleCount - 1;
				if (outRP.LastTuple.Scope != RefTuple.RefScope.RelativeIndex)
				{
					num--;
				}
				if (fromRP[num].IsNumericScopeLTE(toRP[num]))
				{
					normalizedFrom = (RefBase)ultraCalcReference;
					normalizedTo = (RefBase)ultraCalcReference2;
				}
				else
				{
					normalizedFrom = (RefBase)ultraCalcReference2;
					normalizedTo = (RefBase)ultraCalcReference;
				}
			}
			isNormalized = true;
		}

		private static void NormalizeOne(RefParser inRP, ref RefParser outRP)
		{
			RefParser refParser = new RefParser(inRP);
			if (inRP.TupleCount <= 1 || refParser.HasScopeAll)
			{
				outRP = refParser;
				return;
			}
			RefTuple refTuple = refParser[refParser.TupleCount - 2];
			switch (refTuple.Scope)
			{
			case RefTuple.RefScope.Index:
				if (refParser.LastTuple.Scope == RefTuple.RefScope.Index)
				{
					refTuple.ScopeIndex = refParser.LastTuple.ScopeIndex;
					refParser.LastTuple.Scope = RefTuple.RefScope.Any;
				}
				break;
			case RefTuple.RefScope.Any:
				if (refParser.LastTuple.Scope == RefTuple.RefScope.Index)
				{
					refTuple.Scope = RefTuple.RefScope.Index;
					refTuple.ScopeIndex = refParser.LastTuple.ScopeIndex;
					refParser.LastTuple.Scope = RefTuple.RefScope.Any;
				}
				break;
			default:
				throw new UltraCalcException(SR.GetString("Error_RangeNormalizeScope", refTuple.ToString(), refParser.LastTuple.ToString()));
			}
			outRP = refParser;
		}

		public bool Contains(RefParser targetRP)
		{
			VerifyNormalized();
			if (!isNormalized)
			{
				return false;
			}
			RefParser parsedReference = normalizedFrom.ParsedReference;
			RefParser parsedReference2 = normalizedTo.ParsedReference;
			if (parsedReference.TupleCount != targetRP.TupleCount)
			{
				return false;
			}
			int num = 0;
			int num2 = parsedReference.TupleCount - 1;
			if (parsedReference.LastTuple.Scope != RefTuple.RefScope.RelativeIndex && num2 > 0)
			{
				num2--;
			}
			foreach (RefTuple item in parsedReference)
			{
				RefTuple refTuple2 = targetRP[num];
				if (!item.IsSameIdentifier(refTuple2))
				{
					return false;
				}
				if (num == num2)
				{
					if (!refTuple2.Last && !parsedReference.LastTuple.IsSameIdentifier(targetRP.LastTuple))
					{
						return false;
					}
					return refTuple2.IsNumericScopeGTE(item) && (parsedReference2.HasScopeAll || refTuple2.IsNumericScopeLTE(parsedReference2[num]));
				}
				num++;
			}
			throw new UltraCalcException(SR.GetString("Error_RangeFromLast"));
		}

		protected override RefParser CreateParsedReference()
		{
			return from.ParsedReference;
		}

		public override IUltraCalcReference FindItem(string name)
		{
			return new UltraCalcReferenceError(null, SR.GetString("Error_MethodNotSupported", GetType().ToString(), "FindItem( " + name + " )"));
		}

		public override IUltraCalcReference FindAll(string name)
		{
			return new UltraCalcReferenceError(null, SR.GetString("Error_MethodNotSupported", GetType().ToString(), "FindAll( " + name + " )"));
		}

		public override IUltraCalcReference FindSummaryItem(string name)
		{
			return new UltraCalcReferenceError(null, SR.GetString("Error_MethodNotSupported", GetType().ToString(), "FindSummaryItem( " + name + " )"));
		}

		public override IUltraCalcReference FindItem(string name, string index)
		{
			return new UltraCalcReferenceError(null, SR.GetString("Error_MethodNotSupported", GetType().ToString(), "FindItem( " + name + "," + index + " )"));
		}

		public override IUltraCalcReference FindItem(string name, int index, bool isRelative)
		{
			string text = "";
			if (isRelative)
			{
				text = ((index >= 0) ? "+" : "-");
			}
			return new UltraCalcReferenceError(null, SR.GetString("Error_MethodNotSupported", GetType().ToString(), "FindItem( " + name + "," + text + index + " )"));
		}

		public override IUltraCalcReferenceCollection ScopedReferences(RefParser scopeRP)
		{
			return this;
		}

		public override void MarkRelativeIndices(IUltraCalcReference inReference)
		{
			if (inReference is RefBase)
			{
				ParsedReference.MarkRelativeIndices(((RefBase)inReference).ParsedReference, forceLastTuple: true);
			}
		}

		public override IUltraCalcReference ResolveReference(IUltraCalcReference inReference, ResolveReferenceType referenceType)
		{
			return from.ResolveReference(inReference, referenceType);
		}

		public override bool ContainsReference(IUltraCalcReference inReference)
		{
			if (!(inReference is RefBase) && !(inReference is UCReference))
			{
				return false;
			}
			RefBase refBase = null;
			if (inReference is UCReference)
			{
				if (RefParser.AreStringsEqual(FindRoot().ElementName, ((UCReference)inReference).RootName, ignoreCase: true))
				{
					IUltraCalcReference reference = ((UCReference)inReference).Reference;
					if (reference is RefBase)
					{
						refBase = (RefBase)reference;
					}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				refBase = (RefBase)inReference;
			}
			if (refBase == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return Contains(refBase.ParsedReference);
		}

		public override string ToString()
		{
			return $"{GetType()}-\n  from = {from}\n  to ={to}";
		}

		public IEnumerator GetEnumerator()
		{
			return new RefRangeEnumerator(this);
		}
	}
	public class RefTuple
	{
		public enum RefType
		{
			Parent,
			ExplicitThis,
			Identifier,
			Unknown
		}

		public enum RefScope
		{
			Any,
			Index,
			RelativeIndex,
			Identifier,
			SummaryValue,
			All,
			Unknown
		}

		[Flags]
		private enum RefTupleFlags
		{
			Marked = 1,
			Last = 2,
			NextToLast = 4,
			ScopeIDRequiresQuotes = 8
		}

		[Flags]
		private enum State
		{
			TupleNamePortion = 1,
			Escape = 2,
			TupleScopePortion = 4,
			QuotedString = 8,
			TupleScopeEnded = 0x10
		}

		private const string ParentTypeString = "..";

		private const string ExplicitThisTypeString = ".";

		private const string AllScopeString = "*";

		private string refName;

		private RefType refType = RefType.Unknown;

		private string scopeIdentifier;

		private RefScope scopeType = RefScope.Unknown;

		private int scopeIndex;

		private RefTupleFlags flags;

		public RefType Type => refType;

		public RefScope Scope
		{
			get
			{
				return scopeType;
			}
			set
			{
				scopeType = value;
			}
		}

		public string Name => refName;

		public bool Marked
		{
			get
			{
				return (flags & RefTupleFlags.Marked) != 0;
			}
			set
			{
				if (Marked != value)
				{
					flags ^= RefTupleFlags.Marked;
				}
			}
		}

		public bool Last
		{
			get
			{
				return (flags & RefTupleFlags.Last) != 0;
			}
			set
			{
				if (Last != value)
				{
					flags ^= RefTupleFlags.Last;
				}
			}
		}

		public bool NextToLast
		{
			get
			{
				return (flags & RefTupleFlags.NextToLast) != 0;
			}
			set
			{
				if (NextToLast != value)
				{
					flags ^= RefTupleFlags.NextToLast;
				}
			}
		}

		private bool ScopeIDRequiresQuotes
		{
			get
			{
				return (RefTupleFlags.ScopeIDRequiresQuotes & flags) != 0;
			}
			set
			{
				if (ScopeIDRequiresQuotes != value)
				{
					flags ^= RefTupleFlags.ScopeIDRequiresQuotes;
				}
			}
		}

		public string ScopeID => scopeIdentifier;

		public int ScopeIndex
		{
			get
			{
				return scopeIndex;
			}
			set
			{
				scopeIndex = value;
			}
		}

		public bool IsAbsolute
		{
			get
			{
				if (refType == RefType.Identifier)
				{
					switch (scopeType)
					{
					case RefScope.Index:
					case RefScope.Identifier:
					case RefScope.SummaryValue:
					case RefScope.All:
						return true;
					default:
						return false;
					}
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public RefTuple(RefTuple clone)
		{
			refType = clone.refType;
			refName = clone.refName;
			scopeIdentifier = clone.scopeIdentifier;
			scopeType = clone.scopeType;
			scopeIndex = clone.scopeIndex;
			flags = clone.flags;
		}

		public RefTuple(string refName)
			: this(refName, maintainCase: false)
		{
		}

		internal RefTuple(string refName, bool maintainCase)
		{
			refType = RefType.Unknown;
			scopeType = RefScope.Unknown;
			string text = Parse(refName, maintainCase);
			if (text != null)
			{
				throw new UltraCalcException(text);
			}
		}

		private string ParseAndNormalizeScope(string scopePortion, out string normalizedScopePortion)
		{
			normalizedScopePortion = null;
			RefParser.NameValuePair[] nameValuePairs;
			string nonNameValuePairScope;
			string text = RefParser.ParseScopeHelper(scopePortion, out nameValuePairs, out nonNameValuePairScope);
			if (text != null)
			{
				return text;
			}
			if (nameValuePairs != null && nameValuePairs.Length != 0)
			{
				StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder(scopePortion.Length);
				for (int i = 0; i < nameValuePairs.Length; i++)
				{
					if (i > 0)
					{
						stringBuilder.Append(',');
					}
					RefParser.NameValuePair nameValuePair = nameValuePairs[i];
					stringBuilder.Append(RefParser.EscapeString(nameValuePair.Name.ToLower(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture), withinQuotes: false));
					stringBuilder.Append('=');
					stringBuilder.Append('"');
					stringBuilder.Append(RefParser.EscapeString(nameValuePair.Value, withinQuotes: true));
					stringBuilder.Append('"');
				}
				ScopeIDRequiresQuotes = false;
				normalizedScopePortion = stringBuilder.ToString();
			}
			else if (nonNameValuePairScope != null)
			{
				ScopeIDRequiresQuotes = true;
				normalizedScopePortion = RefParser.EscapeString(nonNameValuePairScope, withinQuotes: true);
			}
			else
			{
				text = SR.GetString("Error_RT_InvalidScope", scopePortion);
			}
			return text;
		}

		private string Parse(string refName, bool maintainCase)
		{
			if (refName == null || refName.Length == 0)
			{
				return SR.GetString("Error_RT_NullRefName");
			}
			string text = null;
			string text2 = null;
			int lastEscapedCharIndex = -1;
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder(refName.Length);
			State state = State.TupleNamePortion;
			for (int i = 0; i < refName.Length; i++)
			{
				char c = refName[i];
				if ((State.Escape & state) != 0)
				{
					state ^= State.Escape;
					if (State.TupleScopePortion == state || RefParser.DoesCharNeedEscaping(c))
					{
						stringBuilder.Append('\\');
					}
					lastEscapedCharIndex = stringBuilder.Length;
					stringBuilder.Append(c);
				}
				else if ('\\' == c)
				{
					state |= State.Escape;
				}
				else if ('(' == c && State.TupleNamePortion == state)
				{
					RefParser.TrimTrailingSpaces(stringBuilder, lastEscapedCharIndex);
					text = stringBuilder.ToString();
					stringBuilder.Length = 0;
					if (text.Length == 0)
					{
						return SR.GetString("Error_RT_InvalidTuple_NamePortionEmpty", refName);
					}
					state = State.TupleScopePortion;
				}
				else if (')' == c && State.TupleScopePortion == state)
				{
					if (1 + i != refName.Length)
					{
						return SR.GetString("Error_RT_InvalidTuple_CharactersAfterScopeEnd", refName);
					}
					RefParser.TrimTrailingSpaces(stringBuilder, lastEscapedCharIndex);
					text2 = stringBuilder.ToString();
					state = State.TupleScopeEnded;
				}
				else if (State.TupleScopeEnded == state)
				{
					if (!char.IsWhiteSpace(c))
					{
						return SR.GetString("Error_RT_InvalidTuple_CharactersAfterScopeEnd", refName);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					if (stringBuilder.Length == 0 && (State.QuotedString & state) == 0 && char.IsWhiteSpace(c))
					{
						continue;
					}
					if ('"' == c && State.TupleScopePortion == state)
					{
						state |= State.QuotedString;
						stringBuilder.Append(c);
						continue;
					}
					if ('"' == c && (State.QuotedString & state) != 0)
					{
						state ^= State.QuotedString;
						stringBuilder.Append(c);
						continue;
					}
					if ((State.QuotedString & state) != 0)
					{
						stringBuilder.Append(c);
						continue;
					}
					if ((State.TupleScopePortion & state) == 0 && RefParser.DoesCharNeedEscaping(c))
					{
						return SR.GetString("Error_RT_UnescapedCharacterInTuple", c, refName, i);
					}
					stringBuilder.Append(c);
				}
			}
			if ((State.Escape & state) != 0)
			{
				return SR.GetString("Error_RT_InvalidTuple_UnescapedCharacter", '\\', refName, refName.Length - 1);
			}
			if ((State.QuotedString & state) != 0)
			{
				return SR.GetString("Error_RT_InvalidTuple_MissingEndQuotes", refName);
			}
			if ((State.TupleScopePortion & state) != 0)
			{
				return SR.GetString("Error_RT_InvalidTuple_MissingCloseParenthesis", refName);
			}
			if (state == State.TupleNamePortion)
			{
				RefParser.TrimTrailingSpaces(stringBuilder, lastEscapedCharIndex);
				if (stringBuilder.Length == 0)
				{
					return SR.GetString("Error_RT_NullRefName");
				}
				text = stringBuilder.ToString();
			}
			if (string.Equals(text, ".."))
			{
				refType = RefType.Parent;
			}
			else if (string.Equals(text, "."))
			{
				refType = RefType.ExplicitThis;
			}
			else
			{
				if (maintainCase)
				{
					this.refName = text;
				}
				else
				{
					this.refName = text.ToLower(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
				}
				refType = RefType.Identifier;
			}
			if (text2 == null)
			{
				scopeType = RefScope.Any;
			}
			else if (text2.Length == 0)
			{
				scopeType = RefScope.SummaryValue;
			}
			else if (string.Equals("*", text2))
			{
				scopeType = RefScope.All;
			}
			else
			{
				bool flag = '+' == text2[0] || '-' == text2[0];
				int j;
				for (j = (flag ? 1 : 0); j < text2.Length && char.IsDigit(text2, j); j++)
				{
				}
				if (j == text2.Length)
				{
					try
					{
						scopeIndex = Convert.ToInt32(text2, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
					}
					catch (Exception ex)
					{
						return SR.GetString("Error_RT_InvalidTuple_ScopeIndexLarge", refName, text2, ex.Message);
					}
					scopeType = ((!flag) ? RefScope.Index : RefScope.RelativeIndex);
				}
				else
				{
					scopeType = RefScope.Identifier;
					string text3 = ParseAndNormalizeScope(text2, out scopeIdentifier);
					if (text3 != null)
					{
						return text3;
					}
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		public bool IsSameTuple(RefTuple tuple)
		{
			if (refType == tuple.Type)
			{
				if (refType != RefType.Identifier)
				{
					return IsSameScope(tuple);
				}
				if (RefParser.AreStringsEqual(refName, tuple.Name, ignoreCase: true))
				{
					return IsSameScope(tuple);
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		public bool IsSameIdentifier(RefTuple tuple)
		{
			if (refType == tuple.Type && refType == RefType.Identifier && RefParser.AreStringsEqual(refName, tuple.Name, ignoreCase: true))
			{
				if (scopeType == RefScope.SummaryValue || tuple.Scope == RefScope.SummaryValue)
				{
					return scopeType == tuple.Scope;
				}
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public bool Contains(RefTuple tuple)
		{
			if (refType == tuple.Type)
			{
				if (refType != RefType.Identifier)
				{
					return ContainsScope(tuple);
				}
				if (RefParser.AreStringsEqual(refName, tuple.Name, ignoreCase: true))
				{
					return ContainsScope(tuple);
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		public bool IsSubset(RefTuple tuple)
		{
			if (refType == tuple.Type)
			{
				if (refType != RefType.Identifier)
				{
					return IsScopeSubset(tuple);
				}
				if (RefParser.AreStringsEqual(refName, tuple.Name, ignoreCase: true))
				{
					return IsScopeSubset(tuple);
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		private bool IsSameScope(RefTuple tuple)
		{
			if (scopeType == RefScope.SummaryValue || tuple.Scope == RefScope.SummaryValue)
			{
				return scopeType == tuple.Scope;
			}
			if (scopeType == RefScope.All || tuple.Scope == RefScope.All)
			{
				return scopeType == tuple.Scope;
			}
			if (scopeType == tuple.Scope)
			{
				switch (scopeType)
				{
				case RefScope.Identifier:
					return string.Compare(scopeIdentifier, tuple.ScopeID) == 0;
				case RefScope.Index:
				case RefScope.RelativeIndex:
					return scopeIndex == tuple.ScopeIndex;
				default:
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		private bool ContainsScope(RefTuple tuple)
		{
			if (scopeType == RefScope.Unknown || tuple.Scope == RefScope.Unknown)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (scopeType == RefScope.SummaryValue || tuple.Scope == RefScope.SummaryValue)
			{
				return scopeType == tuple.Scope;
			}
			if (scopeType == RefScope.Any || tuple.Scope == RefScope.Any)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (scopeType == RefScope.All || tuple.Scope == RefScope.All)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (scopeType == RefScope.Identifier && tuple.Scope == RefScope.Identifier)
			{
				return string.Compare(scopeIdentifier, tuple.ScopeID) == 0;
			}
			if (scopeType == RefScope.Index && tuple.Scope == RefScope.Index)
			{
				return scopeIndex == tuple.ScopeIndex;
			}
			if (scopeType == RefScope.RelativeIndex && tuple.Scope == RefScope.RelativeIndex)
			{
				return scopeIndex == tuple.ScopeIndex;
			}
			return false;
		}

		private bool IsScopeSubset(RefTuple tuple)
		{
			if (scopeType == RefScope.Unknown || tuple.Scope == RefScope.Unknown)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (scopeType == RefScope.SummaryValue || tuple.Scope == RefScope.SummaryValue)
			{
				return scopeType == tuple.Scope;
			}
			if (scopeType == RefScope.Any)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (scopeType == RefScope.All)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (scopeType == RefScope.Identifier && tuple.Scope == RefScope.Identifier)
			{
				return string.Compare(scopeIdentifier, tuple.ScopeID) == 0;
			}
			if (scopeType == RefScope.Index && tuple.Scope == RefScope.Index)
			{
				return scopeIndex == tuple.ScopeIndex;
			}
			if (scopeType == RefScope.RelativeIndex && tuple.Scope == RefScope.RelativeIndex)
			{
				return scopeIndex == tuple.ScopeIndex;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public bool IsNumericScopeLTE(RefTuple tuple)
		{
			if (scopeType == RefScope.SummaryValue || tuple.Scope == RefScope.SummaryValue)
			{
				return scopeType == tuple.Scope;
			}
			if (scopeType == RefScope.Any || tuple.Scope == RefScope.Any)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (scopeType == RefScope.All || tuple.Scope == RefScope.All)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (refType != tuple.Type)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (refType == RefType.Identifier && !RefParser.AreStringsEqual(refName, tuple.Name, ignoreCase: true))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (scopeType == RefScope.Index)
			{
				if (tuple.Scope == RefScope.Index)
				{
					return scopeIndex <= tuple.ScopeIndex;
				}
				if (tuple.Scope == RefScope.RelativeIndex)
				{
					return scopeIndex <= scopeIndex + tuple.ScopeIndex;
				}
			}
			else if (scopeType == RefScope.RelativeIndex)
			{
				if (tuple.Scope == RefScope.Index)
				{
					return scopeIndex + tuple.ScopeIndex <= tuple.ScopeIndex;
				}
				if (tuple.Scope == RefScope.RelativeIndex)
				{
					return scopeIndex <= tuple.ScopeIndex;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		public bool IsNumericScopeGTE(RefTuple tuple)
		{
			if (scopeType == RefScope.SummaryValue || tuple.Scope == RefScope.SummaryValue)
			{
				return scopeType == tuple.Scope;
			}
			if (scopeType == RefScope.Any || tuple.Scope == RefScope.Any)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (scopeType == RefScope.All || tuple.Scope == RefScope.All)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (refType != tuple.Type)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (refType == RefType.Identifier && !RefParser.AreStringsEqual(refName, tuple.Name, ignoreCase: true))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (scopeType == RefScope.Index)
			{
				if (tuple.Scope == RefScope.Index)
				{
					return scopeIndex >= tuple.ScopeIndex;
				}
				if (tuple.Scope == RefScope.RelativeIndex)
				{
					return scopeIndex >= scopeIndex + tuple.ScopeIndex;
				}
			}
			else if (scopeType == RefScope.RelativeIndex)
			{
				if (tuple.Scope == RefScope.Index)
				{
					return scopeIndex + tuple.ScopeIndex >= tuple.ScopeIndex;
				}
				if (tuple.Scope == RefScope.RelativeIndex)
				{
					return scopeIndex >= tuple.ScopeIndex;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		public bool IncrementScope()
		{
			RefScope refScope = scopeType;
			if (refScope == RefScope.Index || refScope == RefScope.RelativeIndex)
			{
				scopeIndex++;
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public void ToString(StringBuilder sb)
		{
			switch (refType)
			{
			case RefType.ExplicitThis:
				sb.Append(".");
				break;
			case RefType.Parent:
				sb.Append("..");
				break;
			case RefType.Identifier:
				sb.Append(refName);
				break;
			case RefType.Unknown:
				sb.Append("?unknown?");
				break;
			default:
				sb.Append("?default?");
				break;
			}
			if (scopeType == RefScope.Any)
			{
				return;
			}
			sb.Append('(');
			switch (scopeType)
			{
			case RefScope.Identifier:
				if (ScopeIDRequiresQuotes)
				{
					sb.Append('"').Append(scopeIdentifier).Append('"');
				}
				else
				{
					sb.Append(scopeIdentifier);
				}
				break;
			case RefScope.All:
				sb.Append("*");
				break;
			case RefScope.Index:
				sb.Append(scopeIndex);
				break;
			case RefScope.RelativeIndex:
				if (scopeIndex > 0)
				{
					sb.Append('+');
				}
				sb.Append(scopeIndex);
				break;
