			case RefScope.Unknown:
				sb.Append("?unknown?");
				break;
			default:
				sb.Append("?default?");
				break;
			case RefScope.SummaryValue:
				break;
			}
			sb.Append(')');
		}

		public override string ToString()
		{
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			ToString(stringBuilder);
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}
	}
	public class RefParser : IEnumerable
	{
		[Flags]
		private enum RefParserFlags
		{
			FullyQualified = 1,
			Root = 2,
			HasRelativeIndex = 4,
			HasSummaryScope = 8,
			HasAbsoluteIndex = 0x10,
			HasScopeAll = 0x20
		}

		[Flags]
		private enum State
		{
			Tuple = 1,
			TupleEnded = 2,
			Escape = 4,
			Scope = 8,
			QuotedString = 0x10
		}

		public class NameValuePair
		{
			private object tag;

			private string name;

			private string value;

			public string Name => name;

			public string Value => value;

			public object Tag
			{
				get
				{
					return tag;
				}
				set
				{
					tag = value;
				}
			}

			public NameValuePair(string name, string value)
			{
				this.name = name;
				this.value = value;
			}
		}

		[Flags]
		private enum ScopeParseState
		{
			Initial = 0,
			Escape = 1,
			Column = 2,
			Value = 4,
			Quote = 8
		}

		public const string RefFullyQualifiedString = "//";

		public const char RefSeperator = '/';

		public const string RefSeperatorString = "/";

		public const char RefBeginScope = '(';

		public const char RefEndScope = ')';

		public const string RangeSeparator = ":";

		public const string RangeSeparatorWithSpaces = " : ";

		public const char EscapeCharacter = '\\';

		public const char QuotesCharacter = '"';

		public const char NameValuePairSeparatorCharacter = ',';

		public const char NameValueSeparatorCharacter = '=';

		private ArrayList tuples = new ArrayList();

		private RefParserFlags flags;

		private static string SPECIAL_CHARACTERS;

		private static string SPECIAL_CHARACTERS_WITHIN_QUOTES;

		private static readonly long mask_specialCharacters;

		private static readonly long mask_specialCharactersWithinQuotes;

		public int TupleCount => tuples.Count;

		public RefTuple this[int index] => (RefTuple)tuples[index];

		public RefTuple LastTuple
		{
			get
			{
				int count = tuples.Count;
				if (count <= 0)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return (RefTuple)tuples[count - 1];
			}
		}

		public RefTuple NextToLastTuple
		{
			get
			{
				if (tuples.Count > 1)
				{
					return (RefTuple)tuples[tuples.Count - 2];
				}
				return null;
			}
		}

		internal string RootName
		{
			get
			{
				if (TupleCount <= 0)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return this[0].Name;
			}
		}

		public bool IsFullyQualified => (RefParserFlags.FullyQualified & flags) != 0;

		public bool IsRoot => (RefParserFlags.Root & flags) != 0;

		public bool IsRelative
		{
			get
			{
				if (!IsRoot)
				{
					return !IsFullyQualified;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public bool HasRelativeIndex => (RefParserFlags.HasRelativeIndex & flags) != 0;

		public bool HasScopeAll => (RefParserFlags.HasScopeAll & flags) != 0;

		public bool HasAbsoluteIndex => (RefParserFlags.HasAbsoluteIndex & flags) != 0;

		public bool HasSummaryScope => (RefParserFlags.HasSummaryScope & flags) != 0;

		public RefParser()
		{
		}

		public RefParser(string refName)
			: this(refName, maintainCase: false)
		{
		}

		internal RefParser(string refName, bool maintainCase)
		{
			string text = ParseReference(refName, maintainCase);
			if (text != null)
			{
				throw new UltraCalcException(text);
			}
		}

		public RefParser(RefParser rp)
		{
			flags = rp.flags;
			foreach (RefTuple item in rp)
			{
				tuples.Add(new RefTuple(item));
			}
		}

		private void SetFlag(RefParserFlags flag, bool value)
		{
			if (value)
			{
				flags |= flag;
			}
			else
			{
				flags &= ~flag;
			}
		}

		private void MarkIndices()
		{
			tuples.TrimToSize();
			flags &= ~(RefParserFlags.HasRelativeIndex | RefParserFlags.HasSummaryScope | RefParserFlags.HasAbsoluteIndex | RefParserFlags.HasScopeAll);
			if (tuples.Count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (RefTuple tuple in tuples)
			{
				if (tuple.Scope == RefTuple.RefScope.RelativeIndex)
				{
					flags |= RefParserFlags.HasRelativeIndex;
				}
				if (tuple.Scope == RefTuple.RefScope.All)
				{
					flags |= RefParserFlags.HasRelativeIndex | RefParserFlags.HasScopeAll;
				}
				if (tuple.Scope == RefTuple.RefScope.Index)
				{
					flags |= RefParserFlags.HasAbsoluteIndex;
				}
				if (HasRelativeIndex && HasAbsoluteIndex && HasScopeAll)
				{
					break;
				}
			}
			((RefTuple)tuples[tuples.Count - 1]).Last = true;
			if (tuples.Count > 1)
			{
				((RefTuple)tuples[tuples.Count - 2]).NextToLast = true;
			}
			if (((RefTuple)tuples[tuples.Count - 1]).Scope == RefTuple.RefScope.SummaryValue)
			{
				flags |= RefParserFlags.HasSummaryScope;
			}
		}

		public virtual void MarkRelativeIndices(RefParser targetRP, bool forceLastTuple)
		{
			int tupleCount = TupleCount;
			int num = 0;
			int tupleCount2 = targetRP.TupleCount;
			int num2 = 0;
			if (IsFullyQualified != targetRP.IsFullyQualified)
			{
				if (IsFullyQualified)
				{
					num++;
				}
				if (targetRP.IsFullyQualified)
				{
					num2++;
				}
			}
			while (num < tupleCount && num2 < tupleCount2)
			{
				RefTuple refTuple = this[num];
				RefTuple refTuple2 = targetRP[num2];
				if (refTuple.Type != refTuple2.Type)
				{
					break;
				}
				if (refTuple.Type == RefTuple.RefType.Identifier)
				{
					if (!refTuple.IsSameIdentifier(refTuple2))
					{
						if ((refTuple.Last || refTuple2.Last) && refTuple.Scope != RefTuple.RefScope.SummaryValue && refTuple2.Scope != RefTuple.RefScope.SummaryValue && (forceLastTuple || refTuple.Scope == RefTuple.RefScope.RelativeIndex || refTuple.Scope == RefTuple.RefScope.All || RefTuple.RefScope.Index == refTuple.Scope))
						{
							refTuple2.Marked = true;
						}
						break;
					}
					if (refTuple.NextToLast && (LastTuple.Scope == RefTuple.RefScope.RelativeIndex || LastTuple.Scope == RefTuple.RefScope.All || RefTuple.RefScope.Index == LastTuple.Scope))
					{
						refTuple2.Marked = true;
					}
					if (refTuple.Scope == RefTuple.RefScope.RelativeIndex || refTuple.Scope == RefTuple.RefScope.All || RefTuple.RefScope.Index == refTuple.Scope)
					{
						refTuple2.Marked = true;
					}
				}
				num++;
				num2++;
			}
		}

		public void Merge(RefParser baseRP, RefParser mergeRP, bool respectMarked)
		{
			SetFlag(RefParserFlags.FullyQualified, baseRP.IsFullyQualified);
			SetFlag(RefParserFlags.Root, baseRP.IsRoot);
			if (baseRP.IsRelative)
			{
				throw new UltraCalcException(SR.GetString("Error_RP_MergeRelativeBase"));
			}
			int tupleCount = baseRP.TupleCount;
			int i = 0;
			int tupleCount2 = mergeRP.TupleCount;
			int j = 0;
			if (baseRP.IsFullyQualified)
			{
				if (mergeRP.IsFullyQualified && !baseRP[i].IsSameIdentifier(mergeRP[j]))
				{
					for (int k = 0; k < mergeRP.TupleCount; k++)
					{
						tuples.Add(new RefTuple(mergeRP[k]));
					}
					SetFlag(RefParserFlags.Root, mergeRP.IsRoot);
					MarkIndices();
					return;
				}
				tuples.Add(new RefTuple(baseRP[i]));
				i++;
			}
			if (mergeRP.IsFullyQualified)
			{
				j++;
			}
			for (; i < tupleCount; i++)
			{
				RefTuple refTuple = baseRP[i];
				if (refTuple.Type == RefTuple.RefType.Identifier)
				{
					break;
				}
			}
			for (; j < tupleCount2; j++)
			{
				RefTuple refTuple2 = mergeRP[j];
				if (refTuple2.Type == RefTuple.RefType.Identifier)
				{
					break;
				}
			}
			if (baseRP.IsRoot && !mergeRP.IsRoot)
			{
				RefTuple refTuple3 = mergeRP[j];
				for (; i < tupleCount; i++)
				{
					RefTuple refTuple4 = baseRP[i];
					if (refTuple4.Last || AreStringsEqual(refTuple4.Name, refTuple3.Name, ignoreCase: true))
					{
						break;
					}
					tuples.Add(new RefTuple(refTuple4));
				}
			}
			while (i < tupleCount && j < tupleCount2)
			{
				RefTuple refTuple5 = baseRP[i];
				RefTuple refTuple6 = mergeRP[j];
				if (refTuple5.Type != RefTuple.RefType.Identifier && refTuple5.IsSameTuple(refTuple6))
				{
					tuples.Add(new RefTuple(refTuple5));
					i++;
					j++;
					continue;
				}
				if (refTuple6.Type != RefTuple.RefType.Identifier)
				{
					j++;
					continue;
				}
				if (refTuple5.Type != RefTuple.RefType.Identifier)
				{
					i++;
					tuples.Add(new RefTuple(refTuple5));
					continue;
				}
				if (!refTuple5.IsSameIdentifier(refTuple6))
				{
					break;
				}
				bool flag = false;
				if (respectMarked)
				{
					if (!refTuple6.Last)
					{
						RefTuple refTuple7 = mergeRP[j + 1];
						if (refTuple7.Last)
						{
							flag = refTuple7.Marked;
						}
					}
					flag |= refTuple6.Marked;
				}
				if (flag)
				{
					tuples.Add(new RefTuple(refTuple6.Name));
				}
				else if (refTuple6.Scope == RefTuple.RefScope.Any)
				{
					tuples.Add(new RefTuple(refTuple5));
				}
				else
				{
					tuples.Add(new RefTuple(refTuple6));
				}
				i++;
				j++;
			}
			while (j < tupleCount2)
			{
				RefTuple refTuple8 = mergeRP[j];
				if (refTuple8.Type != RefTuple.RefType.Identifier)
				{
					j++;
					continue;
				}
				tuples.Add(new RefTuple(refTuple8));
				j++;
			}
			MarkIndices();
		}

		public void BuildReference(RefParser baseRP, RefParser mergeRP)
		{
			SetFlag(RefParserFlags.FullyQualified, baseRP.IsFullyQualified);
			SetFlag(RefParserFlags.Root, baseRP.IsRoot);
			if (baseRP.IsRelative)
			{
				throw new UltraCalcException(SR.GetString("Error_RP_MergeRelativeBase"));
			}
			if (mergeRP.IsFullyQualified)
			{
				throw new UltraCalcException(SR.GetString("Error_RP_MergeRelativeBase"));
			}
			int num = baseRP.TupleCount - 1;
			int tupleCount = mergeRP.TupleCount;
			int num2 = 0;
			if (!mergeRP.IsRoot)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < tupleCount && mergeRP[i].Type != RefTuple.RefType.Identifier; i++)
				{
					if (mergeRP[i].Type == RefTuple.RefType.Parent)
					{
						num--;
						num2++;
					}
					if (mergeRP[i].Type == RefTuple.RefType.ExplicitThis)
					{
						num2++;
					}
				}
			}
			else
			{
				num = 1;
			}
			for (int j = 0; j < num; j++)
			{
				tuples.Add(new RefTuple(baseRP[j]));
			}
			for (int k = num2; k < tupleCount; k++)
			{
				tuples.Add(new RefTuple(mergeRP[k]));
			}
			MarkIndices();
		}

		static RefParser()
		{
			SPECIAL_CHARACTERS = new string(new char[9] { '(', ')', '/', '\\', '"', ',', '=', '[', ']' });
			SPECIAL_CHARACTERS_WITHIN_QUOTES = new string(new char[2] { '\\', '"' });
			mask_specialCharacters = 1L;
			mask_specialCharactersWithinQuotes = 1L;
			string sPECIAL_CHARACTERS = SPECIAL_CHARACTERS;
			foreach (char c in sPECIAL_CHARACTERS)
			{
				long num = mask_specialCharacters * (int)c;
				if (num > mask_specialCharacters)
				{
					mask_specialCharacters = num;
					continue;
				}
				mask_specialCharacters = 0L;
				break;
			}
			string sPECIAL_CHARACTERS_WITHIN_QUOTES = SPECIAL_CHARACTERS_WITHIN_QUOTES;
			foreach (char c2 in sPECIAL_CHARACTERS_WITHIN_QUOTES)
			{
				long num2 = mask_specialCharactersWithinQuotes * (int)c2;
				if (num2 > mask_specialCharactersWithinQuotes)
				{
					mask_specialCharactersWithinQuotes = num2;
					continue;
				}
				mask_specialCharactersWithinQuotes = 0L;
				break;
			}
		}

		internal static bool DoesCharNeedEscaping(char c, bool withinQuotes)
		{
			if (!withinQuotes)
			{
				return DoesCharNeedEscaping(c);
			}
			return DoesCharNeedEscapingWithinQuotes(c);
		}

		internal static bool DoesCharNeedEscaping(char c)
		{
			if (mask_specialCharacters % (int)c == 0L)
			{
				return SPECIAL_CHARACTERS.IndexOf(c) >= 0;
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal static bool DoesCharNeedEscapingWithinQuotes(char c)
		{
			if (mask_specialCharactersWithinQuotes % (int)c == 0L)
			{
				return SPECIAL_CHARACTERS_WITHIN_QUOTES.IndexOf(c) >= 0;
			}
			return false;
		}

		internal static bool AreStringsEqual(string x, string y, bool ignoreCase)
		{
			return CompareStrings(x, y, ignoreCase) == 0;
		}

		internal static int CompareStrings(string x, string y, bool ignoreCase)
		{
			return string.Compare(x, y, ignoreCase ? StringComparison.InvariantCultureIgnoreCase : StringComparison.InvariantCulture);
		}

		public static string EscapeString(string text, bool withinQuotes)
		{
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = null;
			for (int i = 0; i < text.Length; i++)
			{
				char c = text[i];
				if (DoesCharNeedEscaping(c, withinQuotes) || ((i == 0 || 1 + i == text.Length) && !withinQuotes && char.IsWhiteSpace(c)))
				{
					if (stringBuilder == null)
					{
						stringBuilder = new StringBuilder(text, 0, i, 2 * text.Length - i);
					}
					stringBuilder.Append('\\').Append(c);
				}
				else
				{
					stringBuilder?.Append(c);
				}
			}
			if (stringBuilder != null)
			{
				return stringBuilder.ToString();
			}
			return text;
		}

		public static string UnEscapeString(string text)
		{
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = null;
			for (int i = 0; i < text.Length; i++)
			{
				char c = text[i];
				if ('\\' == c)
				{
					if (stringBuilder == null)
					{
						stringBuilder = new StringBuilder(text, 0, i, text.Length - 1);
					}
					i++;
					if (i < text.Length)
					{
						stringBuilder.Append(text[i]);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					stringBuilder?.Append(c);
				}
			}
			if (stringBuilder != null)
			{
				return stringBuilder.ToString();
			}
			return text;
		}

		public string NormalizeEscapement(string text, bool withinQuotes)
		{
			return EscapeString(UnEscapeString(text), withinQuotes);
		}

		internal static RefParser Parse(string refName)
		{
			return new RefParser(refName);
		}

		public static NameValuePair[] ParseNameValuePairs(string pairs, out string error)
		{
			error = ParseScopeHelper(pairs, out var nameValuePairs, out var _);
			return nameValuePairs;
		}

		internal static void TrimTrailingSpaces(StringBuilder sb, int lastEscapedCharIndex)
		{
			int num = sb.Length - 1;
			while (num >= 0 && num > lastEscapedCharIndex && char.IsWhiteSpace(sb[num]))
			{
				sb.Remove(num, 1);
				num--;
			}
		}

		internal static string ParseScopeHelper(string scope, out NameValuePair[] nameValuePairs, out string nonNameValuePairScope)
		{
			nameValuePairs = null;
			nonNameValuePairScope = null;
			bool flag = false;
			string result = null;
			ScopeParseState scopeParseState = ScopeParseState.Column;
			ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList(4);
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			StringBuilder stringBuilder2 = new StringBuilder();
			int lastEscapedCharIndex = -1;
			int i;
			for (i = 0; i < scope.Length; i++)
			{
				char c = scope[i];
				if ((ScopeParseState.Escape & scopeParseState) != ScopeParseState.Initial)
				{
					if ((ScopeParseState.Column & scopeParseState) != ScopeParseState.Initial)
					{
						lastEscapedCharIndex = stringBuilder.Length;
						stringBuilder.Append(c);
					}
					else
					{
						lastEscapedCharIndex = stringBuilder2.Length;
						stringBuilder2.Append(c);
					}
					scopeParseState ^= ScopeParseState.Escape;
				}
				else if ('\\' == c)
				{
					scopeParseState |= ScopeParseState.Escape;
				}
				else if (ScopeParseState.Quote == scopeParseState && '"' == c)
				{
					arrayList.Add(new NameValuePair(stringBuilder.ToString(), stringBuilder2.ToString()));
					int length = (stringBuilder2.Length = 0);
					stringBuilder.Length = length;
					lastEscapedCharIndex = -1;
					scopeParseState = ScopeParseState.Initial;
				}
				else if (ScopeParseState.Quote == scopeParseState)
				{
					stringBuilder2.Append(c);
				}
				else
				{
					if (char.IsWhiteSpace(c) && ((ScopeParseState.Column == scopeParseState && stringBuilder.Length == 0) || (ScopeParseState.Value == scopeParseState && stringBuilder2.Length == 0) || scopeParseState == ScopeParseState.Initial))
					{
						continue;
					}
					if (ScopeParseState.Column == scopeParseState && '=' == c)
					{
						if (stringBuilder.Length == 0)
						{
							result = SR.GetString("Error_RT_InvalidTuple_UnescapedCharacter", c, scope, i);
							flag = true;
							break;
						}
						TrimTrailingSpaces(stringBuilder, lastEscapedCharIndex);
						scopeParseState = ScopeParseState.Value;
						continue;
					}
					if (ScopeParseState.Value == scopeParseState && '"' == c)
					{
						if (stringBuilder2.Length > 0)
						{
							result = SR.GetString("Error_RT_InvalidTuple_UnescapedCharacter", c, scope, i);
							flag = true;
							break;
						}
						scopeParseState = ScopeParseState.Quote;
						continue;
					}
					if (ScopeParseState.Value == scopeParseState && ',' == c && stringBuilder2.Length != 0)
					{
						TrimTrailingSpaces(stringBuilder2, lastEscapedCharIndex);
						arrayList.Add(new NameValuePair(stringBuilder.ToString(), stringBuilder2.ToString()));
						int length = (stringBuilder2.Length = 0);
						stringBuilder.Length = length;
						lastEscapedCharIndex = -1;
						scopeParseState = ScopeParseState.Column;
						continue;
					}
					if (scopeParseState == ScopeParseState.Initial && ',' == c)
					{
						scopeParseState = ScopeParseState.Column;
						continue;
					}
					if (DoesCharNeedEscaping(c))
					{
						result = SR.GetString("Error_RT_InvalidTuple_UnescapedCharacter", c, scope, i);
						flag = true;
						break;
					}
					if (ScopeParseState.Column == scopeParseState)
					{
						stringBuilder.Append(c);
						continue;
					}
					if (ScopeParseState.Value != scopeParseState)
					{
						result = SR.GetString("Error_RT_InvalidScope", scope);
						flag = true;
						break;
					}
					stringBuilder2.Append(c);
				}
			}
			if (!flag)
			{
				TrimTrailingSpaces(stringBuilder2, lastEscapedCharIndex);
				if (i >= scope.Length && ScopeParseState.Value == scopeParseState && stringBuilder.Length != 0 && stringBuilder2.Length != 0)
				{
					arrayList.Add(new NameValuePair(stringBuilder.ToString(), stringBuilder2.ToString()));
				}
				else if (scopeParseState != ScopeParseState.Initial || i < scope.Length)
				{
					flag = true;
					result = ((ScopeParseState.Escape == scopeParseState) ? SR.GetString("Error_RT_InvalidTuple_UnescapedCharacter", scope[scope.Length - 1], scope, scope.Length - 1) : ((ScopeParseState.Quote != scopeParseState) ? SR.GetString("Error_RT_InvalidScope", scope) : SR.GetString("Error_RP_MissingEndQuotes", scope)));
				}
			}
			if (arrayList.Count <= 0)
			{
				scopeParseState = ScopeParseState.Value;
				stringBuilder2.Length = 0;
				i = 0;
				lastEscapedCharIndex = -1;
				for (; i < scope.Length; i++)
				{
					char c2 = scope[i];
					if ((ScopeParseState.Escape & scopeParseState) != ScopeParseState.Initial)
					{
						lastEscapedCharIndex = stringBuilder2.Length;
						stringBuilder2.Append(c2);
						scopeParseState ^= ScopeParseState.Escape;
					}
					else if ('\\' == c2)
					{
						scopeParseState |= ScopeParseState.Escape;
					}
					else if ('"' == c2 && ScopeParseState.Quote == scopeParseState)
					{
						scopeParseState = ScopeParseState.Initial;
					}
					else if ('"' == c2 && ScopeParseState.Value == scopeParseState)
					{
						if (stringBuilder2.Length > 0)
						{
							result = SR.GetString("Error_RT_InvalidTuple_UnescapedCharacter", c2, scope, i);
							break;
						}
						scopeParseState = ScopeParseState.Quote;
					}
					else if (scopeParseState == ScopeParseState.Initial)
					{
						if (!char.IsWhiteSpace(c2))
						{
							result = SR.GetString("Error_RT_InvalidScope_CharactersAfterClosingQuotes", scope, i);
							break;
						}
					}
					else if (ScopeParseState.Value != scopeParseState || stringBuilder2.Length != 0 || !char.IsWhiteSpace(c2))
					{
						if (ScopeParseState.Quote != scopeParseState && DoesCharNeedEscaping(c2))
						{
							result = SR.GetString("Error_RT_InvalidTuple_UnescapedCharacter", c2, scope, i);
							break;
						}
						stringBuilder2.Append(c2);
					}
				}
				if (i >= scope.Length)
				{
					TrimTrailingSpaces(stringBuilder2, lastEscapedCharIndex);
					nonNameValuePairScope = stringBuilder2.ToString();
					result = null;
				}
			}
			else if (!flag)
			{
				nameValuePairs = (NameValuePair[])arrayList.ToArray(typeof(NameValuePair));
			}
			return result;
		}

		private string ParseReference(string refName, bool maintainCase)
		{
			if (refName == null)
			{
				return SR.GetString("Error_RT_NullRefName");
			}
			int i;
			if (refName.StartsWith("//"))
			{
				flags |= RefParserFlags.FullyQualified | RefParserFlags.Root;
				i = "//".Length;
			}
			else if (refName.Length > 0 && refName[0] == '/')
			{
				flags &= ~RefParserFlags.FullyQualified;
				flags |= RefParserFlags.Root;
				i = 1;
			}
			else
			{
				flags &= ~(RefParserFlags.FullyQualified | RefParserFlags.Root);
				i = 0;
			}
			tuples.Clear();
			State state = State.Tuple;
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			for (; i < refName.Length; i++)
			{
				char c = refName[i];
				if ((State.Escape & state) != 0)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(c);
					state ^= State.Escape;
				}
				else if ('\\' == c)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(c);
					state |= State.Escape;
				}
				else if ('"' == c && (State.QuotedString & state) != 0)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(c);
					state ^= State.QuotedString;
				}
				else if ((State.QuotedString & state) != 0)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(c);
				}
				else if ('/' == c && State.TupleEnded == state)
				{
					state = State.Tuple;
				}
				else if ('/' == c && State.Tuple == state)
				{
					if (stringBuilder.Length == 0)
					{
						return SR.GetString("Error_RP_EmptyElement", refName, i);
					}
					tuples.Add(new RefTuple(stringBuilder.ToString(), maintainCase));
					stringBuilder.Length = 0;
				}
				else if ('(' == c && State.Tuple == state)
				{
					if (stringBuilder.Length == 0)
					{
						return SR.GetString("Error_RP_EmptyElement", refName, i);
					}
					stringBuilder.Append(c);
					state |= State.Scope;
				}
				else if (')' == c && (State.Scope & state) != 0)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(c);
					tuples.Add(new RefTuple(stringBuilder.ToString(), maintainCase));
					stringBuilder.Length = 0;
					state ^= State.Tuple | State.Scope;
					state |= State.TupleEnded;
				}
				else if ('"' == c && (State.Scope & state) != 0)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append(c);
					state |= State.QuotedString;
				}
				else
				{
					if ((State.Scope & state) == 0 && DoesCharNeedEscaping(c))
					{
						return SR.GetString("Error_RT_InvalidTuple_UnescapedCharacter", c, refName, i);
					}
					stringBuilder.Append(c);
				}
			}
			if ((State.Escape & state) != 0)
			{
				return SR.GetString("Error_RT_InvalidTuple_UnescapedCharacter", refName[refName.Length - 1], refName, refName.Length - 1);
			}
			if (State.Tuple == state)
			{
				if (stringBuilder.Length == 0)
				{
					if (tuples.Count <= 0)
					{
						return SR.GetString("Error_RP_EmptyReference", refName);
					}
					return SR.GetString("Error_RP_EmptyElement", refName, i);
				}
				tuples.Add(new RefTuple(stringBuilder.ToString(), maintainCase));
				stringBuilder.Length = 0;
			}
			else if (stringBuilder.Length > 0)
			{
				if ((State.QuotedString & state) != 0)
				{
					return SR.GetString("Error_RP_MissingEndQuotes", refName);
				}
				if ((State.Scope & state) != 0)
				{
					return SR.GetString("Error_RP_MissingCloseParenthesis", refName);
				}
			}
			MarkIndices();
			return null;
		}

		public bool Contains(RefParser inputParsed)
		{
			if (inputParsed.TupleCount < TupleCount)
			{
				return false;
			}
			IEnumerator enumerator = GetEnumerator();
			IEnumerator enumerator2 = inputParsed.GetEnumerator();
			bool flag = enumerator.MoveNext();
			if (!flag)
			{
				throw new UltraCalcException(SR.GetString("Error_RP_ContainsBase"));
			}
			bool flag2 = enumerator2.MoveNext();
			if (!flag2)
			{
				throw new UltraCalcException(SR.GetString("Error_RP_ContainsTarget"));
			}
			while (flag && flag2)
			{
				RefTuple refTuple = (RefTuple)enumerator.Current;
				RefTuple tuple = (RefTuple)enumerator2.Current;
				if (!refTuple.Contains(tuple))
				{
					return false;
				}
				flag = enumerator.MoveNext();
				flag2 = enumerator2.MoveNext();
			}
			return true;
		}

		public bool IsSubset(RefParser inputParsed)
		{
			if (inputParsed.TupleCount < TupleCount)
			{
				return false;
			}
			IEnumerator enumerator = GetEnumerator();
			IEnumerator enumerator2 = inputParsed.GetEnumerator();
			bool flag = enumerator.MoveNext();
			if (!flag)
			{
				throw new UltraCalcException(SR.GetString("Error_RP_ContainsBase"));
			}
			bool flag2 = enumerator2.MoveNext();
			if (!flag2)
			{
				throw new UltraCalcException(SR.GetString("Error_RP_ContainsTarget"));
			}
			while (flag && flag2)
			{
				RefTuple refTuple = (RefTuple)enumerator.Current;
				RefTuple tuple = (RefTuple)enumerator2.Current;
				if (!refTuple.IsSubset(tuple))
				{
					return false;
				}
				flag = enumerator.MoveNext();
				flag2 = enumerator2.MoveNext();
			}
			return true;
		}

		public void ToString(StringBuilder sb)
		{
			if (IsFullyQualified)
			{
				sb.Append("//");
			}
			else if (IsRoot)
			{
				sb.Append("/");
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < tuples.Count; i++)
			{
				if (i > 0)
				{
					sb.Append('/');
				}
				((RefTuple)tuples[i]).ToString(sb);
			}
		}

		public override string ToString()
		{
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			ToString(stringBuilder);
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		public IEnumerator GetEnumerator()
		{
			return tuples.GetEnumerator();
		}
	}
	public class RefUnAnchored : RefBase
	{
		protected RefBase baseRef;

		protected RefParser relativeRP;

		private int hasSameAbsoluteNameAsBaseRef = -1;

		public virtual bool IsUnQualifiedReference => RelativeReference.TupleCount == 1;

		public override string ElementName => baseRef.ElementName;

		public override RefBase BaseParent => baseRef.BaseParent;

		public override bool IsRange => baseRef.IsRange;

		public override bool IsAnchored => false;

		public override bool IsDataReference => baseRef.IsDataReference;

		public override RefBase WrappedReference => baseRef.WrappedReference;

		public override RefParser ParsedReference
		{
			get
			{
				return base.ParsedReference;
			}
			set
			{
				base.ParsedReference = value;
			}
		}

		public override string AbsoluteName => ParsedReference.ToString();

		public override RefParser RelativeReference
		{
			get
			{
				return relativeRP;
			}
			set
			{
				relativeRP = value;
				RefParser refParser = new RefParser();
				refParser.Merge(baseRef.ParsedReference, relativeRP, respectMarked: false);
				if (refParser.NextToLastTuple != null && relativeRP.IsRelative && refParser.LastTuple.Scope == RefTuple.RefScope.RelativeIndex && refParser.NextToLastTuple.Scope == RefTuple.RefScope.Index)
				{
					refParser.NextToLastTuple.ScopeIndex -= refParser.LastTuple.ScopeIndex;
				}
				ParsedReference = refParser;
			}
		}

		public override object Context => baseRef.Context;

		public override UltraCalcValue Value
		{
			get
			{
				return baseRef.Value;
			}
			set
			{
				baseRef.Value = value;
			}
		}

		public override IUltraCalcFormula Formula => baseRef.Formula;

		public override bool RecalcVisible
		{
			get
			{
				return baseRef.RecalcVisible;
			}
			set
			{
				baseRef.RecalcVisible = value;
			}
		}

		public override bool RecalcDeferred
		{
			get
			{
				return baseRef.RecalcDeferred;
			}
			set
			{
				baseRef.RecalcDeferred = value;
			}
		}

		public override bool HasRelativeIndex => relativeRP.HasRelativeIndex;

		public override bool HasScopeAll => relativeRP.HasScopeAll;

		public override bool HasAbsoluteIndex => relativeRP.HasAbsoluteIndex;

		public override IUltraCalcReferenceCollection References => baseRef.ScopedReferences(ParsedReference);

		public override bool IsEnumerable => baseRef.IsEnumerable;

		public override bool IsDisposedReference => baseRef.IsDisposedReference;

		public RefUnAnchored(RefBase baseRef)
		{
			this.baseRef = baseRef;
			relativeRP = null;
		}

		public override IUltraCalcReference FindItem(string name)
		{
			return baseRef.FindItem(name);
		}

		public override IUltraCalcReference FindAll(string name)
		{
			return baseRef.FindAll(name);
		}

		public override IUltraCalcReference FindSummaryItem(string name)
		{
			return baseRef.FindSummaryItem(name);
		}

		public override IUltraCalcReference FindItem(string name, string index)
		{
			return baseRef.FindItem(name, index);
		}

		public override IUltraCalcReference FindItem(string name, int index, bool isRelative)
		{
			return baseRef.FindItem(name, index, isRelative);
		}

		public override RefBase FindParent()
		{
			return baseRef.FindParent();
		}

		public override IUltraCalcReferenceCollection ScopedReferences(RefParser scopeRP)
		{
			return baseRef.ScopedReferences(scopeRP);
		}

		public override IUltraCalcReference ResolveReference(IUltraCalcReference reference, ResolveReferenceType referenceType)
		{
			return baseRef.ResolveReference(reference, referenceType);
		}

		public override bool ContainsReference(IUltraCalcReference reference)
		{
			return baseRef.ContainsReference(reference);
		}

		public override bool IsSubsetReference(IUltraCalcReference reference)
		{
			return baseRef.IsSubsetReference(reference);
		}

		public override bool IsSiblingReference(IUltraCalcReference reference)
		{
			return baseRef.IsSiblingReference(reference);
		}

		public override string ToString()
		{
			return $"{GetType()} ({baseRef.GetType()}) -{ParsedReference}";
		}

		public override int GetHashCode()
		{
			int num = baseRef.GetHashCode();
			if (-1 == hasSameAbsoluteNameAsBaseRef)
			{
				hasSameAbsoluteNameAsBaseRef = ((NormalizedAbsoluteName == baseRef.NormalizedAbsoluteName) ? 1 : 0);
			}
			if (hasSameAbsoluteNameAsBaseRef == 0)
			{
				num ^= NormalizedAbsoluteName.GetHashCode();
			}
			return num;
		}

		public override bool Equals(object obj)
		{
			if (obj is RefUnAnchored refUnAnchored)
			{
				if (baseRef.Equals(refUnAnchored.baseRef))
				{
					if (1 != hasSameAbsoluteNameAsBaseRef || 1 != refUnAnchored.hasSameAbsoluteNameAsBaseRef)
					{
						return NormalizedAbsoluteName == refUnAnchored.NormalizedAbsoluteName;
					}
					return true;
				}
				return false;
			}
			if (obj is IUltraCalcReference obj2 && 1 == hasSameAbsoluteNameAsBaseRef)
			{
				return baseRef.Equals(obj2);
			}
			return false;
		}
	}
	public class UCReference : IUltraCalcReference
	{
		private UltraCalcFormulaStates formulaStates;

		private string referenceName;

		private IUltraCalcReference reference;

		private bool connected;

		private bool isRange;

		private string fromName;

		private string toName;

		private string cachedUnconnectedElementName;

		private string originalReferenceName;

		private string lastAbsoluteName;

		private string lastNormalizedAbsoluteName;

		public object Context => null;

		public string AbsoluteName
		{
			get
			{
				if (connected)
				{
					return reference.AbsoluteName;
				}
				return referenceName;
			}
		}

		public string NormalizedAbsoluteName
		{
			get
			{
				string absoluteName = AbsoluteName;
				if (lastAbsoluteName == absoluteName)
				{
					return lastNormalizedAbsoluteName;
				}
				lastAbsoluteName = absoluteName;
				lastNormalizedAbsoluteName = ((!isRange) ? new RefParser(absoluteName).ToString() : (new RefParser(fromName).ToString() + " : " + new RefParser(toName).ToString()));
				return lastNormalizedAbsoluteName;
			}
		}

		public string ElementName
		{
			get
			{
				if (connected)
				{
					return reference.ElementName;
				}
				if (cachedUnconnectedElementName == null)
				{
					RefParser refParser = new RefParser(referenceName);
					cachedUnconnectedElementName = refParser.LastTuple.Name;
				}
				return cachedUnconnectedElementName;
			}
		}

		public UltraCalcValue Value
		{
			get
			{
				if (connected)
				{
					return reference.Value;
				}
				return new UltraCalcValue(new UltraCalcErrorValue(UltraCalcErrorCode.Reference));
			}
			set
			{
				if (connected)
				{
					reference.Value = value;
				}
			}
		}

		public IUltraCalcFormula Formula
		{
			get
			{
				if (connected)
				{
					return reference.Formula;
				}
				return null;
			}
		}

		public bool HasRelativeIndex
		{
			get
			{
				if (connected)
				{
					return reference.HasRelativeIndex;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public bool HasAbsoluteIndex
		{
			get
			{
				if (connected)
				{
					return reference.HasAbsoluteIndex;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public bool HasScopeAll
		{
			get
			{
				if (connected)
				{
					return reference.HasScopeAll;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public bool RecalcDeferred
		{
			get
			{
				if (connected)
				{
					return reference.RecalcDeferred;
				}
				return formulaStates.RecalcDeferred;
			}
			set
			{
				if (connected)
				{
					reference.RecalcDeferred = value;
				}
				else
				{
					formulaStates.RecalcDeferred = value;
				}
			}
		}

		public bool RecalcVisible
		{
			get
			{
				if (connected)
				{
					return reference.RecalcVisible;
				}
				return formulaStates.RecalcVisible;
			}
			set
			{
				if (connected)
				{
					reference.RecalcVisible = value;
				}
				else
				{
					formulaStates.RecalcVisible = value;
				}
			}
		}

		public IUltraCalcReferenceCollection References
		{
			get
			{
				if (connected)
				{
					return reference.References;
				}
				return null;
			}
		}

		public bool IsEnumerable
		{
			get
			{
				if (connected)
				{
					return reference.IsEnumerable;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public bool IsDataReference
		{
			get
			{
				if (connected)
				{
					return reference.IsDataReference;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public IUltraCalcReference Parent
		{
			get
			{
				if (connected)
				{
					return reference.Parent;
				}
				return null;
			}
		}

		public bool IsDisposedReference
		{
			get
			{
				if (connected)
				{
					return reference.IsDisposedReference;
				}
				return false;
			}
		}

		public virtual IUltraCalcReference Reference
		{
			get
			{
				if (connected)
				{
					return reference;
				}
				return this;
			}
		}

		public virtual string RootName
		{
			get
			{
				if (AbsoluteName.StartsWith("//"))
				{
					int num = AbsoluteName.IndexOf('/', 2);
					return AbsoluteName.Substring(2, (num == -1) ? (AbsoluteName.Length - 2) : (num - 2));
				}
				return null;
			}
		}

		public bool IsConnected => connected;

		private UCReference(bool isConnected, bool isRange)
		{
			connected = isConnected;
			this.isRange = isRange;
			formulaStates = default(UltraCalcFormulaStates);
		}

		public UCReference()
			: this(isConnected: false, isRange: false)
		{
		}

		public UCReference(string referenceName)
			: this(isConnected: false, isRange: false)
		{
			RefParser refParser = new RefParser(referenceName);
			this.referenceName = refParser.ToString();
			originalReferenceName = this.referenceName;
		}

		public UCReference(string fromName, string toName)
			: this(isConnected: false, isRange: true)
		{
			RefParser refParser = new RefParser(fromName);
			this.fromName = refParser.ToString();
			RefParser refParser2 = new RefParser(toName);
			this.toName = refParser2.ToString();
			referenceName = this.fromName + " : " + this.toName;
			originalReferenceName = referenceName;
		}

		internal UCReference(IUltraCalcReference reference)
			: this(isConnected: true, isRange: false)
		{
			this.reference = reference;
		}

		public IUltraCalcReference CreateReference(string reference)
		{
			if (connected)
			{
				return this.reference.CreateReference(reference);
			}
			return new UCReference(reference);
		}

		public IUltraCalcReference CreateRange(string fromReference, string toReference)
		{
			if (connected)
			{
				return reference.CreateRange(fromReference, toReference);
			}
			return new UCReference(fromReference, toReference);
		}

		public IUltraCalcReference ResolveReference(IUltraCalcReference reference, ResolveReferenceType referenceType)
		{
			if (connected)
			{
				return this.reference.ResolveReference(reference, referenceType);
			}
			return reference;
		}

		public void MarkRelativeIndices(IUltraCalcReference reference)
		{
			if (connected)
			{
				this.reference.MarkRelativeIndices(reference);
			}
		}

		public bool ContainsReference(IUltraCalcReference reference)
		{
			if (connected)
			{
				return this.reference.ContainsReference(reference);
			}
			return NormalizedAbsoluteName == reference.NormalizedAbsoluteName;
		}

		public bool IsSubsetReference(IUltraCalcReference reference)
		{
			if (connected)
			{
				return this.reference.IsSubsetReference(reference);
			}
			return string.Compare(referenceName, reference.AbsoluteName) == 0;
		}

		public bool IsSiblingReference(IUltraCalcReference reference)
		{
			if (connected)
			{
				return this.reference.IsSiblingReference(reference);
			}
			return false;
		}

		IUltraCalcReference[] IUltraCalcReference.GetChildReferences(ChildReferenceType referenceType)
		{
			if (!connected || reference == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return reference.GetChildReferences(referenceType);
		}

		public bool Connect(IUltraCalcReference reference)
		{
			if (!connected)
			{
				referenceName = originalReferenceName;
				cachedUnconnectedElementName = null;
				if (isRange)
				{
					this.reference = reference.CreateRange(fromName, toName);
				}
				else
				{
					this.reference = reference.CreateReference(referenceName);
					if (this.reference != null && !(this.reference is UltraCalcReferenceError))
					{
						referenceName = this.reference.AbsoluteName;
						cachedUnconnectedElementName = null;
					}
				}
				if (this.reference != null && !(this.reference is UltraCalcReferenceError))
				{
					formulaStates.Reset();
					connected = true;
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		public bool Disconnect()
		{
			if (connected)
			{
				formulaStates = default(UltraCalcFormulaStates);
				reference = null;
				connected = false;
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public bool Reconnect(IUltraCalcReference reference)
		{
			bool flag = connected;
			IUltraCalcReference ultraCalcReference = this.reference;
			Disconnect();
			Connect(reference);
			if (flag == connected)
			{
				if (ultraCalcReference != this.reference)
				{
					if (ultraCalcReference != null && this.reference != null)
					{
						return !ultraCalcReference.Equals(this.reference);
					}
					return true;
				}
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		public override int GetHashCode()
		{
			return NormalizedAbsoluteName.GetHashCode();
		}

		public override bool Equals(object obj)
		{
			if (!(obj is IUltraCalcReference))
			{
				return false;
			}
			UCReference uCReference = obj as UCReference;
			if (uCReference != null && uCReference.connected && connected)
			{
				IUltraCalcReference ultraCalcReference = reference;
				IUltraCalcReference ultraCalcReference2 = uCReference.reference;
				if (ultraCalcReference != ultraCalcReference2)
				{
					if (ultraCalcReference != null && ultraCalcReference2 != null)
					{
						return ultraCalcReference.Equals(ultraCalcReference2);
					}
					return false;
				}
				return true;
			}
			if (uCReference == null && connected && Reference != null)
			{
				return Reference.Equals(obj);
			}
			return NormalizedAbsoluteName == ((IUltraCalcReference)obj).NormalizedAbsoluteName;
		}
	}
}
namespace Infragistics.Shared
{
	public interface ISparseArrayItem
	{
		object GetOwnerData(SparseArray context);

		void SetOwnerData(object ownerData, SparseArray context);
	}
	public interface ISparseArrayMultiItem : ISparseArrayItem
	{
		int ScrollCount { get; }

		object GetItemAtScrollIndex(int scrollIndex);
	}
	public interface ICreateItemCallback
	{
		object CreateItem(SparseArray array, int relativeIndex);
	}
	public class SparseArray : IList, ICollection, IEnumerable
	{
		private class Node
		{
			internal Node parent;

			internal Node left;

			internal Node right;

			internal int leftCount;

			internal int rightCount;

			internal int count;

			internal object[] arr;

			internal int TotalCount => leftCount + rightCount + count;

			internal object[] ItemsArray
			{
				get
				{
					if (arr == null)
					{
						return arr = new object[count];
					}
					return arr;
				}
			}

			internal Node()
			{
			}

			internal Node(Node parent)
			{
				this.parent = parent;
			}

			public override string ToString()
			{
				StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
				stringBuilder.Append(GetType().Name).Append(" ").Append(GetHashCode());
				stringBuilder.Append(" ").Append((parent == null) ? "no parent" : ((this == parent.left) ? "lc" : ((this == parent.right) ? "rc" : "invalid")));
				return stringBuilder.ToString();
			}
		}

		private class NodeExtended : Node
		{
			internal int visibleCount;

			internal int leftVisibleCount;

			internal int rightVisibleCount;

			internal int scrollCount;

			internal int leftScrollCount;

			internal int rightScrollCount;

			internal bool isNodeDirty;

			internal bool isLeftDirty;

			internal bool isRightDirty;

			internal int TotalVisibleCount => leftVisibleCount + visibleCount + rightVisibleCount;

			internal int TotalScrollCount => leftScrollCount + scrollCount + rightScrollCount;

			internal NodeExtended()
			{
			}

			internal NodeExtended(NodeExtended parent)
				: base(parent)
			{
			}
		}

		private class Enumerator : IEnumerator
		{
			private SparseArray sarr;

			private int nodeIndex = -1;

			private Node currNode;

			object IEnumerator.Current
			{
				get
				{
					EnsureNotExhausted();
					if (nodeIndex < 0 || nodeIndex >= currNode.count || currNode.arr == null)
					{
						return null;
					}
					return currNode.arr[nodeIndex];
				}
			}

			internal Enumerator(SparseArray sarr)
			{
				this.sarr = sarr;
				((IEnumerator)this).Reset();
			}

			private void EnsureNotExhausted()
			{
				if (currNode == null)
				{
					throw new InvalidOperationException(SR.GetString("LE_InvalidOperationException_1"));
				}
			}

			void IEnumerator.Reset()
			{
				currNode = GetMinimum(sarr.tree);
				nodeIndex = -currNode.leftCount - 1;
			}

			bool IEnumerator.MoveNext()
			{
				EnsureNotExhausted();
				nodeIndex++;
				if (nodeIndex < currNode.count)
				{
					return true;
				}
				if (nodeIndex < currNode.count + currNode.rightCount && currNode.right == null)
				{
					return true;
				}
				currNode = GetSuccessor(currNode);
				if (currNode != null)
				{
					nodeIndex = ((currNode.left == null) ? (-currNode.leftCount) : 0);
				}
				return currNode != null;
			}
		}

		private class CreateItemEnumerator : IEnumerator
		{
			private SparseArray sarr;

			private int index = -1;

			private ICreateItemCallback createItemCallback;

			object IEnumerator.Current
			{
				get
				{
					EnsureNotExhausted();
					return sarr.GetItem(index, createItemCallback);
				}
			}

			internal CreateItemEnumerator(SparseArray sarr, ICreateItemCallback createItemCallback)
			{
				this.sarr = sarr;
				this.createItemCallback = createItemCallback;
				((IEnumerator)this).Reset();
			}

			private void EnsureNotExhausted()
			{
				if (index >= sarr.Count)
				{
					throw new InvalidOperationException(SR.GetString("LE_InvalidOperationException_1"));
				}
			}

			void IEnumerator.Reset()
			{
				index = -1;
			}

			bool IEnumerator.MoveNext()
			{
				EnsureNotExhausted();
				index++;
				return index < sarr.Count;
			}
		}

		private class NonNullEnumerator : IEnumerator
		{
			private SparseArray sarr;

			private int nodeIndex = -1;

			private Node currNode;

			object IEnumerator.Current
			{
				get
				{
					EnsureNotExhausted();
					if (currNode.arr == null)
					{
						return null;
					}
					return currNode.arr[nodeIndex];
				}
			}

			internal NonNullEnumerator(SparseArray sarr)
			{
				this.sarr = sarr;
				((IEnumerator)this).Reset();
			}

			private void EnsureNotExhausted()
			{
				if (currNode == null)
				{
					throw new InvalidOperationException(SR.GetString("LE_InvalidOperationException_1"));
				}
			}

			void IEnumerator.Reset()
			{
				currNode = GetMinimum(sarr.tree);
				nodeIndex = -1;
			}

			bool IEnumerator.MoveNext()
			{
				EnsureNotExhausted();
				nodeIndex++;
				while (currNode != null && (nodeIndex >= currNode.count || currNode.arr == null || currNode.arr[nodeIndex] == null))
				{
					nodeIndex++;
					if (nodeIndex >= currNode.count)
					{
						currNode = GetSuccessor(currNode);
						nodeIndex = 0;
					}
				}
				return currNode != null;
			}
		}

		private class NonNullEnumerable : IEnumerable
		{
			private SparseArray sarr;

			internal NonNullEnumerable(SparseArray sarr)
			{
				this.sarr = sarr;
			}

			IEnumerator IEnumerable.GetEnumerator()
			{
				return new NonNullEnumerator(sarr);
			}
		}

		private const int DEFAULT_FACTOR = 40;

		private const float DEFAULT_GROWTH_FACTOR = 0.25f;

		private const int NULL_ITEM_VISIBLE_COUNT = 1;

		private const int NULL_ITEM_SCROLL_COUNT = 1;

		private Node tree;

		private int factor;

		private int growBy;

		private bool scrollCountInfoDirtyOnAllNodes;

		private bool useOwnerData;

		private bool manageScrollCounts;

		private bool onScrollCountChangedCalledBefore;

		public int Count => tree.TotalCount;

		public object this[int index]
		{
			get
			{
				return GetItem(index, null);
			}
			set
			{
				SetItem(index, value);
			}
		}

		public bool IsSynchronized => false;

		public object SyncRoot => this;

		public IEnumerable NonNullItems => new NonNullEnumerable(this);

		private int MaxFactor => factor + factor - 1;

		bool IList.IsReadOnly => false;

		bool IList.IsFixedSize => false;

		public SparseArray()
			: this(40)
		{
		}

		public SparseArray(bool useOwnerData)
			: this(40, 0.25f, useOwnerData)
		{
		}

		public SparseArray(int factor)
			: this(factor, 0.25f)
		{
		}

		public SparseArray(int factor, float growthFactor)
			: this(factor, growthFactor, useOwnerData: false)
		{
		}

		public SparseArray(int factor, float growthFactor, bool useOwnerData)
			: this(factor, growthFactor, useOwnerData, manageScrollCounts: false)
		{
		}

		protected SparseArray(bool manageScrollCounts, int factor, float growthFactor)
			: this(factor, growthFactor, useOwnerData: true, manageScrollCounts)
		{
		}

		private SparseArray(int factor, float growthFactor, bool useOwnerData, bool manageScrollCounts)
		{
			if (factor < 2)
			{
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("factor", factor, SR.GetString("LE_ArgumentOutOfRangeException_1"));
			}
			if (growthFactor <= 0f || growthFactor > 1f)
			{
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("growthFactor", growthFactor, SR.GetString("LE_ArgumentOutOfRangeException_2"));
			}
			this.factor = factor;
			growBy = Math.Max(1, (int)((float)factor * growthFactor));
			this.useOwnerData = useOwnerData || manageScrollCounts;
			this.manageScrollCounts = manageScrollCounts;
			tree = CreateNode();
		}

		public void Expand(int newCount)
		{
			if (newCount < Count)
			{
				throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("newCount", newCount, SR.GetString("LE_ArgumentOutOfRangeException_3"));
			}
			int num = newCount - Count;
			Node right = tree;
			while (right.right != null)
			{
				right.rightCount += num;
				right = right.right;
			}
			bool setIsNodeDirtyFlag = false;
			if (right.count < factor)
			{
				int num2 = Math.Min(num, factor - right.count);
				Lend(null, right, num2, toLeft: false);
				num -= num2;
				setIsNodeDirtyFlag = true;
			}
			right.rightCount += num;
			DirtyScrollCount(right, setIsNodeDirtyFlag, overallScrollCountChanged: true);
			MaintainAncestors(right);
		}

		public int Add(object item)
		{
			Node right = tree;
			while (right.right != null)
			{
				right.rightCount++;
				right = right.right;
			}
			if (right.rightCount <= 0)
			{
				Array_Insert(right, right.count, item);
				DirtyScrollCount_OverallScrollCountChanged(right);
				FixupNode(right);
			}
			else
			{
				right.rightCount++;
				if (item != null)
				{
					SetItem(Count - 1, item);
				}
			}
			return Count - 1;
		}

		public void Insert(int index, object item)
		{
			if (index >= 0 && index < Count)
			{
				int index2 = index;
				Node nodeContainingIndex = GetNodeContainingIndex(ref index, item != null);
				if (nodeContainingIndex != null)
				{
					Array_Insert(nodeContainingIndex, index, item);
					ApplyDeltaToAncestors(nodeContainingIndex, 1);
					FixupNode(nodeContainingIndex);
				}
				else
				{
					ApplyDeltaToDescendantPath(index2, 1);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				if (Count != index)
				{
					throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("index");
				}
				Add(item);
			}
		}

		public void AddRange(ICollection items)
		{
			InsertRange(Count, items);
		}

		public void InsertRange(int index, ICollection items)
		{
			if (index < 0)
			{
				ValidateIndex(index);
			}
			if (index > Count)
			{
				ValidateIndex(index);
			}
			int count = items.Count;
			if (count <= 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			Expand(Count + count);
			for (int num = Count - 1 - count; num >= index; num--)
			{
				this[num + count] = this[num];
			}
			IEnumerator enumerator = items.GetEnumerator();
			enumerator.Reset();
			bool flag = true;
			while (flag && enumerator.MoveNext())
			{
				object current = enumerator.Current;
				int indexVal = index;
				Node nodeContainingIndex = GetNodeContainingIndex(ref indexVal, current != null);
				index++;
				if (nodeContainingIndex != null)
				{
					object[] itemsArray = nodeContainingIndex.ItemsArray;
					itemsArray[indexVal++] = current;
					SetOwnerDataHelper(current, nodeContainingIndex);
					while (indexVal < nodeContainingIndex.count && (flag = enumerator.MoveNext()))
					{
						current = enumerator.Current;
						SetOwnerDataHelper(current, nodeContainingIndex);
						itemsArray[indexVal++] = current;
						index++;
					}
					DirtyScrollCount_OverallScrollCountChanged(nodeContainingIndex);
				}
			}
		}

		public void RemoveAt(int index)
		{
			ValidateIndex(index);
			int index2 = index;
			Node nodeContainingIndex = GetNodeContainingIndex(ref index, forceCreation: false);
			if (nodeContainingIndex != null)
			{
				Array_Remove(nodeContainingIndex, index);
				ApplyDeltaToAncestors(nodeContainingIndex, -1);
				FixupNode(nodeContainingIndex);
			}
			else
			{
				ApplyDeltaToDescendantPath(index2, -1);
			}
		}

		public int IndexOf(object item)
		{
			if (useOwnerData)
			{
				if (GetOwnerDataHelper(item) is Node node)
				{
					int nodeStartIndex = GetNodeStartIndex(node);
					if (nodeStartIndex >= 0 && node.arr != null)
					{
						int num = Array.IndexOf(node.arr, item);
						if (num >= 0)
						{
							return nodeStartIndex + num;
						}
					}
				}
				return -1;
			}
			return IndexOf(tree, item);
		}

		public void RemoveRange(int index, int count)
		{
			if (count <= 0)
			{
				if (count != 0)
				{
					throw new ArgumentOutOfRangeException("count", count, SR.GetString("LE_ArgumentOutOfRangeException_4"));
				}
			}
			else
			{
				int num = index + count - 1;
				ValidateIndex(index);
				ValidateIndex(num);
				RemoveRangeHelper(index, num);
			}
		}

		public void Remove(object value)
		{
			int num = IndexOf(value);
			if (num >= 0)
			{
				RemoveAt(num);
			}
		}

		public bool Contains(object value)
		{
			return IndexOf(value) >= 0;
		}

		public void Clear()
		{
			ClearOwnerDataTreeWide(tree);
			tree = CreateNode();
			OnScrollCountChanged();
		}

		public void CopyTo(Array array, int index)
		{
			CopyTo(array, index, null);
		}

		public void CopyTo(Array array, int index, ICreateItemCallback callback)
		{
			if (array.Length - index < Count)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException(SR.GetString("LE_ArgumentException_3"), "array");
			}
			if (callback == null)
			{
				CopyToHelper(tree, array, ref index);
				return;
			}
			IEnumerator enumerator = GetEnumerator(callback);
			while (enumerator.MoveNext())
			{
				array.SetValue(enumerator.Current, index++);
			}
		}

		public object[] ToArray()
		{
			return (object[])ToArray(typeof(object));
		}

		public Array ToArray(Type type)
		{
			Array array = Array.CreateInstance(type, Count);
			CopyTo(array, 0);
			return array;
		}

		public object GetItem(int index)
		{
			return GetItem(index, null);
		}

		public object GetItem(int index, ICreateItemCallback createItemCallback)
		{
			ValidateIndex(index);
			int indexVal = index;
			Node nodeContainingIndex = GetNodeContainingIndex(ref indexVal, createItemCallback != null);
			if (nodeContainingIndex != null)
			{
				if (createItemCallback != null && (nodeContainingIndex.arr == null || nodeContainingIndex.arr[indexVal] == null))
				{
					CreateItemHelper(createItemCallback, nodeContainingIndex, indexVal, index);
				}
				if (nodeContainingIndex.arr == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				return nodeContainingIndex.arr[indexVal];
			}
			return null;
		}

		public void Reverse()
		{
			Reverse(0, Count);
		}

		public void Reverse(int index, int count)
		{
			if (count >= 1)
			{
				int num = index;
				int num2 = index + count - 1;
				ValidateIndex(num);
				ValidateIndex(num2);
				while (num < num2)
				{
					object value = this[num];
					this[num] = this[num2];
					this[num2] = value;
					num++;
					num2--;
				}
			}
		}

		public void Sort()
		{
			Sort(null);
		}

		public void Sort(IComparer comparer)
		{
			object[] array = (object[])ToArray(typeof(object));
			Array.Sort(array, comparer);
			Clear();
			AddRange(array);
		}

		protected virtual object GetOwnerData(object item)
		{
			if (!(item is ISparseArrayItem sparseArrayItem))
			{
				return null;
			}
			return sparseArrayItem.GetOwnerData(this);
		}

		protected virtual void SetOwnerData(object item, object ownerData)
		{
			if (item is ISparseArrayItem sparseArrayItem)
			{
				sparseArrayItem.SetOwnerData(ownerData, this);
			}
		}

		protected int GetVisibleCount()
		{
			EnsureScrollCountCalculated();
			return ((NodeExtended)tree).TotalVisibleCount;
		}

		protected int GetScrollCount()
		{
			EnsureScrollCountCalculated();
			return ((NodeExtended)tree).TotalScrollCount;
		}

		protected int GetVisibleIndexOf(ISparseArrayMultiItem item)
		{
			return GetVisibleIndexOf(item, ignoreItemHiddenState: false);
		}

		protected int GetScrollIndexOf(ISparseArrayMultiItem item)
		{
			return GetScrollIndexOf(item, ignoreItemHiddenState: false);
		}

		protected int GetScrollIndexOf(ISparseArrayMultiItem item, bool ignoreItemHiddenState)
		{
			if (!manageScrollCounts)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException();
			}
			EnsureScrollCountCalculated();
			if (!ignoreItemHiddenState && item.ScrollCount <= 0)
			{
				return -1;
			}
			if (GetOwnerDataHelper(item) is NodeExtended nodeExtended)
			{
				int nodeStartScrollIndex = GetNodeStartScrollIndex(nodeExtended);
				if (nodeStartScrollIndex >= 0)
				{
					int num = 0;
					if (nodeExtended.arr != null)
					{
						bool flag = false;
						int num2 = 0;
						while (!flag && num2 < nodeExtended.count)
						{
							ISparseArrayMultiItem sparseArrayMultiItem = (ISparseArrayMultiItem)nodeExtended.arr[num2];
							if (sparseArrayMultiItem != item)
							{
								num += GetItemScrollCount(sparseArrayMultiItem);
							}
							else
							{
								flag = true;
							}
							num2++;
						}
						if (flag)
						{
							return nodeStartScrollIndex + num;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			return -1;
		}

		protected object GetItemAtVisibleIndexOffset(ISparseArrayMultiItem startItem, int offset, ICreateItemCallback createItemCallback)
		{
			int visibleIndexOf = GetVisibleIndexOf(startItem, ignoreItemHiddenState: true);
			if (visibleIndexOf < 0)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return GetItemAtVisibleIndex(visibleIndexOf + offset, createItemCallback);
		}

		protected object GetItemAtVisibleIndex(int visibleIndex, ICreateItemCallback createItemCallback)
		{
			if (!manageScrollCounts)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException();
			}
			if (visibleIndex < 0 || visibleIndex >= GetVisibleCount())
			{
				return null;
			}
			int num = visibleIndex;
			int num2 = 0;
			while (num < GetVisibleCount())
			{
				num2++;
				visibleIndex = num;
				NodeExtended nodeExtended = (NodeExtended)tree;
				bool flag = false;
				NodeExtended nodeExtended2 = null;
				while (nodeExtended != null)
				{
					nodeExtended2 = nodeExtended;
					if (visibleIndex < nodeExtended.leftVisibleCount)
					{
						flag = true;
						nodeExtended = (NodeExtended)nodeExtended.left;
						continue;
					}
					visibleIndex -= nodeExtended.leftVisibleCount;
					if (visibleIndex < nodeExtended.visibleCount)
					{
						break;
					}
					visibleIndex -= nodeExtended.visibleCount;
					nodeExtended = (NodeExtended)nodeExtended.right;
					flag = false;
				}
				if (nodeExtended == null)
				{
					if (createItemCallback != null)
					{
						int nodeStartIndex = GetNodeStartIndex(nodeExtended2);
						if (flag)
						{
							nodeStartIndex -= (nodeExtended2.leftVisibleCount - visibleIndex) / 1;
							nodeStartIndex = Math.Max(0, nodeStartIndex);
						}
						else
						{
							nodeStartIndex += nodeExtended2.count;
							nodeStartIndex += visibleIndex / 1;
							nodeStartIndex = Math.Min(Count - 1, nodeStartIndex);
						}
						GetNodeContainingIndex(ref nodeStartIndex, forceCreation: true);
						if (num2 > Count)
						{
							return null;
						}
						continue;
					}
					return null;
				}
				if (nodeExtended.arr == null && createItemCallback == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				bool flag2 = false;
				for (int i = 0; i < nodeExtended.count; i++)
				{
					ISparseArrayMultiItem sparseArrayMultiItem = (ISparseArrayMultiItem)nodeExtended.ItemsArray[i];
					int itemVisibleCount = GetItemVisibleCount(sparseArrayMultiItem);
					if (visibleIndex < itemVisibleCount)
					{
						if (sparseArrayMultiItem == null && createItemCallback != null)
						{
							int overallIndex = GetNodeStartIndex(nodeExtended) + i;
							sparseArrayMultiItem = (ISparseArrayMultiItem)CreateItemHelper(createItemCallback, nodeExtended, i, overallIndex);
							if (sparseArrayMultiItem != null)
							{
								flag2 = true;
								i--;
								continue;
							}
						}
						return sparseArrayMultiItem;
					}
					visibleIndex -= itemVisibleCount;
				}
				if (!flag2)
				{
					DirtyScrollCountInfo();
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		protected object GetItemAtScrollIndex(int scrollIndex, ICreateItemCallback createItemCallback)
		{
			if (!manageScrollCounts)
			{
				throw new InvalidOperationException();
			}
			if (scrollIndex < 0 || scrollIndex >= GetScrollCount())
			{
				return null;
			}
			int num = scrollIndex;
			int num2 = 0;
			while (num < GetScrollCount())
			{
				num2++;
				scrollIndex = num;
				NodeExtended nodeExtended = (NodeExtended)tree;
				bool flag = false;
				NodeExtended nodeExtended2 = null;
				while (nodeExtended != null)
				{
					nodeExtended2 = nodeExtended;
					if (scrollIndex < nodeExtended.leftScrollCount)
					{
						flag = true;
						nodeExtended = (NodeExtended)nodeExtended.left;
						continue;
					}
					scrollIndex -= nodeExtended.leftScrollCount;
					if (scrollIndex < nodeExtended.scrollCount)
					{
						break;
					}
					scrollIndex -= nodeExtended.scrollCount;
					nodeExtended = (NodeExtended)nodeExtended.right;
					flag = false;
				}
				if (nodeExtended == null)
				{
					if (createItemCallback != null)
					{
						int nodeStartIndex = GetNodeStartIndex(nodeExtended2);
						if (flag)
						{
							nodeStartIndex -= (nodeExtended2.leftScrollCount - scrollIndex) / 1;
							nodeStartIndex = Math.Max(0, nodeStartIndex);
						}
						else
						{
							nodeStartIndex += nodeExtended2.count;
							nodeStartIndex += scrollIndex / 1;
							nodeStartIndex = Math.Min(Count - 1, nodeStartIndex);
						}
						GetNodeContainingIndex(ref nodeStartIndex, forceCreation: true);
						if (num2 > Count)
						{
							return null;
						}
						continue;
					}
					return null;
				}
				if (nodeExtended.arr == null && createItemCallback == null)
				{
					return null;
				}
				bool flag2 = false;
				for (int i = 0; i < nodeExtended.count; i++)
				{
					ISparseArrayMultiItem sparseArrayMultiItem = (ISparseArrayMultiItem)nodeExtended.ItemsArray[i];
					int itemScrollCount = GetItemScrollCount(sparseArrayMultiItem);
					if (scrollIndex < itemScrollCount)
					{
						if (sparseArrayMultiItem == null && createItemCallback != null)
						{
							int overallIndex = GetNodeStartIndex(nodeExtended) + i;
							sparseArrayMultiItem = (ISparseArrayMultiItem)CreateItemHelper(createItemCallback, nodeExtended, i, overallIndex);
							if (sparseArrayMultiItem != null)
							{
								flag2 = true;
								i--;
								continue;
							}
						}
						return sparseArrayMultiItem?.GetItemAtScrollIndex(scrollIndex);
					}
					scrollIndex -= itemScrollCount;
				}
				if (!flag2)
				{
					DirtyScrollCountInfo();
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		protected void NotifyItemScrollCountChanged(ISparseArrayMultiItem item)
		{
			if (item != null && !scrollCountInfoDirtyOnAllNodes && GetOwnerDataHelper(item) is NodeExtended node)
			{
				DirtyScrollCount_OverallScrollCountChanged(node);
			}
		}

		protected void DirtyScrollCountInfo()
		{
			if (!scrollCountInfoDirtyOnAllNodes)
			{
				scrollCountInfoDirtyOnAllNodes = true;
				OnScrollCountChanged();
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnScrollCountChanged()
		{
		}

		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		public void OnDeserializationComplete()
		{
			if (useOwnerData && tree != null)
			{
				SetOwnerDataTreeWide(tree);
			}
		}

		private void ValidateIndex(int index)
		{
			if (index < 0 || index >= Count)
			{
				throw new IndexOutOfRangeException();
			}
		}

		private void SetItem(int index, object item)
		{
			ValidateIndex(index);
			Node nodeContainingIndex = GetNodeContainingIndex(ref index, item != null);
			if (nodeContainingIndex == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			object[] itemsArray = nodeContainingIndex.ItemsArray;
			bool flag = false;
			if (manageScrollCounts)
			{
				flag = true;
				object obj = itemsArray[index];
				if (obj == null && 1 == GetItemScrollCount((ISparseArrayMultiItem)item))
				{
					flag = false;
				}
			}
			itemsArray[index] = item;
			SetOwnerDataHelper(item, nodeContainingIndex);
			if (flag)
			{
				DirtyScrollCount_OverallScrollCountChanged(nodeContainingIndex);
			}
		}

		private void ApplyDeltaToAncestors(Node n, int delta)
		{
			DirtyScrollCount_OverallScrollCountChanged(n);
			while (n.parent != null)
			{
				if (n == n.parent.left)
				{
					n.parent.leftCount += delta;
				}
				else
				{
					n.parent.rightCount += delta;
				}
				n = n.parent;
			}
		}

		private void ApplyDeltaToDescendantPath(int index, int delta)
		{
			Node node = tree;
			Node node2 = null;
			while (node != null)
			{
				node2 = node;
				if (index < node.leftCount)
				{
					node.leftCount += delta;
					node = node.left;
					continue;
				}
				index -= node.leftCount;
				if (index < node.count)
				{
					break;
				}
				index -= node.count;
				node.rightCount += delta;
				node = node.right;
			}
			DirtyScrollCount(node2, setIsNodeDirtyFlag: false, overallScrollCountChanged: true);
		}

		private void RemoveRangeHelper(int startIndex, int endIndex)
		{
			while (startIndex <= endIndex)
			{
				int indexVal = startIndex;
				int indexVal2 = endIndex;
				Node nodeContainingIndex = GetNodeContainingIndex(ref indexVal, forceCreation: true);
				Node nodeContainingIndex2 = GetNodeContainingIndex(ref indexVal2, forceCreation: true);
				if (nodeContainingIndex == nodeContainingIndex2)
				{
					int num = 1 + indexVal2 - indexVal;
					Array_RemoveRange(nodeContainingIndex, indexVal, num, clearOwnerData: true);
					ApplyDeltaToAncestors(nodeContainingIndex, -num);
					FixupNode(nodeContainingIndex);
					endIndex -= num;
				}
				else if (indexVal == 0 || nodeContainingIndex2.count - 1 == indexVal2)
				{
					Node node = ((indexVal == 0) ? nodeContainingIndex : nodeContainingIndex2);
					int count = node.count;
					Array_RemoveRange(node, 0, count, clearOwnerData: true);
					ApplyDeltaToAncestors(node, -count);
					FixupNode(node);
					endIndex -= count;
				}
				else
				{
					RemoveRangeHelper(startIndex, endIndex - (indexVal2 + 1));
					endIndex = startIndex + indexVal2;
				}
			}
		}

		private bool CanBorrow(Node n, int itemCount)
		{
			return n.count - itemCount >= factor;
		}

		private object[] Expand_Collapse(Node n, int capacity)
		{
			int num = ((n.arr != null) ? n.arr.Length : 0);
			if (capacity <= num && num - capacity < growBy)
			{
				return n.arr;
			}
			int num2 = capacity / growBy * growBy;
			if (num2 < capacity)
			{
				num2 += growBy;
			}
			if (num2 > MaxFactor + 1)
			{
				num2 = Math.Max(capacity, MaxFactor + 1);
			}
			object[] arr = n.arr;
			n.arr = new object[num2];
			return arr;
		}

		private object GetOwnerDataHelper(object item)
		{
			if (!useOwnerData || item == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			return GetOwnerData(item);
		}

		private void SetOwnerDataHelper(Node n, int startIndex, int count)
		{
			SetOwnerDataHelper(n.arr, n, startIndex, count);
		}

		private void SetOwnerDataHelper(object[] arr, object ownerData, int startIndex, int count)
		{
			if (useOwnerData && arr != null)
			{
				count += startIndex;
				for (int i = startIndex; i < count; i++)
				{
					SetOwnerDataHelper(arr[i], ownerData);
				}
			}
		}

		private void SetOwnerDataHelper(object item, object ownerData)
		{
			if (useOwnerData && item != null)
			{
				SetOwnerData(item, ownerData);
			}
		}

		private void ClearOwnerData(Node n, int startIndex, int count)
		{
			if (n != null)
			{
				ClearOwnerData(n.arr, startIndex, count);
			}
		}

		private void ClearOwnerData(object[] arr, int startIndex, int count)
		{
			SetOwnerDataHelper(arr, null, startIndex, count);
		}

		private void Array_Insert(Node n, int index, object item)
		{
			if (n.arr != null || item != null)
			{
				object[] array = Expand_Collapse(n, 1 + n.count);
				if (array != null)
				{
					if (array != n.arr && index > 0)
					{
						Array.Copy(array, 0, n.arr, 0, index);
					}
					Array.Copy(array, index, n.arr, index + 1, n.count - index);
				}
				n.arr[index] = item;
				SetOwnerDataHelper(item, n);
			}
			n.count++;
		}

		private void Array_Remove(Node n, int index)
		{
			Array_RemoveRange(n, index, 1, clearOwnerData: true);
		}

		private void Array_RemoveRange(Node n, int index, int count, bool clearOwnerData)
		{
			if (n.arr != null)
			{
				if (count < n.count)
				{
					object[] array = Expand_Collapse(n, n.count - count);
					if (array != n.arr && index > 0)
					{
						Array.Copy(array, 0, n.arr, 0, index);
					}
					if (clearOwnerData)
					{
						ClearOwnerData(array, index, count);
					}
					if (index + count < n.count)
					{
						Array.Copy(array, index + count, n.arr, index, n.count - (index + count));
					}
					if (array == n.arr)
					{
						Array.Clear(array, n.count - count, count);
					}
				}
				else
				{
					if (clearOwnerData)
					{
						ClearOwnerData(n, 0, n.count);
					}
					n.arr = null;
				}
			}
			n.count -= count;
		}

		private void Array_InsertRange(Node dest, int destIndex, Node src, int srcIndex, int count)
		{
			if (dest.arr != null || (src != null && src.arr != null))
			{
				object[] array = Expand_Collapse(dest, count + dest.count);
				if (array != dest.arr && destIndex > 0 && array != null)
				{
					Array.Copy(array, 0, dest.arr, 0, destIndex);
				}
				if (destIndex < dest.count && array != null)
				{
					Array.Copy(array, destIndex, dest.arr, destIndex + count, dest.count - destIndex);
				}
				if (src != null && src.arr != null)
				{
					Array.Copy(src.arr, srcIndex, dest.arr, destIndex, count);
					SetOwnerDataHelper(dest, destIndex, count);
				}
				else if (array == dest.arr)
				{
					Array.Clear(array, destIndex, count);
				}
			}
			dest.count += count;
		}

		private void Lend(Node nodeFrom, Node nodeTo, int itemsToLend, bool toLeft)
		{
			if (nodeTo.count == 0 && nodeFrom != null && itemsToLend == nodeFrom.count)
			{
				nodeTo.arr = nodeFrom.arr;
				nodeFrom.arr = null;
				nodeTo.count = nodeFrom.count;
				nodeFrom.count = 0;
				SetOwnerDataHelper(nodeTo, 0, nodeTo.count);
			}
			else if (toLeft)
			{
				Array_InsertRange(nodeTo, 0, nodeFrom, (nodeFrom != null) ? (nodeFrom.count - itemsToLend) : 0, itemsToLend);
				if (nodeFrom != null)
				{
					Array_RemoveRange(nodeFrom, nodeFrom.count - itemsToLend, itemsToLend, clearOwnerData: false);
				}
			}
			else
			{
				Array_InsertRange(nodeTo, nodeTo.count, nodeFrom, 0, itemsToLend);
				if (nodeFrom != null)
				{
					Array_RemoveRange(nodeFrom, 0, itemsToLend, clearOwnerData: false);
				}
			}
		}

		private static Node GetMaximum(Node n)
		{
			if (n != null)
			{
				while (n.right != null)
				{
					n = n.right;
				}
			}
			return n;
		}

		private static Node GetMinimum(Node n)
		{
			if (n != null)
			{
				while (n.left != null)
				{
					n = n.left;
				}
			}
			return n;
		}

		private static Node GetSuccessor(Node n)
		{
			if (n.right != null)
			{
				return GetMinimum(n.right);
			}
			while (n.parent != null)
			{
				if (n == n.parent.left)
				{
					return n.parent;
				}
				n = n.parent;
			}
			return null;
		}

		private void FixupNode(Node n)
		{
			while (n != null && n.count < factor)
			{
				Node node = null;
				int num = factor - n.count;
				int num2 = num;
				if (n.parent == null && n.leftCount == 0 && n.rightCount == 0)
				{
					break;
				}
				if (n.parent != null && ((n == n.parent.left && n.rightCount == 0) || (n == n.parent.right && n.leftCount == 0)))
				{
					if (CanBorrow(n.parent, num))
					{
						Lend(n.parent, n, num, n != n.parent.left);
						if (n == n.parent.left)
						{
							n.parent.leftCount += num;
						}
						else
						{
							n.parent.rightCount += num;
						}
						DirtyScrollCount(n);
					}
					else
					{
						int count = n.count;
						if (count > 0)
						{
							Lend(n, n.parent, count, n.parent.left == n);
						}
						if (n == n.parent.left)
						{
							n.parent.leftCount -= count;
							n.parent.left = n.left;
							if (n.left != null)
							{
								n.left.parent = n.parent;
							}
						}
						else
						{
							n.parent.rightCount -= count;
							n.parent.right = n.right;
							if (n.right != null)
							{
								n.right.parent = n.parent;
							}
						}
					}
					node = n.parent;
					n = null;
				}
				else if (n.leftCount > n.rightCount)
				{
					if (n.left == null)
					{
						int count = Math.Min(n.leftCount, num);
						Lend(null, n, count, toLeft: true);
						num -= count;
						n.leftCount -= count;
						node = n;
					}
					else
					{
						Node node2 = GetMaximum(n.left);
						int count;
						if (node2.rightCount > 0)
						{
							count = Math.Min(node2.rightCount, num);
							Lend(null, n, count, toLeft: true);
							node2.rightCount -= count;
							num -= count;
						}
						if (num > 0)
						{
							count = (CanBorrow(node2, num) ? num : node2.count);
							Lend(node2, n, count, toLeft: true);
							num -= count;
						}
						node = node2;
						if (node2.count == 0)
						{
							if (node2 == node2.parent.left)
							{
								node2.parent.left = node2.left;
							}
							else
							{
								node2.parent.right = node2.left;
							}
							if (node2.left != null)
							{
								node2.left.parent = node2.parent;
							}
							node = node2.parent;
						}
						count = num2 - num;
						n.leftCount -= count;
						while (node2.parent != n)
						{
							node2 = node2.parent;
							node2.rightCount -= count;
						}
					}
				}
				else if (n.right == null)
				{
					int count = Math.Min(n.rightCount, num);
					Lend(null, n, count, toLeft: false);
					num -= count;
					n.rightCount -= count;
					node = n;
				}
				else
				{
					Node node3 = GetMinimum(n.right);
					int count;
					if (node3.leftCount > 0)
					{
						count = Math.Min(node3.leftCount, num);
						Lend(null, n, count, toLeft: false);
						node3.leftCount -= count;
						num -= count;
					}
					if (num > 0)
					{
						count = (CanBorrow(node3, num) ? num : node3.count);
						Lend(node3, n, count, toLeft: false);
						num -= count;
					}
					node = node3;
					if (node3.count == 0)
					{
						if (node3 == node3.parent.left)
						{
							node3.parent.left = node3.right;
						}
						else
						{
							node3.parent.right = node3.right;
						}
						if (node3.right != null)
						{
							node3.right.parent = node3.parent;
						}
						node = node3.parent;
					}
					count = num2 - num;
					n.rightCount -= count;
					while (node3.parent != n)
					{
						node3 = node3.parent;
						node3.leftCount -= count;
					}
				}
				if (node != null)
				{
					DirtyScrollCount(node);
					if (n != null && node != n)
					{
						DirtyScrollCount(n);
					}
					MaintainAncestors(node);
				}
			}
			while (n != null && n.count > MaxFactor)
			{
				Node node4 = CreateNode();
				bool flag = n.leftCount < n.rightCount;
				Lend(n, node4, factor, !flag);
				if (flag)
				{
					n.leftCount += node4.count;
					if (n.left == null)
					{
						n.left = node4;
						node4.parent = n;
					}
					else
					{
						Node node5 = n.left;
						node5.rightCount += node4.count;
						while (node5.right != null)
						{
							node5 = node5.right;
							node5.rightCount += node4.count;
						}
						node5.right = node4;
						node4.parent = node5;
					}
					if (node4 == node4.parent.left)
					{
						node4.leftCount = node4.parent.leftCount - node4.count;
					}
					else
					{
						node4.leftCount = node4.parent.rightCount - node4.count;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					n.rightCount += node4.count;
					if (n.right == null)
					{
						n.right = node4;
						node4.parent = n;
					}
					else
					{
						Node node6 = n.right;
						node6.leftCount += node4.count;
						while (node6.left != null)
						{
							node6 = node6.left;
							node6.leftCount += node4.count;
						}
						node6.left = node4;
						node4.parent = node6;
					}
					if (node4 == node4.parent.right)
					{
						node4.rightCount = node4.parent.rightCount - node4.count;
					}
					else
					{
						node4.rightCount = node4.parent.leftCount - node4.count;
					}
				}
				DirtyScrollCount(n);
				DirtyScrollCount(node4);
				if (node4.parent != null)
				{
					MaintainAncestors(node4.parent);
				}
			}
		}

		private Node RotateLeft(Node p)
		{
			Node right = p.right;
			if (right != null)
			{
				if (p == tree)
				{
					tree = right;
				}
				else if (p == p.parent.left)
				{
					p.parent.left = right;
				}
				else if (p == p.parent.right)
				{
					p.parent.right = right;
				}
				right.parent = p.parent;
				p.parent = right;
				p.right = right.left;
				right.left = p;
				if (p.right != null)
				{
					p.right.parent = p;
				}
				p.rightCount = right.leftCount;
				right.leftCount = p.TotalCount;
			}
			return right;
		}

		private Node RotateRight(Node p)
		{
			Node left = p.left;
			if (left != null)
			{
				if (p == tree)
				{
					tree = left;
				}
				else if (p == p.parent.left)
				{
					p.parent.left = left;
				}
				else if (p == p.parent.right)
				{
					p.parent.right = left;
				}
				left.parent = p.parent;
				p.parent = left;
				p.left = left.right;
				left.right = p;
				if (p.left != null)
				{
					p.left.parent = p;
				}
				p.leftCount = left.rightCount;
				left.rightCount = p.TotalCount;
			}
			return left;
		}

		private void Maintain(Node p)
		{
			while (p != null)
			{
				Node node = null;
				if (p.rightCount > p.leftCount && p.right != null)
				{
					int num = p.leftCount + p.count + p.right.leftCount;
					int rightCount = p.right.rightCount;
					if (Math.Abs(rightCount - num) < p.rightCount - p.leftCount)
					{
						node = RotateLeft(p);
					}
					else if (factor + p.rightCount >= (factor + p.leftCount) * 4)
					{
						Node right = p.right;
						if (RotateRight(right) != null)
						{
							DirtyScrollCount(right, setIsNodeDirtyFlag: false, overallScrollCountChanged: false);
							node = RotateLeft(p);
						}
					}
				}
				if (p.leftCount > p.rightCount && node == null && p.left != null)
				{
					int num = p.left.leftCount;
					int rightCount = p.count + p.rightCount + p.left.rightCount;
