			new TypeListBoxEntry(typeof(decimal), "TypeDesc_Decimal"),
			new TypeListBoxEntry(typeof(DateTime), "TypeDesc_DateTime"),
			new TypeListBoxEntry(typeof(double), "TypeDesc_Double"),
			new TypeListBoxEntry(typeof(float), "TypeDesc_Single"),
			new TypeListBoxEntry(typeof(string), "TypeDesc_String"),
			new TypeListBoxEntry(typeof(DateTimeIncrement), "TypeDesc_DateTimeIncrement")
		};

		private IWindowsFormsEditorService edSvc;

		private bool designMode;

		[PermissionSet(SecurityAction.LinkDemand, Name = "linkcheck", Unrestricted = true)]
		public override object EditValue(ITypeDescriptorContext context, IServiceProvider provider, object value)
		{
			if (context != null && context.Instance != null && provider != null)
			{
				IDesignerHost designerHost = (IDesignerHost)provider.GetService(typeof(IDesignerHost));
				if (designerHost != null && designerHost.RootComponent != null && designerHost.RootComponent.Site != null)
				{
					designMode = designerHost.RootComponent.Site.DesignMode;
				}
				edSvc = (IWindowsFormsEditorService)provider.GetService(typeof(IWindowsFormsEditorService));
				if (edSvc != null)
				{
					TypeListBoxEntry currentEntry = null;
					DropDownListBox dropDownListBox = CreateListBox(value, ref currentEntry);
					edSvc.DropDownControl(dropDownListBox);
					if (dropDownListBox.SelectedItem != null && dropDownListBox.WasItemClicked && dropDownListBox.SelectedItem is TypeListBoxEntry typeListBoxEntry)
					{
						if (typeListBoxEntry.Type == null)
						{
							value = null;
						}
						else if (typeListBoxEntry.Type == typeof(DBNull))
						{
							value = DBNull.Value;
						}
						else if (value == null || typeListBoxEntry.Type != value.GetType())
						{
							bool flag = true;
							if (value != null && typeListBoxEntry.Type != typeof(char))
							{
								try
								{
									if (typeListBoxEntry.Type == typeof(double) && value.GetType() == typeof(float))
									{
										value = Utils.ConvertValue(value, typeof(string), useCurrentCulture: true);
									}
									value = Utils.ConvertValue(value, typeListBoxEntry.Type, useCurrentCulture: true);
									if (value != null && value.GetType() == typeListBoxEntry.Type)
									{
										flag = false;
									}
								}
								catch
								{
								}
							}
							if (flag)
							{
								value = GetDefaultValueForType(typeListBoxEntry.Type);
							}
						}
					}
				}
			}
			return value;
		}

		[PermissionSet(SecurityAction.LinkDemand, Name = "linkcheck", Unrestricted = true)]
		public override UITypeEditorEditStyle GetEditStyle(ITypeDescriptorContext context)
		{
			return UITypeEditorEditStyle.DropDown;
		}

		[PermissionSet(SecurityAction.LinkDemand, Name = "linkcheck", Unrestricted = true)]
		public override bool GetPaintValueSupported(ITypeDescriptorContext context)
		{
			return context != null;
		}

		[PermissionSet(SecurityAction.LinkDemand, Name = "linkcheck", Unrestricted = true)]
		public override void PaintValue(PaintValueEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.Value == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			TypeListBoxEntry typeListBoxEntry = e.Value as TypeListBoxEntry;
			if (typeListBoxEntry == null)
			{
				Type type = e.Value.GetType();
				TypeListBoxEntry[] array = s_primitiveTypes;
				foreach (TypeListBoxEntry typeListBoxEntry2 in array)
				{
					if (type == typeListBoxEntry2.Type)
					{
						typeListBoxEntry = typeListBoxEntry2;
						break;
					}
				}
				if (typeListBoxEntry == null)
				{
					return;
				}
			}
			PaintIndicator(typeListBoxEntry, e.Bounds, e.Graphics);
		}

		private DropDownListBox CreateListBox(object value, ref TypeListBoxEntry currentEntry)
		{
			DropDownListBox dropDownListBox = null;
			object[] listItems = GetListItems(value, ref currentEntry);
			dropDownListBox = new DropDownListBox(listItems, currentEntry, sorted: false);
			dropDownListBox.Click += OnListClick;
			dropDownListBox.DrawItem += OnDrawItem;
			dropDownListBox.DrawMode = DrawMode.OwnerDrawFixed;
			InitializeListBox(dropDownListBox);
			return dropDownListBox;
		}

		internal static object GetDefaultValueForType(Type type)
		{
			object result = null;
			if (type == typeof(DBNull))
			{
				result = DBNull.Value;
			}
			else if (type == typeof(string))
			{
				result = string.Empty;
			}
			else if (type == typeof(int))
			{
				result = 0;
			}
			else if (type == typeof(bool))
			{
				result = false;
			}
			else if (type == typeof(DateTime))
			{
				result = DateTime.MinValue;
			}
			else if (type == typeof(decimal))
			{
				result = 0m;
			}
			else if (type == typeof(double))
			{
				result = 0.0;
			}
			else if (type == typeof(float))
			{
				result = 0f;
			}
			else if (type == typeof(byte))
			{
				result = (byte)0;
			}
			else if (type == typeof(char))
			{
				result = 'a';
			}
			else if (type == typeof(short))
			{
				result = (short)0;
			}
			else if (type == typeof(long))
			{
				result = 0L;
			}
			else if (type == typeof(ushort))
			{
				result = (ushort)0;
			}
			else if (type == typeof(uint))
			{
				result = 0u;
			}
			else if (type == typeof(ulong))
			{
				result = 0uL;
			}
			else if (type == typeof(sbyte))
			{
				result = (sbyte)0;
			}
			else if (type == typeof(DateTimeIncrement))
			{
				result = new DateTimeIncrement(0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0, 0);
			}
			return result;
		}

		private object[] GetListItems(object value, ref TypeListBoxEntry currentEntry)
		{
			Type type = null;
			if (value != null)
			{
				type = value.GetType();
			}
			ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList(s_primitiveTypes.Length);
			TypeListBoxEntry[] array = s_primitiveTypes;
			foreach (TypeListBoxEntry typeListBoxEntry in array)
			{
				if (IsTypeSupported(typeListBoxEntry.Type))
				{
					arrayList.Add(typeListBoxEntry);
					if (typeListBoxEntry.Type == type)
					{
						currentEntry = typeListBoxEntry;
					}
				}
			}
			return (object[])arrayList.ToArray(typeof(object));
		}

		protected virtual void InitializeListBox(ListBox listbox)
		{
			listbox.ItemHeight = 18;
		}

		protected virtual bool IsTypeSupported(Type type)
		{
			if (designMode && (type == typeof(ushort) || type == typeof(uint) || type == typeof(ulong) || type == typeof(sbyte)))
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		protected virtual void OnDrawItem(object sender, DrawItemEventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is ListBox listBox)
			{
				e.DrawBackground();
				TypeListBoxEntry typeListBoxEntry = (TypeListBoxEntry)listBox.Items[e.Index];
				Size size = new Size(20, e.Bounds.Height - 2);
				Rectangle rect = new Rectangle(e.Bounds.Location, size);
				rect.Offset(1, 1);
				PaintIndicator(typeListBoxEntry, rect, e.Graphics);
				StringFormat stringFormat = new StringFormat(StringFormatFlags.NoWrap);
				stringFormat.LineAlignment = StringAlignment.Center;
				stringFormat.Alignment = StringAlignment.Near;
				string s = typeListBoxEntry.ToString();
				Brush brush = new SolidBrush(e.ForeColor);
				Rectangle rectangle = new Rectangle(rect.Right + 1, e.Bounds.Top, e.Bounds.Width - (rect.Right + 1), e.Bounds.Height);
				e.Graphics.DrawString(s, e.Font, brush, rectangle, stringFormat);
				brush.Dispose();
				stringFormat.Dispose();
				if ((e.State & DrawItemState.Focus) != DrawItemState.None)
				{
					e.DrawFocusRectangle();
				}
			}
		}

		private void OnListClick(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (edSvc != null)
			{
				edSvc.CloseDropDown();
			}
		}

		private static void PaintIndicator(TypeListBoxEntry entry, Rectangle rect, Graphics g)
		{
			Image image = entry.Image;
			if (image != null)
			{
				GraphicsPath graphicsPath = new GraphicsPath();
				Rectangle rect2 = rect;
				rect2.Inflate(10, 7);
				graphicsPath.AddRectangle(rect2);
				PathGradientBrush pathGradientBrush = new PathGradientBrush(graphicsPath);
				pathGradientBrush.CenterColor = SystemColors.ControlLight;
				pathGradientBrush.SurroundColors = new Color[1] { SystemColors.ControlDark };
				pathGradientBrush.CenterPoint = new Point(rect.Left + rect.Width / 2, rect.Top + rect.Height / 2);
				g.FillRectangle(pathGradientBrush, rect);
				pathGradientBrush.Dispose();
				graphicsPath.Dispose();
				ColorMap[] array = new ColorMap[2]
				{
					new ColorMap(),
					null
				};
				array[0].NewColor = Color.Transparent;
				array[0].OldColor = Color.Magenta;
				array[1] = new ColorMap();
				array[1].NewColor = SystemColors.ControlText;
				array[1].OldColor = Color.Black;
				ImageAttributes imageAttributes = new ImageAttributes();
				imageAttributes.SetRemapTable(array);
				Rectangle destRect = new Rectangle(rect.Location, image.Size);
				if (rect.Width != destRect.Width)
				{
					destRect.X += (rect.Width - destRect.Width) / 2;
				}
				if (rect.Height != destRect.Height)
				{
					destRect.Y += (rect.Height - destRect.Height) / 2;
				}
				g.DrawImage(image, destRect, 0, 0, image.Width, image.Height, GraphicsUnit.Pixel, imageAttributes);
			}
		}
	}
	public sealed class PrimitiveTypeConverter : TypeConverter
	{
		public override bool CanConvertFrom(ITypeDescriptorContext context, Type sourceType)
		{
			if (sourceType == typeof(string))
			{
				return true;
			}
			return base.CanConvertFrom(context, sourceType);
		}

		public override bool CanConvertTo(ITypeDescriptorContext context, Type destinationType)
		{
			if (destinationType == typeof(string))
			{
				return true;
			}
			return base.CanConvertTo(context, destinationType);
		}

		public override object ConvertFrom(ITypeDescriptorContext context, CultureInfo culture, object value)
		{
			if (context == null || context.Instance == null || context.PropertyDescriptor == null)
			{
				return value;
			}
			object value2 = context.PropertyDescriptor.GetValue(context.Instance);
			if (value2 == null)
			{
				if (value == null)
				{
					return value;
				}
				if (value is string)
				{
					if (((string)value).Length < 1)
					{
						return value2;
					}
					return value;
				}
			}
			if (value2 == DBNull.Value)
			{
				if (value == null || value == DBNull.Value)
				{
					return value;
				}
				return value2;
			}
			if (value is string && ((string)value).Length < 1)
			{
				return PrimitiveTypeUITypeEditor.GetDefaultValueForType(value2.GetType());
			}
			try
			{
				return Utils.ConvertValue(value, value2.GetType(), culture);
			}
			catch
			{
				return value2;
			}
		}

		public override object ConvertTo(ITypeDescriptorContext context, CultureInfo culture, object value, Type destinationType)
		{
			if (destinationType == typeof(string))
			{
				if (value == null)
				{
					return string.Empty;
				}
				if (value == null || value == DBNull.Value)
				{
					return SR.GetString("DBNullLiteral");
				}
				try
				{
					return Utils.ConvertValue(value, destinationType, culture);
				}
				catch
				{
					return value.ToString();
				}
			}
			return base.ConvertTo(context, culture, value, destinationType);
		}
	}
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public sealed class ResourceCustomizer
	{
		private Hashtable customizedStrings;

		private int versionNumber;

		private Hashtable CustomizedStrings
		{
			get
			{
				if (customizedStrings == null)
				{
					customizedStrings = new Hashtable();
				}
				return customizedStrings;
			}
		}

		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		public int CustomizedStringsVersion => versionNumber;

		public string GetCustomizedString(string name)
		{
			if (CustomizedStrings.Contains(name))
			{
				return CustomizedStrings[name] as string;
			}
			return null;
		}

		public void ResetAllCustomizedStrings()
		{
			if (customizedStrings != null)
			{
				customizedStrings.Clear();
				versionNumber++;
			}
		}

		public void ResetCustomizedString(string name)
		{
			SetCustomizedString(name, null);
		}

		public void SetCustomizedString(string name, string customizedText)
		{
			if (CustomizedStrings.Contains(name))
			{
				CustomizedStrings.Remove(name);
			}
			if (customizedText != null)
			{
				CustomizedStrings.Add(name, customizedText);
			}
			versionNumber++;
		}
	}
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public sealed class Resources
	{
		public static ResourceCustomizer Customizer => ResourceCustomizerLocator.Customizer;

		private Resources()
		{
		}

		public static string GetString(string name, params object[] args)
		{
			return SR.GetString(name, args);
		}

		public static string GetString(string name)
		{
			return SR.GetString(name);
		}

		public static object GetObject(string name)
		{
			return SR.GetObject(name);
		}
	}
	public abstract class SparseCollectionBase : ICollection, IEnumerable
	{
		private class SparseCollectionArray : SparseArray
		{
			private SparseCollectionBase sparseCollection;

			internal SparseCollectionArray(SparseCollectionBase sparseCollection)
				: base(useOwnerData: true)
			{
				this.sparseCollection = sparseCollection;
			}

			protected override object GetOwnerData(object item)
			{
				return sparseCollection.GetOwnerData(item);
			}

			protected override void SetOwnerData(object item, object ownerData)
			{
				sparseCollection.SetOwnerData(item, ownerData);
			}
		}

		private SparseCollectionArray sparseArray;

		private Hashtable ownerDataTable;

		public virtual object SyncRoot => SparseArray.SyncRoot;

		public virtual bool IsSynchronized => SparseArray.IsSynchronized;

		public abstract bool IsReadOnly { get; }

		public virtual int Count => SparseArray.Count;

		[Browsable(false)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public virtual object[] All
		{
			get
			{
				object[] array = CreateArray();
				SparseArray.CopyTo(array, 0);
				return array;
			}
			set
			{
				if (IsReadOnly)
				{
					throw new NotSupportedException();
				}
				SparseArray.Clear();
				OnCleared();
				SparseArray.AddRange(value);
			}
		}

		private SparseArray SparseArray => sparseArray;

		private Hashtable OwnerDataTable
		{
			get
			{
				if (ownerDataTable == null)
				{
					ownerDataTable = new Hashtable();
				}
				return ownerDataTable;
			}
		}

		protected SparseCollectionBase()
		{
			sparseArray = new SparseCollectionArray(this);
		}

		public virtual object GetItem(int index)
		{
			return SparseArray[index];
		}

		public virtual void CopyTo(Array array, int index)
		{
			SparseArray.CopyTo(array, index);
		}

		public bool Contains(object obj)
		{
			return IndexOf(obj) >= 0;
		}

		public virtual int IndexOf(object obj)
		{
			return SparseArray.IndexOf(obj);
		}

		IEnumerator IEnumerable.GetEnumerator()
		{
			return SparseArray.GetEnumerator();
		}

		protected virtual object[] CreateArray()
		{
			return new object[Count];
		}

		protected int InternalAdd(object obj)
		{
			if (obj == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException(SR.GetString("LE_ArgumentNullException_16"));
			}
			return SparseArray.Add(obj);
		}

		protected void InternalInsert(int index, object obj)
		{
			if (obj == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException(SR.GetString("LE_ArgumentNullException_16"));
			}
			SparseArray.Insert(index, obj);
		}

		protected void InternalClear()
		{
			SparseArray.Clear();
			OnCleared();
		}

		protected void InternalRemove(object item)
		{
			SparseArray.Remove(item);
			OnItemRemoved(item);
		}

		protected void InternalRemove(int index)
		{
			object item = SparseArray[index];
			SparseArray.RemoveAt(index);
			OnItemRemoved(item);
		}

		protected void InternalAddRange(ICollection collection)
		{
			SparseArray.AddRange(collection);
		}

		protected void Sort(IComparer comparer)
		{
			SparseArray.Sort(comparer);
		}

		internal object GetOwnerData(object item)
		{
			return OwnerDataTable[item];
		}

		internal void SetOwnerData(object item, object ownerData)
		{
			OwnerDataTable[item] = ownerData;
		}

		private void OnItemRemoved(object item)
		{
			if (ownerDataTable != null && ownerDataTable.ContainsKey(item))
			{
				ownerDataTable.Remove(item);
			}
		}

		private void OnCleared()
		{
			if (ownerDataTable != null)
			{
				ownerDataTable.Clear();
				ownerDataTable = null;
			}
		}
	}
	public class PropChangeInfo
	{
		private PropChangeInfo trigger;

		private object source;

		private Enum propId;

		public PropChangeInfo Trigger => trigger;

		public object Source => source;

		public Enum PropId => propId;

		public PropChangeInfo(object source, Enum propId, PropChangeInfo trigger)
		{
			this.source = source;
			this.propId = propId;
			this.trigger = trigger;
		}

		public PropChangeInfo FindPropId(Enum propId)
		{
			if (this.propId.Equals(propId))
			{
				return this;
			}
			if (trigger != null)
			{
				return trigger.FindPropId(propId);
			}
			return null;
		}

		public object FindSource(Type type)
		{
			if (source.GetType() == type)
			{
				return source;
			}
			if (trigger != null)
			{
				return trigger.FindSource(type);
			}
			return null;
		}

		public override string ToString()
		{
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder();
			PropChangeInfo propChangeInfo = this;
			int num = 0;
			while (propChangeInfo != null)
			{
				if (num > 0)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append("\r\n");
					stringBuilder.Append(' ', num * 3);
				}
				stringBuilder.Append(propChangeInfo.Source.GetType().Name);
				stringBuilder.Append(".");
				stringBuilder.Append(propChangeInfo.propId.ToString());
				num++;
				propChangeInfo = propChangeInfo.Trigger;
			}
			return stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		public PropChangeInfo FindTrigger(object source)
		{
			if (source == this.source)
			{
				return this;
			}
			if (trigger != null)
			{
				return trigger.FindTrigger(source);
			}
			if (source == null)
			{
				return this;
			}
			return null;
		}
	}
	public delegate void SubObjectPropChangeEventHandler(PropChangeInfo propChange);
	public delegate void SubObjectDisposedEventHandler(SubObjectBase notifier);
	public abstract class SubObjectBase : DisposableObject
	{
		public enum NotifyId
		{
			Tag = 0x1000000,
			Key = 0x2000000
		}

		private SubObjectPropChangeEventHandler propChangedDelegate;

		private SubObjectDisposedEventHandler disposedDelegate;

		private SubObjectPropChangeEventHandler subObjectPropChangeHandler;

		protected object tagValue;

		protected const string TagSerializationName = "Tag";

		private string forceApplyResources;

		protected SubObjectPropChangeEventHandler SubObjectPropChangeHandler
		{
			get
			{
				if (subObjectPropChangeHandler == null)
				{
					subObjectPropChangeHandler = OnSubObjectPropChanged;
				}
				return subObjectPropChangeHandler;
			}
		}

		[LocalizedDescription("LD_SubObjectBase_P_Tag")]
		[Editor(typeof(PrimitiveTypeUITypeEditor), typeof(UITypeEditor))]
		[TypeConverter(typeof(PrimitiveTypeConverter))]
		public virtual object Tag
		{
			get
			{
				return tagValue;
			}
			set
			{
				tagValue = value;
				NotifyPropChange(NotifyId.Tag);
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public string ForceApplyResources
		{
			get
			{
				return forceApplyResources;
			}
			set
			{
				forceApplyResources = value;
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		public event SubObjectPropChangeEventHandler SubObjectPropChanged
		{
			add
			{
				if (propChangedDelegate == null)
				{
					propChangedDelegate = value;
				}
				else
				{
					propChangedDelegate = (SubObjectPropChangeEventHandler)Delegate.Combine(propChangedDelegate, value);
				}
			}
			remove
			{
				if (propChangedDelegate != null)
				{
					propChangedDelegate = (SubObjectPropChangeEventHandler)Delegate.Remove(propChangedDelegate, value);
				}
			}
		}

		[Browsable(false)]
		public event SubObjectDisposedEventHandler SubObjectDisposed
		{
			add
			{
				if (disposedDelegate == null)
				{
					disposedDelegate = value;
				}
				else
				{
					disposedDelegate = (SubObjectDisposedEventHandler)Delegate.Combine(disposedDelegate, value);
				}
			}
			remove
			{
				if (disposedDelegate != null)
				{
					disposedDelegate = (SubObjectDisposedEventHandler)Delegate.Remove(disposedDelegate, value);
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnSubObjectPropChanged(PropChangeInfo propChange)
		{
		}

		protected virtual void OnObjectPropChanged(PropChangeInfo propChange)
		{
		}

		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Advanced)]
		public void NotifyPropChange(Enum propId)
		{
			NotifyPropChange(new PropChangeInfo(this, propId, null));
		}

		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Advanced)]
		public void NotifyPropChange(Enum propId, PropChangeInfo trigger)
		{
			NotifyPropChange(new PropChangeInfo(this, propId, trigger));
		}

		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Advanced)]
		public void NotifyPropChange(PropChangeInfo trigger)
		{
			if (propChangedDelegate != null)
			{
				VerifyNotDisposed();
				propChangedDelegate(trigger);
			}
			OnObjectPropChanged(trigger);
		}

		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Advanced)]
		public bool ShouldSerializeTag()
		{
			if (tagValue == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (tagValue is string)
			{
				return ((string)tagValue).Length > 0;
			}
			return Utils.IsSafelySerializable(tagValue);
		}

		protected void ResetTag()
		{
			tagValue = null;
			NotifyPropChange(NotifyId.Tag);
		}

		protected void InitializeTag(object tagValue)
		{
			this.tagValue = tagValue;
		}

		protected override void OnDispose()
		{
			if (disposedDelegate != null)
			{
				disposedDelegate(this);
			}
			tagValue = null;
		}

		protected void SerializeTag(SerializationInfo info, string name)
		{
			if (ShouldSerializeTag())
			{
				Utils.SerializeObjectProperty(info, name, Tag);
			}
		}

		protected void SerializeTag(SerializationInfo info)
		{
			SerializeTag(info, "Tag");
		}

		protected void DeserializeTag(SerializationEntry entry)
		{
			DeserializeTagHelper(entry.Value);
		}

		protected void DeserializeTag(SerializationInfo info, string name)
		{
			object value = info.GetValue(name, typeof(object));
			DeserializeTagHelper(value);
		}

		protected void DeserializeTag(SerializationInfo info)
		{
			DeserializeTag(info, "Tag");
		}

		private void DeserializeTagHelper(object value)
		{
			tagValue = Utils.DeserializeObjectHelper(value);
		}
	}
	public abstract class SubObjectsCollectionBase : DisposableObjectCollectionBase
	{
		[Browsable(false)]
		[DesignerSerializationVisibility(DesignerSerializationVisibility.Hidden)]
		public virtual object[] All
		{
			get
			{
				object[] array = CreateArray();
				if (Count > 0)
				{
					List.CopyTo(array, 0);
				}
				return array;
			}
			set
			{
				if (IsReadOnly)
				{
					throw new NotSupportedException();
				}
				List.Clear();
				if (value != null && value.Length != 0)
				{
					if (List.Capacity < value.Length)
					{
						List.Capacity = value.Length;
					}
					for (int i = 0; i < value.Length; i++)
					{
						InternalAdd((SubObjectBase)value[i]);
					}
				}
			}
		}

		protected virtual object[] CreateArray()
		{
			return new object[Count];
		}

		protected int InternalAdd(SubObjectBase obj)
		{
			if (obj == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException(SR.GetString("LE_ArgumentNullException_16"));
			}
			return List.Add(obj);
		}

		protected void InternalInsert(int index, SubObjectBase obj)
		{
			if (obj == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException(SR.GetString("LE_ArgumentNullException_16"));
			}
			List.Insert(index, obj);
		}

		protected void InternalClear()
		{
			List.Clear();
		}

		protected void InternalRemove(SubObjectBase value)
		{
			List.Remove(value);
		}

		protected void InternalRemove(int index)
		{
			List.RemoveAt(index);
		}
	}

	[ComVisible(false)]
	[PermissionSet(SecurityAction.LinkDemand, Name = "linkcheck", Unrestricted = true)]
	[PermissionSet(SecurityAction.InheritanceDemand, Name = "linkcheck", Unrestricted = true)]
	public abstract class UITypeEditorListBase : UITypeEditor
	{
		private IWindowsFormsEditorService edSvc;

		[PermissionSet(SecurityAction.LinkDemand, Name = "linkcheck", Unrestricted = true)]
		public override object EditValue(ITypeDescriptorContext context, IServiceProvider provider, object value)
		{
			if (context != null && context.Instance != null && provider != null)
			{
				edSvc = (IWindowsFormsEditorService)provider.GetService(typeof(IWindowsFormsEditorService));
				if (edSvc != null)
				{
					object currentEntry = null;
					object[] listItems = GetListItems(context, provider, value, ref currentEntry);
					DropDownListBox dropDownListBox = CreateListBox(value, ref currentEntry, listItems);
					edSvc.DropDownControl(dropDownListBox);
					if (dropDownListBox.SelectedItem != null && dropDownListBox.WasItemClicked)
					{
						object selectedItem = dropDownListBox.SelectedItem;
						return ValidateEditItem(context, provider, value, selectedItem);
					}
				}
			}
			return value;
		}

		[PermissionSet(SecurityAction.LinkDemand, Name = "linkcheck", Unrestricted = true)]
		public override UITypeEditorEditStyle GetEditStyle(ITypeDescriptorContext context)
		{
			return UITypeEditorEditStyle.DropDown;
		}

		private DropDownListBox CreateListBox(object value, ref object currentEntry, object[] items)
		{
			DropDownListBox dropDownListBox = null;
			dropDownListBox = new DropDownListBox(items, currentEntry, sorted: false);
			dropDownListBox.Click += OnListClick;
			InitializeListBox(dropDownListBox);
			return dropDownListBox;
		}

		protected virtual void InitializeListBox(ListBox listbox)
		{
		}

		private void OnListClick(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (edSvc != null)
			{
				edSvc.CloseDropDown();
			}
		}

		protected abstract object[] GetListItems(ITypeDescriptorContext context, IServiceProvider provider, object value, ref object currentEntry);

		protected abstract object ValidateEditItem(ITypeDescriptorContext context, IServiceProvider provider, object value, object selectedEntry);
	}

	[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public class UltraLicense : License
	{
		private UltraLicenseInfo licenseInfo;

		public UltraProductInfoBase ProductInfo => licenseInfo.ProductInfo;

		public string ComponentName => licenseInfo.ComponentName;

		public override string LicenseKey
		{
			get
			{
				string key = LicenseKeyForDisplay;
				if (licenseInfo.Key == null)
				{
					AddLicenseOptionsTokensToKey(ref key, licenseInfo.LicenseOptions);
				}
				return key;
			}
		}

		public string LicenseKeyForDisplay => licenseInfo.Key ?? licenseInfo.ProductInfo.Key;

		public LicenseOptions LicenseOptions
		{
			get
			{
				if (licenseInfo.LicenseOptions == LicenseOptions.All)
				{
					return LicenseOptions.All;
				}
				UltraProductInfoBase productInfo = ProductInfo;
				if (productInfo.Status == LicenseStatus.BetaValid)
				{
					return LicenseOptions.All;
				}
				return licenseInfo.LicenseOptions;
			}
		}

		internal UltraLicenseInfo LicenseInfo => licenseInfo;

		internal UltraLicense(string componentName, UltraProductInfoBase productInfo, LicenseOptions licenseOptions)
			: this(new UltraLicenseInfo(componentName, productInfo, licenseOptions))
		{
		}

		internal UltraLicense(string componentName, UltraProductInfoBase productInfo, string key, LicenseOptions licenseOptions)
			: this(new UltraLicenseInfo(componentName, productInfo, key, licenseOptions))
		{
		}

		internal UltraLicense(UltraLicenseInfo licenseInfo)
		{
			this.licenseInfo = licenseInfo;
		}

		public override void Dispose()
		{
		}

		internal static void AddLicenseOptionsTokensToKey(ref string key, LicenseOptions licenseOptions)
		{
			if (key == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder(key);
			Array values = Enum.GetValues(typeof(LicenseOptions));
			string[] names = Enum.GetNames(typeof(LicenseOptions));
			for (int i = 0; i < values.Length; i++)
			{
				LicenseOptions licenseOptions2 = (LicenseOptions)values.GetValue(i);
				if (licenseOptions2 != LicenseOptions.None && licenseOptions2 != LicenseOptions.All && (licenseOptions & licenseOptions2) == licenseOptions2)
				{
					stringBuilder.Append("_");
					stringBuilder.Append(names[i]);
				}
			}
			key = stringBuilder.ToString();
		}

		internal static LicenseOptions ParseLicenseOptionsTokensFromKey(ref string key)
		{
			LicenseOptions licenseOptions = LicenseOptions.All;
			if (key != null)
			{
				licenseOptions = LicenseOptions.None;
				Array values = Enum.GetValues(typeof(LicenseOptions));
				string[] names = Enum.GetNames(typeof(LicenseOptions));
				for (int i = 0; i < values.Length; i++)
				{
					LicenseOptions licenseOptions2 = (LicenseOptions)values.GetValue(i);
					if (licenseOptions2 != LicenseOptions.None && licenseOptions2 != LicenseOptions.All)
					{
						string text = $"_{names[i]}";
						if (key.IndexOf(text) >= 0)
						{
							licenseOptions |= licenseOptions2;
							key = key.Replace(text, string.Empty);
						}
					}
				}
			}
			return licenseOptions;
		}
	}
	[Flags]
	public enum LicenseOptions
	{
		None = 0,
		[Obsolete("This constant has been deprecated; Office2007 user interface features are no longer bound by licensing.", false)]
		Office2007UI = 1,
		All = int.MaxValue
	}
	internal struct UltraLicenseInfo
	{
		public UltraProductInfoBase ProductInfo;

		public string Key;

		public string ComponentName;

		public LicenseOptions LicenseOptions;

		internal UltraLicenseInfo(string componentName, UltraProductInfoBase productInfo, LicenseOptions licenseOptions)
			: this(componentName, productInfo, null, licenseOptions)
		{
		}

		internal UltraLicenseInfo(string componentName, UltraProductInfoBase productInfo, string key, LicenseOptions licenseOptions)
		{
			ProductInfo = productInfo;
			ComponentName = componentName;
			Key = key;
			LicenseOptions = licenseOptions;
		}
	}
	[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
	[AttributeUsage(AttributeTargets.Class)]
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public abstract class UltraLicenseBaseAttribute : Attribute
	{
		private string componentName;

		private ArrayList products;

		public string ComponentName => componentName;

		internal UltraProductInfoBase[] AllProducts
		{
			get
			{
				if (products == null)
				{
					products = new ArrayList(3);
					LoadProductArray();
				}
				return (UltraProductInfoBase[])products.ToArray(typeof(UltraProductInfoBase));
			}
		}

		protected UltraLicenseBaseAttribute(string componentName)
		{
			if (!DisposableObject.HasSamePublicKey(GetType()))
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException(SR.GetString("LE_NotSupportedException_17"));
			}
			this.componentName = componentName;
		}

		public abstract UltraProductInfoBase[] GetLicensedWithProducts();

		private void LoadProductArray()
		{
			UltraProductInfoBase[] licensedWithProducts = GetLicensedWithProducts();
			if (licensedWithProducts == null || licensedWithProducts.Length == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			UltraProductInfoBase[] array = licensedWithProducts;
			foreach (UltraProductInfoBase ultraProductInfoBase in array)
			{
				if (!(ultraProductInfoBase is UltraProductInfo ultraProductInfo))
				{
					products.Add(ultraProductInfoBase);
					continue;
				}
				products.Add(ultraProductInfo);
				UltraProductInfoBase[] allNewerProducts = ultraProductInfo.AllNewerProducts;
				if (allNewerProducts != null && allNewerProducts.Length != 0)
				{
					products.AddRange(allNewerProducts);
				}
			}
		}
	}
	[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public sealed class UltraLicenseProvider : LicenseProvider
	{
		private static bool hasDisplayedRuntimeDemoMsg;

		[ThreadStatic]
		private static Dictionary<Type, UltraLicenseInfo> LicenseInfos;

		[ThreadStatic]
		private static Dictionary<string, UltraProductInfoBase> ValidRunTimeKeys;

		private static LicenseOptions LicenseOptions => LicenseOptions.All;

		public override License GetLicense(LicenseContext context, Type type, object instance, bool allowExceptions)
		{
			UltraLicense ultraLicense = null;
			if (context != null && context.UsageMode == LicenseUsageMode.Runtime)
			{
				if (LicenseInfos == null)
				{
					LicenseInfos = new Dictionary<Type, UltraLicenseInfo>();
				}
				if (LicenseInfos.TryGetValue(type, out var value))
				{
					ultraLicense = new UltraLicense(value);
				}
			}
			if (ultraLicense == null)
			{
				UltraLicenseBaseAttribute licenseAttribute = GetLicenseAttribute(type, instance, allowExceptions);
				if (licenseAttribute != null)
				{
					ultraLicense = new UltraLicense(new UltraLicenseInfo(licenseAttribute.ComponentName, new UnlicensedProductInfo(""), LicenseOptions.All));
					if (context != null && context.UsageMode == LicenseUsageMode.Runtime)
					{
						LicenseInfos[type] = ultraLicense.LicenseInfo;
					}
				}
			}
			return ultraLicense;
		}

		private static UltraLicenseBaseAttribute GetLicenseAttribute(Type type, object instance, bool allowExceptions)
		{
			if (!DisposableObject.HasSamePublicKey(type))
			{
				if (allowExceptions)
				{
					throw new LicenseException(type, instance, SR.GetString("LE_LicenseException_1"));
				}
				return null;
			}
			UltraLicenseBaseAttribute ultraLicenseBaseAttribute = null;
			object[] array = null;
			try
			{
				array = type.GetCustomAttributes(typeof(UltraLicenseBaseAttribute), inherit: false);
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			if (array != null)
			{
				for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
				{
					ultraLicenseBaseAttribute = array[i] as UltraLicenseBaseAttribute;
					if (ultraLicenseBaseAttribute != null)
					{
						break;
					}
				}
			}
			if (ultraLicenseBaseAttribute == null && allowExceptions)
			{
				throw new LicenseException(type, instance, SR.GetString("LE_LicenseException_2"));
			}
			return ultraLicenseBaseAttribute;
		}

		private static UltraLicense GetDesignTimeLicense(Type type, object instance, bool allowExceptions, UltraLicenseBaseAttribute licenseAttribute)
		{
			LicenseOptions licenseOptions = LicenseOptions;
			UltraProductInfoBase[] allProducts = licenseAttribute.AllProducts;
			if (allProducts == null || allProducts.Length < 1)
			{
				return null;
			}
			UltraProductInfoBase ultraProductInfoBase = null;
			for (int i = 0; i < allProducts.Length; i++)
			{
				if (ultraProductInfoBase == null || ultraProductInfoBase.Status < allProducts[i].Status)
				{
					ultraProductInfoBase = allProducts[i];
				}
				if (ultraProductInfoBase.Status == LicenseStatus.Licensed)
				{
					break;
				}
			}
			if (ultraProductInfoBase == null)
			{
				if (allowExceptions)
				{
					throw new LicenseException(type, instance, SR.GetString("LicenseExceptionUnlicensed"));
				}
			}
			else
			{
				switch (ultraProductInfoBase.Status)
				{
				case LicenseStatus.BetaValid:
				case LicenseStatus.TrialValid:
				case LicenseStatus.ActivationGracePeriodValid:
				case LicenseStatus.Licensed:
				{
					UltraProductInfo ultraProductInfo = ultraProductInfoBase as UltraProductInfo;
					if (ultraProductInfo != null && allowExceptions)
					{
						ultraProductInfo.DisplayPrompt();
					}
					return new UltraLicense(licenseAttribute.ComponentName, ultraProductInfoBase, licenseOptions);
				}
				case LicenseStatus.ActivationGracePeriodExpired:
					if (allowExceptions)
					{
						throw new LicenseException(type, instance, SR.GetString("LicenseExceptionGracePeriodExpired", ultraProductInfoBase.ProductName, ultraProductInfoBase.Expiration));
					}
					break;
				case LicenseStatus.BetaExpired:
					if (allowExceptions)
					{
						throw new LicenseException(type, instance, SR.GetString("LicenseExceptionBetaExpired", ultraProductInfoBase.ProductName, ultraProductInfoBase.Expiration));
					}
					break;
				case LicenseStatus.TrialExpired:
					if (allowExceptions)
					{
						throw new LicenseException(type, instance, SR.GetString("LicenseExceptionTrialExpired", ultraProductInfoBase.ProductName, ultraProductInfoBase.Expiration));
					}
					break;
				case LicenseStatus.CDKeyValid:
					if (allowExceptions)
					{
						throw new LicenseException(type, instance, SR.GetString("LicenseExceptionIncomplete"));
					}
					break;
				case LicenseStatus.CDKeyInvalid:
					if (allowExceptions)
					{
						throw new LicenseException(type, instance, SR.GetString("LicenseExceptionInvalidCDKey"));
					}
					break;
				case LicenseStatus.RegKeyNotFound:
					if (allowExceptions)
					{
						throw new LicenseException(type, instance, SR.GetString("LicenseExceptionInformationNotFound"));
					}
					break;
				case LicenseStatus.InsufficientSecurityPermissions:
					if (allowExceptions)
					{
						throw new LicenseException(type, instance, SR.GetString("LicenseExceptionSecurityPermission"));
					}
					break;
				case LicenseStatus.UnableToLocateLicenseAssembly:
					if (allowExceptions)
					{
						throw new LicenseException(type, instance, SR.GetString("LE_LicenseException_3"));
					}
					break;
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		private static UltraLicense GetRunTimeLicense(string key, Type type, object instance, bool allowExceptions, UltraLicenseBaseAttribute licenseAttribute)
		{
			if (ValidRunTimeKeys == null)
			{
				ValidRunTimeKeys = new Dictionary<string, UltraProductInfoBase>();
			}
			LicenseOptions licenseOptions = UltraLicense.ParseLicenseOptionsTokensFromKey(ref key);
			if (key != null && ValidRunTimeKeys.TryGetValue(key, out var value))
			{
				return new UltraLicense(licenseAttribute.ComponentName, value, key, licenseOptions);
			}
			UltraProductInfoBase[] licensedWithProducts = licenseAttribute.GetLicensedWithProducts();
			if (licensedWithProducts == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (key == null)
			{
				return new UltraLicense(licenseAttribute.ComponentName, licensedWithProducts[0], string.Empty, licenseOptions);
			}
			bool flag = false;
			for (int i = 0; i < licensedWithProducts.Length; i++)
			{
				if (licensedWithProducts[i].Type == ProductType.WebFormsComponent)
				{
					flag = true;
					break;
				}
			}
			for (int j = 0; j < licensedWithProducts.Length; j++)
			{
				if (!licensedWithProducts[j].IsKeyValid(key))
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (!hasDisplayedRuntimeDemoMsg && !flag && allowExceptions && key == "{Trial}")
				{
					try
					{
						hasDisplayedRuntimeDemoMsg = true;
						string text = SR.GetString("LicenseExceptionUnlicensedProgram", licensedWithProducts[j].ProductName);
						string caption = SR.GetString("LicensedProductPromptDialogTitle", licensedWithProducts[j].ProductName);
						MessageBox.Show(text, caption, MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
					}
					catch
					{
					}
				}
				ValidRunTimeKeys[key] = licensedWithProducts[j];
				return new UltraLicense(licenseAttribute.ComponentName, licensedWithProducts[j], key, licenseOptions);
			}
			if (allowExceptions)
			{
				throw new LicenseException(type, instance, SR.GetString("LE_LicenseException_4"));
			}
			return null;
		}
	}

		private class DirtyObjectTypeDescriptorAdapter : CustomTypeDescriptorAdapter
		{
			internal DirtyObjectTypeDescriptorAdapter(object component)
				: base(component)
			{
			}

			protected override bool ShouldIncludeProperty(PropertyDescriptor propertyDescriptor, Attribute[] attributes)
			{
				if (propertyDescriptor.Attributes[typeof(SurrogatePropertySerializerAttribute)] is SurrogatePropertySerializerAttribute surrogatePropertySerializerAttribute)
				{
					PropertyDescriptor propertyDescriptor2 = TypeDescriptor.GetProperties(component)[surrogatePropertySerializerAttribute.PropertyName];
					if (propertyDescriptor2 != null && propertyDescriptor2.ShouldSerializeValue(component))
					{
						return true;
					}
				}
				return propertyDescriptor.ShouldSerializeValue(component);
			}

			protected override object WrapPropertyValue(object value)
			{
				if (value != null)
				{
					Type type = value.GetType();
					if (!type.IsPrimitive && !type.IsEnum)
					{
						return new DirtyObjectTypeDescriptorAdapter(value);
					}
				}
				return value;
			}
		}

			}
		}

		protected override void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (disposing)
			{
				if (propertyGridObject != null)
				{
					propertyGridObject.SelectedObject = null;
					propertyGridObject.SelectedObjects = null;
					propertyGridObject.ContextMenuStrip = null;
					propertyGridObject.PropertyValueChanged -= propertyGridObject_PropertyValueChanged;
				}
				if (menuItemReset != null)
				{
					menuItemReset.Click -= menuItemReset_Click;
					menuItemReset.Dispose();
					menuItemReset = null;
				}
				if (menuItemSeparator != null)
				{
					menuItemSeparator.Dispose();
					menuItemSeparator = null;
				}
				if (menuItemShowToolBar != null)
				{
					menuItemShowToolBar.Click -= menuItemShowToolBar_Click;
					menuItemShowToolBar.Dispose();
					menuItemShowToolBar = null;
				}
				if (menuItemShowDescription != null)
				{
					menuItemShowDescription.Click -= menuItemShowDescription_Click;
					menuItemShowDescription.Dispose();
					menuItemShowDescription = null;
				}
				if (PropertyGridContextMenu != null)
				{
					PropertyGridContextMenu.Dispose();
					PropertyGridContextMenu.Opened -= PropertyGridContextMenu_Popup;
					PropertyGridContextMenu = null;
				}
				if (components != null)
				{
					components.Dispose();
				}
			}
			base.Dispose(disposing);
		}

		private void menuItemShowToolBar_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			menuItemShowToolBar.Checked = !menuItemShowToolBar.Checked;
			propertyGridObject.ToolbarVisible = menuItemShowToolBar.Checked;

			propertyGridObject.HelpVisible = menuItemShowDescription.Checked;

		private void PropertyGridContextMenu_Popup(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			menuItemReset.Enabled = CanResetSelectedItems(propertyGridObject);
		}

		private void menuItemReset_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			try
			{
				propertyGridObject.ResetSelectedPropertyWithEvents();
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				string message = ex.Message;
				Exception ex2 = ex;
				while (ex2.InnerException != null)
				{
					message = ex2.InnerException.Message;
					ex2 = ex2.InnerException;
				}
				MessageBox.Show(FindForm(), message, SR.GetString("PropertySetErrorCaption"), MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Hand);
			}
		}

		private static bool CanResetSelectedItems(PropertyGrid propGrid)
		{
			if (propGrid == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (propGrid.SelectedObjects == null || propGrid.SelectedObjects.Length == 0 || propGrid.SelectedGridItem == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			object[] selectedObjects = propGrid.SelectedObjects;
			foreach (object component in selectedObjects)
			{
				try
				{
					GridItem selectedGridItem = propGrid.SelectedGridItem;
					if (selectedGridItem.Parent != null)
					{
						if (!selectedGridItem.PropertyDescriptor.CanResetValue(selectedGridItem.Parent.Value))
						{
							return false;
						}
					}
					else if (!selectedGridItem.PropertyDescriptor.CanResetValue(component))
					{
						return false;
					}
				}
				catch (InvalidCastException)
				{
					return false;
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
				}
			}
			return true;
		}

		private void InitializeProperties(object obj)
		{
			treeViewObjects.Nodes.Clear();
			baseObject = obj;
			if (obj == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			Type underlyingType = GetUnderlyingType(obj);
			TreeNode treeNode = null;
			if (includeBaseObject)
			{
				string name = underlyingType.Name;
				string className = TypeDescriptor.GetClassName(baseObject);
				if (className != underlyingType.FullName)
				{
					Type type = Type.GetType(className);
					if (type != null)
					{
						name = type.Name;
					}
				}
				treeNode = treeViewObjects.Nodes.Add(name);
				treeNode.Tag = obj;
			}
			AddPropertiesToTree((treeNode == null) ? treeViewObjects.Nodes : treeNode.Nodes, obj, null);
			if (treeViewObjects.Nodes.Count > 0)
			{
				treeViewObjects.SelectedNode = treeViewObjects.Nodes[0];
				treeViewObjects.Nodes[0].Expand();
			}
		}

		private void AddPropertiesToTree(TreeNodeCollection nodes, object obj, TypeConverter converter)
		{
			if (obj == null || nodes == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (converter == null)
			{
				converter = TypeDescriptor.GetConverter(obj);
			}
			if (converter == null || !converter.GetPropertiesSupported())
			{
				return;
			}
			PropertyDescriptorCollection properties = converter.GetProperties(obj);
			if (properties == null || properties.Count < 1)
			{
				return;
			}
			PropertyDescriptor[] array = new PropertyDescriptor[properties.Count];
			properties.CopyTo(array, 0);
			if (properties.Count > 1)
			{
				TypeDescriptor.SortDescriptorArray(array);
			}
			PropertyDescriptor[] array2 = array;
			foreach (PropertyDescriptor propertyDescriptor in array2)
			{
				TreeNode treeNode = null;
				try
				{
					if (!IsUsableType(propertyDescriptor.PropertyType))
					{
						continue;
					}
					object value = propertyDescriptor.GetValue(obj);
					object underlyingObject = GetUnderlyingObject(value);
					if (underlyingObject == null || (underlyingObject is ICollection && ((ICollection)underlyingObject).Count == 0))
					{
						continue;
					}
					string className = TypeDescriptor.GetClassName(underlyingObject);
					if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(className) && className != underlyingObject.GetType().FullName)
					{
						Type type = Type.GetType(className);
						if (!(type != null) || IsUsableType(type))
						{
							goto IL_010b;
						}
					}
					else if (!underlyingObject.GetType().IsValueType)
					{
						goto IL_010b;
					}
					goto end_IL_006a;
					IL_010b:
					treeNode = new TreeNode(propertyDescriptor.DisplayName);
					nodes.Add(treeNode);
					treeNode.Tag = value;
					TypeConverter converter2 = propertyDescriptor.Converter;
					if (converter2 == null)
					{
						converter2 = TypeDescriptor.GetConverter(value);
					}
					if (converter2 == null || converter2.GetPropertiesSupported())
					{
						if (!IsRecursive(treeNode, value))
						{
							AddPropertiesToTree(treeNode.Nodes, value, converter2);
						}
						continue;
					}
					value = GetUnderlyingObject(value);
					IEnumerable enumerable = value as IEnumerable;
					if (enumerable != null)
					{
						goto IL_01d7;
					}
					PropertyDescriptor propertyDescriptor2 = TypeDescriptor.GetProperties(value)["All"];
					if (propertyDescriptor2 == null)
					{
						goto IL_01d7;
					}
					object value2 = propertyDescriptor2.GetValue(value);
					enumerable = value2 as IEnumerable;
					if (!(enumerable is ICollection) || ((ICollection)enumerable).Count != 0)
					{
						goto IL_01d7;
					}
					nodes.Remove(treeNode);
					goto end_IL_006a;
					IL_01d7:
					if (enumerable == null)
					{
						continue;
					}
					int num = 0;
					foreach (object item in enumerable)
					{
						if (item == null || !IsUsableType(item.GetType()))
						{
							continue;
						}
						string text = num.ToString();
						StringBuilder stringBuilder = new StringBuilder(3);
						stringBuilder.Append(text);
						string text2 = item.ToString();
						TypeConverter converter3 = TypeDescriptor.GetConverter(item);
						if (converter3 != null && converter3.CanConvertTo(typeof(string)))
						{
							try
							{
								text2 = converter3.ConvertToString(item);
							}
							catch
							{
							}
						}
						if (text2.Length > 0 && text2 != text)
						{
							stringBuilder.Append(" - ");
							stringBuilder.Append(text2);
						}
						TreeNode treeNode2 = new TreeNode(stringBuilder.ToString());
						object obj3 = item;
						if (toolBarButtonDirty.Checked)
						{
							obj3 = new DirtyObjectTypeDescriptorAdapter(obj3);
						}
						treeNode2.Tag = obj3;
						treeNode.Nodes.Add(treeNode2);
						if (!IsRecursive(treeNode2, obj3))
						{
							AddPropertiesToTree(treeNode2.Nodes, obj3, null);
						}
						num++;
					}
					if (treeNode.Nodes.Count == 0)
					{
						treeNode.Remove();
					}
					end_IL_006a:;
				}
				catch (Exception)
				{
					treeNode?.Remove();
				}
			}
		}

		private static bool IsUsableType(Type propType)
		{
			if (!propType.IsClass || propType.IsValueType)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (propType.IsSubclassOf(typeof(Component)))
			{
				if (IsTypeOf("System.Web.UI.WebControls.Style", propType))
				{
					return true;
				}
				return false;
			}
			if (propType == typeof(string) || propType == typeof(ControlBindingsCollection) || propType == typeof(Image) || propType == typeof(Bitmap) || propType == typeof(Icon) || propType == typeof(Cursor) || propType == typeof(object) || propType == typeof(Type) || propType == typeof(Font))
			{
				return false;
			}
			switch (propType.FullName)
			{
			case "Infragistics.Win.ImageBackgroundStretchMargins":
			case "Infragistics.Win.FontData":
			case "Microsoft.VisualStudio.Configuration.ComponentSettings":
			case "Microsoft.VisualStudio.Windows.Forms.ConfigBindings":
				return false;
			default:
				if (propType.IsGenericType)
				{
					Type genericTypeDefinition = propType.GetGenericTypeDefinition();
					string fullName = genericTypeDefinition.FullName;
					if (fullName == "Microsoft.DotNet.DesignTools.Client.Proxies.EnumProxy`1")
					{
						return false;
					}
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		private static bool IsTypeOf(string fullTypeName, Type type)
		{
			if (type.FullName == fullTypeName)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (type.BaseType == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return IsTypeOf(fullTypeName, type.BaseType);
		}

		private static bool IsRecursive(TreeNode node, object objValue)
		{
			if (node == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (objValue == null)
			{
				return true;
			}
			for (TreeNode treeNode = node.Parent; treeNode != null; treeNode = treeNode.Parent)
			{
				if (treeNode.Tag.Equals(objValue))
				{
					return true;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		private void treeViewObjects_AfterSelect(object sender, TreeViewEventArgs e)
		{
			object tag = e.Node.Tag;
			object underlyingObject = GetUnderlyingObject(tag);
			if (underlyingObject != null && underlyingObject is IEnumerable && (TypeDescriptor.GetConverter(underlyingObject) == null || !TypeDescriptor.GetConverter(underlyingObject).GetPropertiesSupported()))
			{
				underlyingObject = null;
			}
			try
			{
				propertyGridObject.SelectedObject = tag;
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				propertyGridObject.SelectedObject = null;
			}
		}

		private void buttonCloseObjects_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			panelObjects.Visible = false;
			splitterVert.Visible = false;
			toolBarButtonObject.Checked = false;
		}

		private void toolBarMain_ButtonClick(object sender, ToolStripItemClickedEventArgs e)
		{
			if (e.ClickedItem == toolBarButtonObject)
			{
				bool flag = toolBarButtonObject.Checked;
				panelObjects.Visible = flag;
				splitterVert.Visible = flag;
				toolBarMain.SendToBack();
				if (flag)
				{
					if (treeViewObjects.SelectedNode != null)
					{
						propertyGridObject.SelectedObject = treeViewObjects.SelectedNode.Tag;
					}
					else
					{
						propertyGridObject.SelectedObject = null;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					propertyGridObject.SelectedObject = baseObject;
				}
			}
			else if (e.ClickedItem == toolBarButtonRefresh)
			{
				InitializeProperties(baseObject);
			}
			else if (e.ClickedItem == toolBarButtonDirty)
			{
				toolBarButtonDirty.Checked = !toolBarButtonDirty.Checked;
				string[] currentNodePath = GetCurrentNodePath();
				bool flag2 = toolBarButtonDirty.Checked;
				object selectedObject = propertyGridObject.SelectedObject;
				object underlyingObject = GetUnderlyingObject(selectedObject);
				object underlyingObject2 = GetUnderlyingObject(baseObject);
				object selectedObject2 = ((!flag2) ? underlyingObject : new DirtyObjectTypeDescriptorAdapter(underlyingObject));
				object obj = ((!flag2) ? underlyingObject2 : new DirtyObjectTypeDescriptorAdapter(underlyingObject2));
				treeViewObjects.BeginUpdate();
				try
				{
					propertyGridObject.SelectedObject = selectedObject2;
					InitializeProperties(obj);
					SelectClosestNodeFromPath(currentNodePath);
				}
				finally
				{
					treeViewObjects.EndUpdate();
				}
			}
		}

		private string[] GetCurrentNodePath()
		{
			TreeNode selectedNode = treeViewObjects.SelectedNode;
			TreeNode treeNode = selectedNode;
			int num = 0;
			while (treeNode != null)
			{
				num++;
				treeNode = treeNode.Parent;
			}
			string[] array = new string[num];
			for (int num2 = num - 1; num2 >= 0; num2--)
			{
				array[num2] = selectedNode.Text;
				selectedNode = selectedNode.Parent;
			}
			return array;
		}

		private void SelectClosestNodeFromPath(string[] nodeTexts)
		{
			if (nodeTexts == null || nodeTexts.Length == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			TreeNodeCollection nodes = treeViewObjects.Nodes;
			TreeNode treeNode = null;
			foreach (string text in nodeTexts)
			{
				TreeNode treeNode2 = null;
				foreach (TreeNode item in nodes)
				{
					if (item.Text == text)
					{
						treeNode2 = item;
						break;
					}
				}
				if (treeNode2 == null)
				{
					break;
				}
				treeNode = treeNode2;
				nodes = treeNode2.Nodes;
			}
			if (treeNode != null)
			{
				treeNode.EnsureVisible();
				treeNode.TreeView.SelectedNode = treeNode;
			}
		}

		private static object GetUnderlyingObject(object obj)
		{
			if (obj is DirtyObjectTypeDescriptorAdapter)
			{
				return ((DirtyObjectTypeDescriptorAdapter)obj).Component;
			}
			return obj;
		}

		private static Type GetUnderlyingType(object obj)
		{
			if (obj is DirtyObjectTypeDescriptorAdapter)
			{
				return ((DirtyObjectTypeDescriptorAdapter)obj).Component.GetType();
			}
			return obj.GetType();
		}

		public override void ApplyChanges()
		{
			Clean();
		}

		public override void InitializeControl(object baseObject)
		{
			propertyGridObject.Initialize(baseObject as IComponent);
			propertyGridObject.SelectedObject = baseObject;
			propertyGridObject.PropertyValueChanged -= propertyGridObject_PropertyValueChanged;
			propertyGridObject.PropertyValueChanged += propertyGridObject_PropertyValueChanged;
			string[] nodeTexts = null;
			object objB = (toolBarButtonDirty.Checked ? new DirtyObjectTypeDescriptorAdapter(baseObject) : baseObject);
			bool flag = object.Equals(this.baseObject, objB);
			if (flag)
			{
				nodeTexts = GetCurrentNodePath();
			}
			try
			{
				treeViewObjects.BeginUpdate();
				InitializeProperties(baseObject);
			}
			finally
			{
				if (flag)
				{
					SelectClosestNodeFromPath(nodeTexts);
				}
				treeViewObjects.EndUpdate();
			}
		}

		private void SaveSettingsToRegistry()
		{
			RegistryKey registryKey = null;
			try
			{
				registryKey = Registry.CurrentUser.CreateSubKey("Software\\Infragistics\\Shared\\CustomPropertyPage");
				if (registryKey != null)
				{
					registryKey.SetValue("SplitPosition", splitterVert.SplitPosition);
					registryKey.SetValue("ObjectTree", toolBarButtonObject.Checked.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
					registryKey.SetValue("ShowToolbar", menuItemShowToolBar.Checked);
					registryKey.SetValue("ShowDescription", menuItemShowDescription.Checked);
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				registryKey?.Close();
			}
		}

		private static bool GetBoolRegValue(RegistryKey key, string name, bool defaultValue)
		{
			return (bool)Utils.ConvertValue(key.GetValue(name, defaultValue.ToString(CultureInfo.InvariantCulture)), typeof(bool), defaultValue);
		}

		private void ApplySettingsFromRegistry()
		{
			RegistryKey registryKey = null;
			try
			{
				registryKey = Registry.CurrentUser.OpenSubKey("Software\\Infragistics\\Shared\\CustomPropertyPage");
				if (registryKey != null)
				{
					int splitPosition = Convert.ToInt32(registryKey.GetValue("SplitPosition", splitterVert.SplitPosition));
					splitterVert.SplitPosition = splitPosition;
					if (!GetBoolRegValue(registryKey, "ObjectTree", defaultValue: false))
					{
						panelObjects.Visible = false;
						splitterVert.Visible = false;
						toolBarButtonObject.Checked = false;
						toolBarMain.SendToBack();
					}
					bool boolRegValue = GetBoolRegValue(registryKey, "ShowToolbar", defaultValue: true);
					bool boolRegValue2 = GetBoolRegValue(registryKey, "ShowDescription", defaultValue: false);
					UltraPropPagePropertyGrid ultraPropPagePropertyGrid = propertyGridObject;
					bool helpVisible = (menuItemShowDescription.Checked = boolRegValue2);
					ultraPropPagePropertyGrid.HelpVisible = helpVisible;
					UltraPropPagePropertyGrid ultraPropPagePropertyGrid2 = propertyGridObject;
					helpVisible = (menuItemShowToolBar.Checked = boolRegValue);
					ultraPropPagePropertyGrid2.ToolbarVisible = helpVisible;
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				registryKey?.Close();
			}
		}

		protected internal override void SaveState()
		{
			SaveSettingsToRegistry();
		}

		private void propertyGridObject_PropertyValueChanged(object sender, PropertyValueChangedEventArgs e)
		{
			Dirty();
		}

		public void RefreshProperties()
		{
			propertyGridObject.SelectedObject = null;
			if (baseObject != null)
			{
				InitializeProperties(baseObject);
				propertyGridObject.SelectedObject = baseObject;
			}
			else
			{
				treeViewObjects.Nodes.Clear();
			}
		}
	}

	public enum LicenseStatus
	{
		Unchecked,
		UnableToLocateLicenseAssembly,
		InsufficientSecurityPermissions,
		RegKeyNotFound,
		CDKeyInvalid,
		BetaExpired,
		BetaValid,
		TrialExpired,
		ActivationGracePeriodExpired,
		CDKeyValid,
		TrialValid,
		ActivationGracePeriodValid,
		Licensed
	}

	[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public abstract class UltraProductInfoBase
	{
		private string productName;

		private ProductType productType;

		public abstract string Expiration { get; }

		public abstract bool RequiresActivation { get; }

		public string ProductName => productName;

		public abstract bool Expired { get; }

		public abstract LicenseStatus Status { get; }

		public abstract string Key { get; }

		public abstract string CompanyName { get; }

		public abstract string UserName { get; }

		public ProductType Type => productType;

		internal UltraProductInfoBase(string productName)
		{
			this.productName = productName;
		}

		internal UltraProductInfoBase(string productName, ProductType productType)
		{
			this.productName = productName;
			this.productType = productType;
		}

		protected internal abstract bool IsKeyValid(string key);

		public string GetStatusText()
		{
			switch (Status)
			{
			case LicenseStatus.ActivationGracePeriodExpired:
				return SR.GetUncustomizedString("LDR_About_GraceExpired");
			case LicenseStatus.ActivationGracePeriodValid:
				return SR.GetUncustomizedString("LDR_About_InGrace");
			case LicenseStatus.BetaExpired:
				return SR.GetUncustomizedString("AboutDialogLicenseDescriptionBetaExpired");
			case LicenseStatus.BetaValid:
				return SR.GetUncustomizedString("AboutDialogLicenseDescriptionBeta");
			case LicenseStatus.TrialExpired:
				return SR.GetUncustomizedString("AboutDialogLicenseDescriptionTrialExpired");
			case LicenseStatus.TrialValid:
				return SR.GetUncustomizedString("AboutDialogLicenseDescriptionTrial");
			case LicenseStatus.CDKeyValid:
			case LicenseStatus.Licensed:
				return SR.GetUncustomizedString("AboutDialogLicenseDescriptionLicensed");
			case LicenseStatus.Unchecked:
			case LicenseStatus.RegKeyNotFound:
			case LicenseStatus.CDKeyInvalid:
				return SR.GetUncustomizedString("AboutDialogLicenseDescriptionUnknown");
			default:
				return string.Empty;
			}
		}

		public string GetKeyText()
		{
			string text = string.Empty;
			if (!string.IsNullOrEmpty(Key))
			{
				if (Key.Length != 18)
				{
					text = Key;
					if (text == "{Trial}")
					{
						text = SR.GetString("TrialKey_AboutDialogLabel");
					}
				}
				else
				{
					text = Key.Substring(0, 4) + "-" + Key.Substring(4, 7) + "-" + Key.Substring(11, 7);
				}
			}
			return text;
		}
	}

				return LicenseStatus.BetaValid;
			}
		}

		public override string Key => key;

		public override string CompanyName => string.Empty;

				nF3 = f3;

		}

		private Guid id;

		internal const string trialKeyText = "{Trial}";

		private const string errorKeyText = "{Error}";

		private const int SIZEOFCDKEY = 18;

		private static ArrayList verifiedProducts;

		private bool promptDisplayed;

		private int daysRemaining;

		private static CDMsk[] s_CDM = new CDMsk[9]
		{
			new CDMsk(123456L, '\t', '\v', '\r', '\u0011', 53L, 1782L),
			new CDMsk(734561L, '\t', '\v', '\u000e', '\u0011', 69L, 2772L),
			new CDMsk(217192L, '\t', '\v', '\u000f', '\u0011', 86L, 1771L),
			new CDMsk(152473L, '\t', '\f', '\u000e', '\u0011', 75L, 6177L),
			new CDMsk(368242L, '\t', '\f', '\u000f', '\u0011', 92L, 3255L),
			new CDMsk(476532L, '\t', '\r', '\u000f', '\u0011', 97L, 2552L),

			new CDMsk(49735L, '\n', '\f', '\u000f', '\u0011', 89L, 2251L),
			new CDMsk(901278L, '\v', '\r', '\u000f', '\u0011', 72L, 1552L)
		};

		private UltraProductInfo[] allNewerProducts;

		public override string Expiration
		{
			get
			{
				switch (Status)
				{
				case LicenseStatus.ActivationGracePeriodExpired:
					return SR.GetString("LDR_UltraProdInfo_Expiration1");
				case LicenseStatus.TrialExpired:
					return SR.GetString("LDR_UltraProdInfo_Expiration2");
				case LicenseStatus.BetaExpired:
					return SR.GetString("LDR_UltraProdInfo_Expiration3");
				case LicenseStatus.TrialValid:
				case LicenseStatus.ActivationGracePeriodValid:
					return SR.GetString("LDR_UltraProdInfo_Expiration4", daysRemaining, GetCalendarWeeksLeft());
				case LicenseStatus.BetaValid:
				case LicenseStatus.Licensed:
					return SR.GetString("LDR_UltraProdInfo_Expiration5");
				case LicenseStatus.CDKeyInvalid:
					return SR.GetString("LDR_UltraProdInfo_Expiration6");
				case LicenseStatus.CDKeyValid:
					return SR.GetString("LDR_UltraProdInfo_Expiration7");
				default:
					return SR.GetString("LDR_UltraProdInfo_Expiration8");
				case LicenseStatus.UnableToLocateLicenseAssembly:
				case LicenseStatus.RegKeyNotFound:
					return SR.GetString("LDR_UltraProdInfo_Expiration9");
				case LicenseStatus.InsufficientSecurityPermissions:
					return SR.GetString("LDR_UltraProdInfo_Expiration10");
				}
			}
		}

		public override bool RequiresActivation => requiresActivation;

		{
			get
			{
				if (status == LicenseStatus.Unchecked)
				{
					CheckLicenseStatus();
				}
				return status;

		public override string Key => cdkey;

		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		public string RegKeyProductName => regKeyProductName;

		internal UltraProductInfo[] AllNewerProducts
		{
			get
			{
				if (allNewerProducts == null)
				{
					LoadAllNewerProductInfo();

				return allNewerProducts;
