		public override bool Expired => DateTime.Today > expirationDate;

		public UltraProductInfo(string productName, string regKeyProductName, string codePrefix, Guid id, bool requiresActivation, byte majorVersion, byte minorVersion)
			: base(productName)
		{
			if (codePrefix == null || codePrefix.Length != 4)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException(SR.GetString("LE_ArgumentException_18"));
			}
			this.regKeyProductName = regKeyProductName;
			this.codePrefix = codePrefix;
			this.id = id;
			this.requiresActivation = requiresActivation;
			this.majorVersion = majorVersion;
			this.minorVersion = minorVersion;
		}

		public UltraProductInfo(string productName, string regKeyProductName, string codePrefix, Guid id, bool requiresActivation, ProductType productType, byte majorVersion, byte minorVersion)
			: base(productName, productType)
		{
			if (codePrefix == null || codePrefix.Length != 4)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException(SR.GetString("LE_ArgumentException_19"));
			}
			this.regKeyProductName = regKeyProductName;
			this.codePrefix = codePrefix;
			this.id = id;
			this.requiresActivation = requiresActivation;
			this.minorVersion = minorVersion;
			this.majorVersion = majorVersion;
		}

		private void CheckLicenseStatus()
		{
			if (status != LicenseStatus.Unchecked)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (CheckProductStatus(this, updateProductInfoIfFound: false) != LicenseStatus.Unchecked)
			{
				CheckProductStatus(this, updateProductInfoIfFound: true);
				return;
			}
			string empty = string.Empty;
			string licenseAssemblyInfo = GetLicenseAssemblyInfo();
			try
			{
				ObjectHandle objectHandle = Activator.CreateInstance(licenseAssemblyInfo, "Infragistics.License.LicenseValidator");
				object obj = objectHandle.Unwrap();
				Type type = obj.GetType();
				object[] array = new object[6] { regKeyProductName, majorVersion, minorVersion, empty, userName, companyName };
				MethodInfo method = type.GetMethod("GetRegistryInfo");
				object obj2 = method.Invoke(obj, array);
				if (obj2 == null || !(bool)obj2 || (string)array[3] == "{Error}")
				{
					UpdateProductStatus(LicenseStatus.RegKeyNotFound, DateTime.MinValue);
					return;
				}
				cdkey = array[3] as string;
				userName = array[4] as string;
				companyName = array[5] as string;
				if (cdkey == "{Trial}")
				{
					IsDemoValid(updateProductInfo: true);
				}
				else
				{
					IsLicenseValid(cdkey, updateProductInfo: true);
				}
			}
			catch (FileNotFoundException)
			{
				if (status == LicenseStatus.Unchecked)
				{
					UpdateProductStatus(LicenseStatus.UnableToLocateLicenseAssembly, DateTime.MinValue);
				}
			}
			catch (SecurityException)
			{
				if (status == LicenseStatus.Unchecked)
				{
					UpdateProductStatus(LicenseStatus.InsufficientSecurityPermissions, DateTime.MinValue);
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				if (status == LicenseStatus.Unchecked)
				{
					UpdateProductStatus(LicenseStatus.RegKeyNotFound, DateTime.MinValue);
				}
			}
		}

		private static string GetLicenseAssemblyInfo()
		{
			string format = "Infragistics.License, Culture=\"\", Version={0}, PublicKeyToken=7dd5c3163f2cd0cb";
			RegistryKey registryKey2;
			RegistryKey registryKey = (registryKey2 = null);
			try
			{
				registryKey2 = Registry.ClassesRoot.OpenSubKey("Infragistics.License");
				registryKey = registryKey2.OpenSubKey("Version2");
				string arg = registryKey.GetValue(null) as string;
				return string.Format(format, arg);
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				return "Infragistics.License, Culture=\"\", PublicKeyToken=7dd5c3163f2cd0cb";
			}
			finally
			{
				registryKey?.Close();
				registryKey2?.Close();
			}
		}

		protected internal override bool IsKeyValid(string key)
		{
			if (key == "{Trial}")
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (key == null || key.Length == 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return IsLicenseValid(key, updateProductInfo: false);
		}

		private bool IsDemoValid(bool updateProductInfo)
		{
			string licenseAssemblyInfo = GetLicenseAssemblyInfo();
			object obj = null;
			try
			{
				ObjectHandle objectHandle = Activator.CreateInstance(licenseAssemblyInfo, "Infragistics.License.LicenseValidator");
				obj = objectHandle.Unwrap();
			}
			catch (FileNotFoundException)
			{
				if (updateProductInfo)
				{
					UpdateProductStatus(LicenseStatus.UnableToLocateLicenseAssembly, DateTime.MinValue);
				}
				return false;
			}
			catch (SecurityException)
			{
				if (updateProductInfo)
				{
					UpdateProductStatus(LicenseStatus.InsufficientSecurityPermissions, DateTime.MinValue);
				}
				return false;
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				if (updateProductInfo)
				{
					UpdateProductStatus(LicenseStatus.RegKeyNotFound, DateTime.MinValue);
				}
				return false;
			}
			try
			{
				Type type = obj.GetType();
				MethodInfo method = type.GetMethod("DaysRemainingBeforeTimeOut");
				object[] parameters = new object[5] { 30, regKeyProductName, majorVersion, minorVersion, id };
				try
				{
					object obj2 = method.Invoke(obj, parameters);
					int num = (daysRemaining = (int)obj2);
					if (updateProductInfo)
					{
						if (num >= 0)
						{
							UpdateProductStatus(LicenseStatus.TrialValid, GetExpirationDate(num));
						}
						else
						{
							UpdateProductStatus(LicenseStatus.TrialExpired, GetExpirationDate(num));
						}
					}
					return num >= 0;
				}
				catch (Exception ex4)
				{
					if (updateProductInfo)
					{
						Exception innerException = ex4.InnerException;
						SecurityException ex5 = innerException as SecurityException;
						if (ex5 == null && innerException != null)
						{
							ex5 = innerException.InnerException as SecurityException;
						}
						if (ex5 != null)
						{
							if (ex5.PermissionType == null || typeof(RegistryPermission).IsAssignableFrom(ex5.PermissionType))
							{
								daysRemaining = 30;
								UpdateProductStatus(LicenseStatus.TrialValid, GetExpirationDate(daysRemaining));
								return true;
							}
							UpdateProductStatus(LicenseStatus.InsufficientSecurityPermissions, DateTime.MinValue);
						}
						else
						{
							UpdateProductStatus(LicenseStatus.RegKeyNotFound, DateTime.MinValue);
						}
					}
					return false;
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
				return false;
			}
		}

		private void UpdateProductStatus(LicenseStatus newStatus, DateTime expirationDate)
		{
			status = newStatus;
			this.expirationDate = expirationDate;
			if (newStatus > LicenseStatus.RegKeyNotFound && CheckProductStatus(this, updateProductInfoIfFound: false) == LicenseStatus.Unchecked)
			{
				ProductVerified(this);
			}
		}

		private bool IsLicenseValid(string key, bool updateProductInfo)
		{
			if (!VerifyCheckSum(key, isInternal: false))
			{
				if (updateProductInfo)
				{
					UpdateProductStatus(LicenseStatus.CDKeyInvalid, DateTime.MinValue);
				}
				return false;
			}
			if (!updateProductInfo)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (updateProductInfo)
			{
				cdkey = key;
				UpdateProductStatus(LicenseStatus.Licensed, DateTime.MaxValue);
			}
			return true;
		}

		private static void ProductVerified(UltraProductInfo product)
		{
			if (product != null)
			{
				if (verifiedProducts == null)
				{
					verifiedProducts = new ArrayList();
				}
				verifiedProducts.Add(product);
			}
		}

		private static bool HasPromptBeenDisplayed(UltraProductInfo product)
		{
			if (verifiedProducts == null || product == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			foreach (UltraProductInfo verifiedProduct in verifiedProducts)
			{
				if (verifiedProduct.Equals(product))
				{
					return verifiedProduct.promptDisplayed;
				}
			}
			return false;
		}

		private static void UpdatePromptStatus(UltraProductInfo product, bool hasBeenDisplayed)
		{
			if (verifiedProducts == null || product == null)
			{
				return;
			}
			foreach (UltraProductInfo verifiedProduct in verifiedProducts)
			{
				if (verifiedProduct.Equals(product))
				{
					verifiedProduct.promptDisplayed = hasBeenDisplayed;
				}
			}
		}

		private static LicenseStatus CheckProductStatus(UltraProductInfo product, bool updateProductInfoIfFound)
		{
			if (verifiedProducts == null || product == null)
			{
				return LicenseStatus.Unchecked;
			}
			foreach (UltraProductInfo verifiedProduct in verifiedProducts)
			{
				if (!verifiedProduct.Equals(product))
				{
					continue;
				}
				if (updateProductInfoIfFound)
				{
					if (verifiedProduct.cdkey != null && verifiedProduct.cdkey.Length > 0)
					{
						product.cdkey = verifiedProduct.cdkey;
					}
					product.companyName = verifiedProduct.companyName;
					product.expirationDate = verifiedProduct.expirationDate;
					product.status = verifiedProduct.status;
					product.userName = verifiedProduct.userName;
					product.promptDisplayed = verifiedProduct.promptDisplayed;
					product.daysRemaining = verifiedProduct.daysRemaining;
				}
				return verifiedProduct.Status;
			}
			return LicenseStatus.Unchecked;
		}

		private bool VerifyCheckSum(string str, bool isInternal)
		{
			if (!isInternal && badCount > 6)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (str.Length != 18)
			{
				return false;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < str.Length; i++)
			{
				char c = str[i];
				if (c < '0' || (c > 'Z' && i > 0))
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
			if (!IsCDKeyPrefixValid(str))
			{
				if (!isInternal)
				{
					badCount++;
				}
				return false;
			}
			if (codePrefix.Substring(0, 2) != str.Substring(0, 2))
			{
				if (!isInternal)
				{
					badCount++;
				}
				return false;
			}
			if (codePrefix.Length >= 4 && str.Length >= 4 && char.IsNumber(codePrefix[2]) && char.IsNumber(codePrefix[3]) && char.IsNumber(str[2]) && char.IsNumber(str[3]))
			{
				int num = int.Parse(str.Substring(2, 2));
				int num2 = int.Parse(codePrefix.Substring(2, 2));
				if (num < num2)
				{
					if (!isInternal)
					{
						badCount++;
					}
					return false;
				}
			}
			for (int j = 8; j < 18; j++)
			{
				if (!char.IsDigit(str[j]))
				{
					if (!isInternal)
					{
						badCount++;
					}
					return false;
				}
			}
			int serialNumber = 0;
			bool flag = VerifyCDKey(str, ref serialNumber) != 0;
			if (!isInternal && !flag)
			{
				Thread.Sleep(1000);
				badCount++;
			}
			return flag;
		}

		private static bool IsCDKeyPrefixValid(string licKey)
		{
			if (licKey == null || licKey.Length < 7)
			{
				return false;
			}
			for (int i = 0; i < 6; i++)
			{
				char c = licKey[i];
				if (!char.IsDigit(c) && (i >= 2 || !char.IsLetter(c)))
				{
					return false;
				}
			}
			if (licKey[4] == '0' && licKey[5] == '0')
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (licKey[6] != '0' && !char.IsLetter(licKey[6]))
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (licKey[7] != '0' && licKey[7] != '1')
			{
				return false;
			}
			return true;
		}

		private static long LicenseCalcCheckSum(string str)
		{
			long num = 27L;
			long num2 = 31L;
			long value = 12765L;
			long value2 = 376L;
			for (int i = 0; i < str.Length; i++)
			{
				long num3 = str[i];
				num3 = num3 * 3 + num2;
				value2 += num * (num3 * num3);
				num2 = str[i];
				num2 *= 3;
				value += value2;
				num++;
				ReduceValue(ref value2, 999999, 697543);
				ReduceValue(ref value, 99999, 58567);
				ReduceValue(ref value, 9999, 9997);
			}
			return value;
		}

		internal static void ReduceValue(ref long value, int maximum, int factor)
		{
			if (value > maximum)
			{
				long num = value - maximum;
				long num2 = num / factor;
				if (num % factor != 0L)
				{
					num2++;
				}
				value -= num2 * factor;
			}
		}

		private static long VerifyCDKey(string licKey, ref int serialNumber)
		{
			char[] array = new char[7];
			CDMsk checkDigitMask = GetCheckDigitMask(licKey);
			int i = 0;
			bool flag = true;
			for (int j = 0; j < 4; j++)
			{
				int num;
				for (num = checkDigitMask.rgCP[j]; i + j + 8 < num; i++)
				{
					array[5 - i] = licKey[8 + j + i];
				}
				string str = new string(licKey.ToCharArray(0, num));
				long checkSum = LicenseCalcCheckSum(str);
				char c = licKey[num];
				if (c != GetCheckDigitFromSum(checkSum, checkDigitMask))
				{
					flag = false;
				}
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				long num2 = long.Parse(new string(array));
				num2 = ((num2 >= checkDigitMask.nF1) ? (num2 - checkDigitMask.nF1) : (num2 + (1000000 - checkDigitMask.nF1)));
				serialNumber = (int)num2;
			}
			if (flag)
			{
				return 3L;
			}
			Thread.Sleep(500);
			return 0L;
		}

		private static CDMsk GetCheckDigitMask(string licKey)
		{
			if (licKey == null || licKey.Length < 18)
			{
				throw new ArgumentException(SR.GetString("LE_ArgumentException_19"));
			}
			string str = licKey.Substring(0, 8);
			long num = LicenseCalcCheckSum(str);
			return s_CDM[num % s_CDM.Length];
		}

		private static char GetCheckDigitFromSum(long checkSum, CDMsk mask)
		{
			return (char)(48 + (checkSum + mask.nF3) % mask.nF2 % 10);
		}

		private int GetCalendarWeeksLeft()
		{
			return (daysRemaining + 2 - 1) / 2;
		}

		private static DateTime GetExpirationDate(int usageDaysRemaining)
		{
			int num = (usageDaysRemaining + 2 - 1) / 2;
			return DateTime.Today.AddDays(7.0 * (double)num);
		}

		private void LoadAllNewerProductInfo()
		{
			if (allNewerProducts != null)
			{
				return;
			}
			string licenseAssemblyInfo = GetLicenseAssemblyInfo();
			ArrayList arrayList = new ArrayList(3);
			ArrayList arrayList2 = new ArrayList(3);
			try
			{
				ObjectHandle objectHandle = Activator.CreateInstance(licenseAssemblyInfo, "Infragistics.License.LicenseValidator");
				object obj = objectHandle.Unwrap();
				Type type = obj.GetType();
				MethodInfo method = type.GetMethod("GetNewerVersions");
				object[] parameters = new object[5] { regKeyProductName, majorVersion, minorVersion, arrayList, arrayList2 };
				method.Invoke(obj, parameters);
				byte[] array = arrayList.ToArray(typeof(byte)) as byte[];
				byte[] array2 = arrayList2.ToArray(typeof(byte)) as byte[];
				if (array != null && array2 != null && array.Length == array2.Length)
				{
					allNewerProducts = new UltraProductInfo[array.Length];
					for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
					{
						byte b = array[i];
						byte b2 = array2[i];
						UltraProductInfo ultraProductInfo = new UltraProductInfo(base.ProductName, regKeyProductName, codePrefix, id, requiresActivation, base.Type, b, b2);
						allNewerProducts[i] = ultraProductInfo;
					}
				}
				else
				{
					allNewerProducts = new UltraProductInfo[0];
				}
			}
			catch
			{
			}
		}

		internal void DisplayPrompt()
		{
			if (promptDisplayed)
			{
				return;
			}
			if (HasPromptBeenDisplayed(this))
			{
				promptDisplayed = true;
				return;
			}
			string text = null;
			switch (status)
			{
			case LicenseStatus.ActivationGracePeriodValid:
				text = string.Format(SR.GetString("LicensedProductActivationGracePrompt"), daysRemaining, GetCalendarWeeksLeft(), base.ProductName);
				break;
			case LicenseStatus.TrialValid:
				text = string.Format(SR.GetString("LicensedProductTrialPeriodPrompt"), daysRemaining, GetCalendarWeeksLeft(), base.ProductName);
				break;
			default:
				return;
			}
			promptDisplayed = true;
			UpdatePromptStatus(this, hasBeenDisplayed: true);
			try
			{
				string caption = SR.GetString("LicensedProductPromptDialogTitle", base.ProductName);
				MessageBox.Show(text, caption, MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Exclamation);
			}
			catch
			{
			}
		}

		public override int GetHashCode()
		{
			return codePrefix.GetHashCode();
		}

		public override bool Equals(object obj)
		{
			if (obj is UltraProductInfo ultraProductInfo && codePrefix == ultraProductInfo.codePrefix && majorVersion == ultraProductInfo.majorVersion && minorVersion == ultraProductInfo.minorVersion && regKeyProductName == ultraProductInfo.regKeyProductName)
			{
				return id == ultraProductInfo.id;
			}
			return false;
		}
	}
	[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public sealed class UnlicensedProductInfo : UltraProductInfoBase
	{
		private string key = string.Empty;

		public override string Expiration => "N/A";

		public override bool RequiresActivation => false;

		public override LicenseStatus Status => LicenseStatus.Licensed;

		public override string Key => string.Empty;

		public override string CompanyName => string.Empty;

		public override string UserName => string.Empty;

		public override bool Expired => false;

		public UnlicensedProductInfo(string productName)
			: base(productName)
		{
		}

		public UnlicensedProductInfo(string productName, ProductType productType)
			: base(productName, productType)
		{
		}

		protected internal override bool IsKeyValid(string key)
		{
			if (key != null)
			{
				return key == this.key;
			}
			return true;
		}
	}
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public class UltraPropertyPageDialog : Form
	{
		private Button buttonOk;

		private Button buttonCancel;

		private Button buttonApply;

		private Container components;

		private object baseObject;

		private TabControl tabControlPropPages;

		private int lastSelectedTabIndex;

		private const string REG_KEY = "Software\\Infragistics\\Shared\\CustomPropertyDialog";

		private DesignerTransaction transaction;

		public UltraPropertyPageDialog()
		{
			InitializeComponent();
			buttonApply.Text = SR.GetString("UltraPropertyPageDialogButtonApply");
			buttonOk.Text = SR.GetString("UltraPropertyPageDialogButtonOk");
			buttonCancel.Text = SR.GetString("UltraPropertyPageDialogButtonCancel");
			base.StartPosition = FormStartPosition.CenterParent;
			Size size = (Size)SR.GetObject("CustomPropertiesDialogButtonSize");
			Point location = new Point(buttonApply.Right, buttonApply.Bottom);
			buttonApply.Size = (buttonOk.Size = (buttonCancel.Size = size));
			location.Offset(-size.Width, -size.Height);
			buttonApply.Location = location;
			location.Offset(-size.Width, 0);
			buttonCancel.Location = location;
			location.Offset(-size.Width, 0);
			buttonOk.Location = location;
		}

		public UltraPropertyPageDialog(object obj)
			: this()
		{
			InitializeDialog(obj);
		}

		public void InitializeDialog(object obj)
		{
			if (obj == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException();
			}
			Type type = obj.GetType();
			baseObject = obj;
			InitializeTransaction();
			Text = SR.GetString("UltraPropertyPageDialogTitle", type.Name);
			AddTab(SR.GetString("UltraPropertyPageCustomPropertyTabCaption"), new UltraObjectPropertiesPage());
			object[] customAttributes = type.GetCustomAttributes(typeof(UltraPropertyPageAttribute), inherit: true);
			if (customAttributes.Length == 0)
			{
				return;
			}
			object[] array = customAttributes;
			for (int i = 0; i < array.Length; i++)
			{
				UltraPropertyPageAttribute ultraPropertyPageAttribute = (UltraPropertyPageAttribute)array[i];
				Type ultraPropertyControlType = ultraPropertyPageAttribute.UltraPropertyControlType;
				Control control = null;
				try
				{
					if (ultraPropertyControlType == null)
					{
						string propertyPageTypeName = ultraPropertyPageAttribute.PropertyPageTypeName;
						int num = propertyPageTypeName.IndexOf(",");
						if (num == 0)
						{
							continue;
						}
						string typeName = propertyPageTypeName.Substring(0, num);
						string text = propertyPageTypeName.Substring(num + 1);
						text = text.Trim();
						ObjectHandle objectHandle = null;
						try
						{
							SecurityPermission securityPermission = new SecurityPermission(SecurityPermissionFlag.UnmanagedCode);
							securityPermission.Demand();
							objectHandle = Activator.CreateInstance(text, typeName);
						}
						catch
						{
						}
						if (objectHandle == null)
						{
							continue;
						}
						control = objectHandle.Unwrap() as Control;
					}
					else
					{
						control = Activator.CreateInstance(ultraPropertyPageAttribute.UltraPropertyControlType) as Control;
					}
				}
				catch
				{
					continue;
				}
				AddTab(ultraPropertyPageAttribute.TabCaption, control);
			}
		}

		private void InitializeTransaction()
		{
			try
			{
				if (!(baseObject is IComponent))
				{
					return;
				}
				IServiceProvider serviceProvider = ((IComponent)baseObject).Site;
				if (serviceProvider != null && serviceProvider.GetService(typeof(IDesignerHost)) is IDesignerHost designerHost)
				{
					transaction = designerHost.CreateTransaction(SR.GetString("TransactionDescription_CustomPropertyPages"));
					if (serviceProvider.GetService(typeof(IComponentChangeService)) is IComponentChangeService componentChangeService)
					{
						componentChangeService.OnComponentChanging(baseObject, null);
						componentChangeService.OnComponentChanged(baseObject, null, null, null);
					}
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
		}

		private void AddTab(string caption, Control ctrl)
		{
			if (ctrl != null && ctrl is UltraPropertyPageBase)
			{
				TabPage tabPage = new TabPage(caption);
				UltraPropertyPageBase ultraPropertyPageBase = (UltraPropertyPageBase)ctrl;
				ultraPropertyPageBase.DirtyStateChanged += OnDirtyStateChanged;
				ultraPropertyPageBase.InitializeControl(baseObject);
				tabPage.Controls.Add(ctrl);
				ctrl.Dock = DockStyle.Fill;
				tabPage.Tag = ctrl;
				tabControlPropPages.TabPages.Add(tabPage);
			}
		}

		protected override void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (disposing && components != null)
			{
				components.Dispose();
			}
			base.Dispose(disposing);
		}

		private void buttonApply_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			ApplyChanges();
			InitializeTransaction();
			buttonApply.Enabled = false;
			SelectNextControl(buttonApply, forward: true, tabStopOnly: true, nested: true, wrap: true);
		}

		private void ApplyChanges()
		{
			if (baseObject == null || tabControlPropPages.SelectedIndex == -1 || !(tabControlPropPages.SelectedTab.Tag is UltraPropertyPageBase))
			{
				return;
			}
			try
			{
				Cursor = Cursors.WaitCursor;
				base.Enabled = false;
				UltraPropertyPageBase ultraPropertyPageBase = tabControlPropPages.SelectedTab.Tag as UltraPropertyPageBase;
				if (ultraPropertyPageBase.IsDirty)
				{
					ultraPropertyPageBase.ApplyChanges();
				}
				if (transaction != null)
				{
					transaction.Commit();
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				base.Enabled = true;
				Cursor = Cursors.Default;
			}
		}

		private void buttonOk_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			ApplyChanges();
			Close();
		}

		private void CancelChanges()
		{
			if (baseObject == null || tabControlPropPages.SelectedIndex == -1 || !(tabControlPropPages.SelectedTab.Tag is UltraPropertyPageBase))
			{
				return;
			}
			IUltraPropertyPageDialogComponent ultraPropertyPageDialogComponent = baseObject as IUltraPropertyPageDialogComponent;
			try
			{
				Cursor = Cursors.WaitCursor;
				base.Enabled = false;
				UltraPropertyPageBase ultraPropertyPageBase = tabControlPropPages.SelectedTab.Tag as UltraPropertyPageBase;
				if (ultraPropertyPageBase.IsDirty)
				{
					ultraPropertyPageBase.CancelChanges();
				}
				if (transaction != null)
				{
					ultraPropertyPageDialogComponent?.BeforeCancelTransaction();
					transaction.Cancel();
				}
			}
			catch
			{
			}
			finally
			{
				base.Enabled = true;
				Cursor = Cursors.Default;
				ultraPropertyPageDialogComponent?.AfterCancelTransaction();
			}
		}

		private void buttonCancel_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			CancelChanges();
			Close();
		}

		private void OnDirtyStateChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (sender is UltraPropertyPageBase ultraPropertyPageBase)
			{
				buttonApply.Enabled = ultraPropertyPageBase.IsDirty;
			}
		}

		private void tabControlPropPages_SelectedIndexChanged(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			if (baseObject == null || tabControlPropPages.SelectedIndex == -1 || !(tabControlPropPages.SelectedTab.Tag is UltraPropertyPageBase))
			{
				return;
			}
			if (lastSelectedTabIndex != -1)
			{
				UltraPropertyPageBase ultraPropertyPageBase = tabControlPropPages.TabPages[lastSelectedTabIndex].Tag as UltraPropertyPageBase;
				if (ultraPropertyPageBase.IsDirty)
				{
					ultraPropertyPageBase.ApplyChanges();
				}
			}
			UltraPropertyPageBase ultraPropertyPageBase2 = tabControlPropPages.SelectedTab.Tag as UltraPropertyPageBase;
			ultraPropertyPageBase2.InitializeControl(baseObject);
			lastSelectedTabIndex = tabControlPropPages.SelectedIndex;
		}

		private void SaveSettingsToRegistry()
		{
			RegistryKey registryKey = null;
			try
			{
				registryKey = Registry.CurrentUser.CreateSubKey("Software\\Infragistics\\Shared\\CustomPropertyDialog");
				if (registryKey != null)
				{
					registryKey.SetValue("DialogX", base.Location.X);
					registryKey.SetValue("DialogY", base.Location.Y);
					registryKey.SetValue("DialogWidth", base.ClientSize.Width);
					registryKey.SetValue("DialogHeight", base.ClientSize.Height);
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				registryKey?.Close();
			}
		}

		private void ApplySettingsFromRegistry()
		{
			RegistryKey registryKey = null;
			try
			{
				registryKey = Registry.CurrentUser.OpenSubKey("Software\\Infragistics\\Shared\\CustomPropertyDialog");
				if (registryKey != null)
				{
					int num = Convert.ToInt32(registryKey.GetValue("DialogX", base.Left));
					int num2 = Convert.ToInt32(registryKey.GetValue("DialogY", base.Top));
					Point point = new Point(num, num2);
					Screen screen = Screen.FromPoint(point);
					if (screen.Bounds.Contains(point))
					{
						base.Location = point;
					}
					int num3 = Convert.ToInt32(registryKey.GetValue("DialogWidth", 584));
					int num4 = Convert.ToInt32(registryKey.GetValue("DialogHeight", 392));
					base.ClientSize = new Size(num3, num4);
				}
			}
			finally
			{
				registryKey?.Close();
			}
		}

		private void SaveState()
		{
			SaveSettingsToRegistry();
			foreach (TabPage tabPage in tabControlPropPages.TabPages)
			{
				if (tabPage.Tag is UltraPropertyPageBase ultraPropertyPageBase)
				{
					ultraPropertyPageBase.SaveState();
				}
			}
		}

		protected override void OnClosing(CancelEventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnClosing(e);
			if (!e.Cancel)
			{
				SaveState();
				if (transaction != null)
				{
					transaction.Cancel();
				}
			}
		}

		protected override void OnLoad(EventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnLoad(e);
			ApplySettingsFromRegistry();
		}
	}
	[ToolboxItem(false)]
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public abstract class UltraPropertyPageBase : UserControl
	{
		private bool isDirty;

		public bool IsDirty => isDirty;

		public event EventHandler DirtyStateChanged;

		public event EventHandler StateDirtied;

		public abstract void InitializeControl(object baseObject);

		public abstract void ApplyChanges();

		protected void Dirty()
		{
			if (this.StateDirtied != null)
			{
				this.StateDirtied(this, EventArgs.Empty);
			}
			if (!isDirty)
			{
				isDirty = true;
				OnDirtyStateChanged(EventArgs.Empty);
			}
		}

		protected virtual void OnDirtyStateChanged(EventArgs e)
		{
			if (this.DirtyStateChanged != null)
			{
				this.DirtyStateChanged(this, e);
			}
		}

		protected void Clean()
		{
			if (isDirty)
			{
				isDirty = false;
				OnDirtyStateChanged(EventArgs.Empty);
			}
		}

		public virtual void CancelChanges()
		{
		}

		protected internal virtual void SaveState()
		{
		}
	}
	[AttributeUsage(AttributeTargets.Class, AllowMultiple = true)]
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public class UltraPropertyPageAttribute : Attribute
	{
		private string tabCaption;

		private Type ultraPropControlType;

		private string propControlTypeName;

		public Type UltraPropertyControlType => ultraPropControlType;

		public virtual string TabCaption => tabCaption;

		internal string PropertyPageTypeName
		{
			get
			{
				if (ultraPropControlType != null)
				{
					return ultraPropControlType.FullName;
				}
				return propControlTypeName;
			}
		}

		public UltraPropertyPageAttribute(string tabCaption, Type ultraPropControlType)
		{
			if (ultraPropControlType == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException();
			}
			if (!ultraPropControlType.IsSubclassOf(typeof(UltraPropertyPageBase)))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException(SR.GetString("LE_ArgumentException_20"));
			}
			this.tabCaption = ((tabCaption == null) ? string.Empty : tabCaption);
			this.ultraPropControlType = ultraPropControlType;
		}

		public UltraPropertyPageAttribute(string tabCaption, string ultraPropControlType)
		{
			propControlTypeName = ultraPropControlType;
			this.tabCaption = tabCaption;
		}
	}
	[ToolboxItem(false)]
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public class UltraPropPagePropertyGrid : PropertyGrid, IServiceProvider
	{
		private ContextMenuStrip propertyGridContextMenu;

		private ToolStripMenuItem menuItemReset;

		private IComponent component;

		private bool CanResetSelectedItems
		{
			get
			{
				if (base.SelectedObjects == null || base.SelectedObjects.Length == 0 || base.SelectedGridItem == null)
				{
					return false;
				}
				object[] selectedObjects = base.SelectedObjects;
				foreach (object obj in selectedObjects)
				{
					try
					{
						GridItem selectedGridItem = base.SelectedGridItem;
						if (selectedGridItem.Parent != null)
						{
							if (!selectedGridItem.PropertyDescriptor.CanResetValue(selectedGridItem.Parent.Value))
							{
								return false;
							}
						}
						else if (!selectedGridItem.PropertyDescriptor.CanResetValue(obj))
						{
							return false;
						}
					}
					catch (InvalidCastException)
					{
						return false;
					}
					catch (Exception)
					{
					}
				}
				return true;
			}
		}

		public UltraPropPagePropertyGrid()
		{
			InitializeContextMenu();
			base.DrawFlatToolbar = true;
		}

		public UltraPropPagePropertyGrid(IComponent component)
			: this()
		{
			this.component = component;
		}

		public void Initialize(IComponent component)
		{
			this.component = component;
		}

		private void InitializeContextMenu()
		{
			menuItemReset = new ToolStripMenuItem();
			menuItemReset.Enabled = false;
			menuItemReset.Text = SR.GetString("CustomPropertiesPageMenuReset");
			menuItemReset.Click += menuItemReset_Click;
			propertyGridContextMenu = new ContextMenuStrip();
			propertyGridContextMenu.Items.AddRange(new ToolStripMenuItem[1] { menuItemReset });
			propertyGridContextMenu.RightToLeft = RightToLeft.No;
			propertyGridContextMenu.Opened += PropertyGridContextMenu_Popup;
			ContextMenuStrip = propertyGridContextMenu;
		}

		protected override object GetService(Type serviceType)
		{
			if (component != null && component.Site != null)
			{
				object service = component.Site.GetService(serviceType);
				if (service != null)
				{
					return service;
				}
			}
			return base.GetService(serviceType);
		}

		object IServiceProvider.GetService(Type serviceType)
		{
			return GetService(serviceType);
		}

		private void PropertyGridContextMenu_Popup(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			menuItemReset.Enabled = CanResetSelectedItems;
		}

		private void menuItemReset_Click(object sender, EventArgs e)
		{
			try
			{
				ResetSelectedPropertyWithEvents();
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				string message = ex.Message;
				Exception ex2 = ex;
				while (ex2.InnerException != null)
				{
					message = ex2.InnerException.Message;
					ex2 = ex2.InnerException;
				}
				MessageBox.Show(FindForm(), message, SR.GetString("PropertySetErrorCaption"), MessageBoxButtons.OK, MessageBoxIcon.Hand);
			}
		}

		protected override void Dispose(bool disposing)
		{
			if (propertyGridContextMenu != null)
			{
				propertyGridContextMenu.Dispose();
				propertyGridContextMenu = null;
			}
			base.Dispose(disposing);
		}

		internal void ResetSelectedPropertyWithEvents()
		{
			object value = base.SelectedGridItem.Value;
			ResetSelectedProperty();
			PropertyValueChangedEventArgs e = new PropertyValueChangedEventArgs(base.SelectedGridItem, value);
			OnPropertyValueChanged(e);
		}
	}
	public class UltraPropPagePropertyGridWithEventsTab : UltraPropPagePropertyGrid
	{
		private bool showEventsTab = true;

		public bool ShowEventsTab
		{
			get
			{
				return showEventsTab;
			}
			set
			{
				showEventsTab = value;
			}
		}

		public UltraPropPagePropertyGridWithEventsTab()
		{
			base.DrawFlatToolbar = true;
		}

		public UltraPropPagePropertyGridWithEventsTab(Component component)
			: base(component)
		{
		}

		protected override PropertyTab CreatePropertyTab(Type tabType)
		{
			if (tabType == typeof(EventsTab))
			{
				return new EventsTab(this);
			}
			return base.CreatePropertyTab(tabType);
		}

		protected override void OnSelectedObjectsChanged(EventArgs e)
		{
			base.OnSelectedObjectsChanged(e);
			try
			{
				base.PropertyTabs.RemoveTabType(typeof(EventsTab));
			}
			catch (Exception ex)
			{
				if (this == null)
				{
					throw ex;
				}
			}
			ShowEventsButton(value: false);
			if (ShowEventsTab)
			{
				base.PropertyTabs.AddTabType(typeof(EventsTab));
				ShowEventsButton(value: true);
			}
		}
	}

		{
			SystemEvents.UserPreferenceChanged += UserPreferenceChanged;
		}

		private static float ToRGBHelper(float rm1, float rm2, float rh)
		{
			if (rh > 360f)
			{
				rh -= 360f;
			}
			else if (rh < 0f)
			{
				rh += 360f;
			}
			if (rh < 60f)
			{
				rm1 += (rm2 - rm1) * rh / 60f;
			}
			else if (rh < 180f)
			{
				rm1 = rm2;
			}
			else if (rh < 240f)
			{
				rm1 += (rm2 - rm1) * (240f - rh) / 60f;
			}
			return rm1 * 255f;
		}

		public static Color ColorFromHLS(float hue, float luminance, float saturation)
		{
			int val;
			int val2;
			int val3;
			if ((double)saturation == 0.0)
			{
				val = (val2 = (val3 = (int)((double)luminance * 255.0)));
			}
			else
			{
				float num = ((!(luminance <= 0.5f)) ? (luminance + saturation - luminance * saturation) : (luminance + luminance * saturation));
				float rm = 2f * luminance - num;
				val = (int)ToRGBHelper(rm, num, hue + 120f);
				val2 = (int)ToRGBHelper(rm, num, hue);
				val3 = (int)ToRGBHelper(rm, num, hue - 120f);
			}
			val = Math.Max(0, Math.Min(255, val));
			val2 = Math.Max(0, Math.Min(255, val2));
			val3 = Math.Max(0, Math.Min(255, val3));
			return Color.FromArgb(val, val2, val3);
		}

		private static void ResetColors()
		{
			highColor = DisplaySupportsHighColor();
			toolBar = ColorFromHLS(SystemColors.Control.GetHue(), SystemColors.Control.GetBrightness() + 2f / 51f, SystemColors.Control.GetSaturation());
			menuDroppedBorder = ColorFromHLS(SystemColors.Menu.GetHue(), SystemColors.Menu.GetBrightness() - 11f / 51f, SystemColors.Menu.GetSaturation());

			toolSeparator = ColorFromHLS(SystemColors.Menu.GetHue(), SystemColors.Menu.GetBrightness() - 0.11764706f, SystemColors.Menu.GetSaturation());
			int num = 58;
			int num2 = 58;
			int num3 = 58;
			int num4 = (int)((double)(int)SystemColors.ControlText.R * 0.2);
			int num5 = (int)((double)(int)SystemColors.ControlText.G * 0.2);
			int num6 = (int)((double)(int)SystemColors.ControlText.B * 0.2);
			int num7 = (int)((double)(int)SystemColors.ControlDark.R * 0.5);
			int num8 = (int)((double)(int)SystemColors.ControlDark.G * 0.5);

			menuDroppedBorder = Color.FromArgb(num + num4 + num7, num2 + num5 + num8, num3 + num6 + num9);
			num = (int)((double)(int)SystemColors.ControlDark.R * 0.85);
			num2 = (int)((double)(int)SystemColors.ControlDark.G * 0.85);
			num3 = (int)((double)(int)SystemColors.ControlDark.B * 0.85);
			num4 = (int)((double)(int)SystemColors.ControlText.R * 0.15);

			num6 = (int)((double)(int)SystemColors.ControlText.B * 0.15);
			floaterBorder = Color.FromArgb(num + num4, num2 + num5, num3 + num6);
			num = (int)((double)(int)SystemColors.Control.R * 0.14);
			num2 = (int)((double)(int)SystemColors.Control.G * 0.14);
			num3 = (int)((double)(int)SystemColors.Control.B * 0.14);

			num5 = (int)((double)(int)SystemColors.Window.G * 0.86);

			num2 = (int)((double)(int)SystemColors.Highlight.G * 0.3);
			num3 = (int)((double)(int)SystemColors.Highlight.B * 0.3);

			highlightMouseDown = Color.FromArgb(num + num4, num2 + num5, num3 + num6);
			num = (int)((double)(int)SystemColors.Highlight.R * 0.1);

			num = 135;
			num2 = 135;
			num3 = 135;
			num4 = (int)((double)(int)SystemColors.Highlight.R * 0.2);
			num5 = (int)((double)(int)SystemColors.Highlight.G * 0.2);

			raisedPictureShadow = Color.FromArgb(num + num4, num2 + num5, num3 + num6);
		}

		private static bool DisplaySupportsHighColor()
		{
			IntPtr zero = IntPtr.Zero;
			try
			{
				SecurityPermission securityPermission = new SecurityPermission(SecurityPermissionFlag.UnmanagedCode);
				securityPermission.Assert();
				Graphics graphics = Graphics.FromHwnd(IntPtr.Zero);
				zero = graphics.GetHdc();
				try
				{
					if (zero != IntPtr.Zero)
					{
						int deviceCapsApi = NativeWindowMethods.GetDeviceCapsApi(zero, 12);
						if (deviceCapsApi <= 8)
						{
							return false;
						}
						return true;
					}
				}
				finally
				{
					if (zero != IntPtr.Zero)
					{
						graphics.ReleaseHdc(zero);
					}
					graphics?.Dispose();
				}
			}
			catch (Exception)
			{
			}
			return true;
		}
	}
}
namespace Infragistics.Shared.Serialization
{
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public class Binder : SerializationBinder
	{
		private struct AssemblyItem
		{
			private string assemblyName;

			private string typeName;

			public AssemblyItem(string assemblyName, string typeName)
			{
				this.assemblyName = assemblyName;
				this.typeName = typeName;
			}

			public override bool Equals(object obj)
			{
				if (obj is AssemblyItem assemblyItem)
				{
					if (assemblyItem.typeName == typeName)
					{
						return assemblyItem.assemblyName == assemblyName;
					}
					return false;
				}
				return base.Equals(obj);
			}

			public override int GetHashCode()
			{
				return typeName.GetHashCode();
			}
		}

		private Dictionary<AssemblyItem, Type> typeList = new Dictionary<AssemblyItem, Type>();

		[ThreadStatic]
		private static Hashtable assemblyTypes;

		[SecurityPermission(SecurityAction.Demand, SerializationFormatter = true)]
		public override Type BindToType(string assemblyName, string typeName)
		{
			AssemblyItem key = new AssemblyItem(assemblyName, typeName);
			if (!typeList.TryGetValue(key, out var value))
			{
				value = BindToTypeImpl(assemblyName, typeName);
				typeList.Add(key, value);
			}
			return value;
		}

		internal static Type BindToTypeImpl(string assemblyName, string typeName)
		{
			Type type = null;
			if (assemblyName == null || assemblyName.StartsWith("Infragistics.Shared"))
			{
				try
				{
					assemblyName = typeof(SubObjectBase).Assembly.FullName;
				}
				catch
				{
					assemblyName = "Infragistics4.Shared.v21.1";
				}
			}
			else
			{
				Type type2 = null;
				Assembly[] assemblies = GetAssemblies();
				Assembly[] array = assemblies;
				foreach (Assembly assembly in array)
				{
					type2 = FindType(assembly, assemblyName, typeName);
					if (type2 != null)
					{
						return type2;
					}
				}
			}
			string text = ((assemblyName == null || assemblyName.IndexOf(',') < 0 || !assemblyName.StartsWith("Infragistics")) ? assemblyName : assemblyName.Split(',')[0]);
			if (text.StartsWith("Infragistics") && text.EndsWith("21.1"))
			{
				type = Type.GetType($"{typeName}, {text}");
			}
			if (type == null)
			{
				Type type3 = null;
				Assembly[] assemblies2 = GetAssemblies();
				Assembly[] array2 = assemblies2;
				foreach (Assembly assembly2 in array2)
				{
					type3 = FindType(assembly2, null, typeName);
					if (type3 != null)
					{
						return type3;
					}
				}
			}
			type = Type.GetType($"{typeName}, {text}");
			if (type != null)
			{
				return type;
			}
			return Type.GetType($"{typeName}, {assemblyName}");
		}

		private static Type FindType(Assembly assembly, string assemblyName, string typeName)
		{
			if (assemblyName == null || assembly.GetName().ToString() == assemblyName || assembly.GetName().Name == assemblyName)
			{
				string name = assembly.GetName().Name;
				if (name != null && name.Length > 0)
				{
					if (assemblyTypes == null)
					{
						assemblyTypes = new Hashtable();
					}
					Hashtable hashtable = (Hashtable)assemblyTypes[name];
					if (hashtable == null)
					{
						Type[] array;
						if (assembly.ReflectionOnly)
						{
							array = new Type[0];
						}
						else
						{
							try
							{
								array = assembly.GetTypes();
							}
							catch (ReflectionTypeLoadException ex)
							{
								array = ex.Types;
							}
							catch
							{
								array = new Type[0];
							}
						}
						hashtable = (Hashtable)(assemblyTypes[name] = new Hashtable(array.Length));
						Type[] array2 = array;
						foreach (Type type in array2)
						{
							if (type != null)
							{
								hashtable.Add(type.FullName, type);
							}
						}
					}
					return (Type)hashtable[typeName];
				}
				if (assembly.ReflectionOnly)
				{
					return null;
				}
				Type[] array3 = null;
				try
				{
					array3 = assembly.GetTypes();
				}
				catch (ReflectionTypeLoadException ex2)
				{
					array3 = ex2.Types;
				}
				catch
				{
					array3 = null;
				}
				if (array3 != null)
				{
					Type[] array4 = array3;
					foreach (Type type2 in array4)
					{
						if (null != type2 && type2.FullName == typeName)
						{
							return type2;
						}
					}
				}
			}
			return null;
		}

		private static Assembly[] GetAssemblies()
		{
			Assembly[] assemblies = AppDomain.CurrentDomain.GetAssemblies();
			int num = 5;
			List<List<Assembly>> list = new List<List<Assembly>>(num);
			for (int i = 0; i < num; i++)
			{
				list.Add(new List<Assembly>());
			}
			Assembly[] array = assemblies;
			foreach (Assembly assembly in array)
			{
				int sortPriority = GetSortPriority(assembly);
				if (sortPriority >= 0 && sortPriority < list.Count)
				{
					list[sortPriority].Add(assembly);
				}
			}
			List<Assembly> list2 = new List<Assembly>();
			for (int k = 0; k < num; k++)
			{
				list2.AddRange(list[k]);
			}
			return list2.ToArray();
		}

		private static int GetSortPriority(Assembly assembly)
		{
			AssemblyName name = assembly.GetName();
			string name2 = name.Name;
			Version version = name.Version;
			Version version2 = Assembly.GetExecutingAssembly().GetName().Version;
			int num = 0;
			if (string.Compare(name2, "System.Drawing") == 0)
			{
				return num;
			}
			num++;
			if (name2.StartsWith("Infragistics"))
			{
				if (version2 != version)
				{
					num++;
				}
				return num;
			}
			num++;
			num++;
			if (name2.StartsWith("System"))
			{
				return num;
			}
			return num + 1;
		}
	}
	[Serializable]

	public struct TypeWrapper : IObjectReference
	{
		private string assemblyQualifiedName;

		private string fullName;

		public string AssemblyQualifiedName => assemblyQualifiedName;

		public string FullName => fullName;

		public TypeWrapper(Type type)
		{
			if (type == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException("type", "A valid type must be specified");
			}
			fullName = type.FullName;
			assemblyQualifiedName = type.AssemblyQualifiedName;
		}

		[SecurityPermission(SecurityAction.LinkDemand, Flags = SecurityPermissionFlag.SerializationFormatter)]
		object IObjectReference.GetRealObject(StreamingContext context)
		{
			return GetResolvedType();
		}

		internal Type GetResolvedType()
		{
			return Binder.BindToTypeImpl(assemblyQualifiedName, fullName);
		}
	}

		private static Cursor GetSerializedCursor(object objectValue)

				return objectValue as Cursor;
			}
			return (SystemCursors)(object)Utils.ConvertEnum(objectValue, SystemCursors.Default) switch
			{
				SystemCursors.AppStarting => Cursors.AppStarting, 
				SystemCursors.Arrow => Cursors.Arrow, 
				SystemCursors.Cross => Cursors.Cross, 
				SystemCursors.Default => Cursors.Default, 
				SystemCursors.Hand => Cursors.Hand, 
				SystemCursors.Help => Cursors.Help, 
				SystemCursors.HSplit => Cursors.HSplit, 
				SystemCursors.IBeam => Cursors.IBeam, 
				SystemCursors.No => Cursors.No, 
				SystemCursors.NoMove2D => Cursors.NoMove2D, 
				SystemCursors.NoMoveHoriz => Cursors.NoMoveHoriz, 
				SystemCursors.NoMoveVert => Cursors.NoMoveVert, 
				SystemCursors.PanEast => Cursors.PanEast, 
				SystemCursors.PanNE => Cursors.PanNE, 
				SystemCursors.PanNorth => Cursors.PanNorth, 
				SystemCursors.PanNW => Cursors.PanNW, 
				SystemCursors.PanSE => Cursors.PanSE, 
				SystemCursors.PanSouth => Cursors.PanSouth, 
				SystemCursors.PanSW => Cursors.PanSW,

				SystemCursors.VSplit => Cursors.VSplit, 
				SystemCursors.WaitCursor => Cursors.WaitCursor, 
				_ => null, 
			};
		}

	[Serializable]
	public class ObjectStreamer : ISerializable
	{
		private IDictionary dictionary;

		private int initialCapacity;

		private string assemblyName;

		public IDictionary Dictionary
		{
			get
			{
				if (dictionary == null)
				{
					dictionary = new Hashtable(initialCapacity);
				}
				return dictionary;
			}
		}

		public string AssemblyName => assemblyName;

		public ObjectStreamer()
			: this(10)
		{
		}

		public ObjectStreamer(int initialCapacity)
			: this(null, initialCapacity)
		{
		}

		public ObjectStreamer(string assemblyName)
			: this(assemblyName, 10)
		{
		}

		public ObjectStreamer(string assemblyName, int initialCapacity)
		{
			this.initialCapacity = initialCapacity;
			this.assemblyName = assemblyName;
		}

		protected ObjectStreamer(SerializationInfo info, StreamingContext context)
		{
			assemblyName = info.AssemblyName;
			SerializationInfoEnumerator enumerator = info.GetEnumerator();
			while (enumerator.MoveNext())
			{
				SerializationEntry current = enumerator.Current;
				string name = current.Name;
				if (name == "ObjectStreamerAssemblyName")
				{
					assemblyName = (string)Utils.DeserializeProperty(current, typeof(string), assemblyName);
				}
				else
				{
					Dictionary.Add(current.Name, current);
				}
			}
		}

		public object GetValue(object key)
		{
			object obj = Dictionary[key];
			if (obj is SerializationEntry serializationEntry)
			{
				return serializationEntry.Value;
			}
			return obj;
		}

		[SecurityPermission(SecurityAction.LinkDemand, Flags = SecurityPermissionFlag.SerializationFormatter)]
		void ISerializable.GetObjectData(SerializationInfo info, StreamingContext context)
		{
			if (assemblyName != null && assemblyName.Length > 0)
			{
				info.AssemblyName = assemblyName;
				Utils.SerializeProperty(info, "ObjectStreamerAssemblyName", assemblyName);
			}
			foreach (DictionaryEntry item in Dictionary)
			{
				string name = ((item.Key != null) ? item.Key.ToString() : string.Empty);
				Utils.SerializeProperty(info, name, GetValue(item.Key));
			}
		}
	}
	[Serializable]
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public struct ObjectWrapper : IObjectReference
	{
		[Browsable(false)]
		[EditorBrowsable(EditorBrowsableState.Never)]
		public object objectValue;

		public object Value => objectValue;

		public ObjectWrapper(object value)
		{
			if (!Utils.IsSafelySerializable(value))
			{
				throw new NotSupportedException("Only simple types may be wrapped");
			}
			objectValue = value;
		}

		[SecurityPermission(SecurityAction.LinkDemand, Flags = SecurityPermissionFlag.SerializationFormatter)]
		object IObjectReference.GetRealObject(StreamingContext context)
		{
			return objectValue;
		}

		public override string ToString()
		{
			if (Value != null)
			{
				return $"ObjectWrapper: Value = {Value.ToString()}, Type = {Value.GetType().ToString()}";
			}
			return base.ToString();
		}
	}

		public object Serializer => serializer;

				handled = value;
			}
		}

		public object CodeObject
		{
			get
			{
				return codeObject;
			}
			set
			{
				codeObject = value;

			this.manager = manager;
		}
	}
	[ComVisible(false)]
	public class Utils
	{
		internal static bool UseTypeWrapperForAllTypes { get; set; }

		private Utils()
		{
		}

		public static string ConvertToString(object value, bool useCurrentCulture)
		{
			return ConvertToString(value, null, useCurrentCulture);
		}

		public static string ConvertToString(object value, string format, bool useCurrentCulture)
		{
			CultureInfo formatProvider = (useCurrentCulture ? CultureInfo.CurrentCulture : CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
			return ConvertToString(value, format, formatProvider);
		}

		public static string ConvertToString(object value, IFormatProvider formatProvider)
		{
			return ConvertToString(value, null, formatProvider);
		}

		public static string ConvertToString(object value, string format, IFormatProvider formatProvider)
		{
			if (value == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (formatProvider == null)
			{
				formatProvider = CultureInfo.CurrentCulture;
			}
			if (value is IFormattable formattable)
			{
				return formattable.ToString(format, formatProvider);
			}
			CultureInfo cultureInfo = formatProvider as CultureInfo;
			if (cultureInfo == null)
			{
				cultureInfo = CultureInfo.CurrentCulture;
			}
			return ConvertValue(value, typeof(string), cultureInfo) as string;
		}

		public static object ConvertValue(object value, Type destinationType)
		{
			return ConvertValue(value, destinationType, useCurrentCulture: false);
		}

		public static object ConvertValue(object value, Type destinationType, bool useCurrentCulture)
		{
			CultureInfo culture = (useCurrentCulture ? CultureInfo.CurrentCulture : CultureInfo.InvariantCulture);
			return ConvertValue(value, destinationType, culture);
		}

		public static object ConvertValue(object value, Type destinationType, CultureInfo culture)
		{
			if (destinationType == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException(SR.GetString("LE_ArgumentNullException_2"));
			}
			if (value == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			value = UnwrapObjectReference(value);
			if (destinationType.IsGenericType && destinationType.GetGenericTypeDefinition() == typeof(Nullable<>))
			{
				destinationType = Nullable.GetUnderlyingType(destinationType);
			}
			Type type = value.GetType();
			if (type == destinationType || destinationType.IsAssignableFrom(type))
			{
				return value;
			}
			if (culture == null)
			{
				culture = CultureInfo.CurrentCulture;
			}
			if (value is IConvertible && (destinationType.IsPrimitive || destinationType == typeof(string) || destinationType == typeof(DateTime)))
			{
				return Convert.ChangeType(value, destinationType, culture);
			}
			TypeConverter converter = TypeDescriptor.GetConverter(destinationType);
			if (!converter.CanConvertFrom(type))
			{
				TypeConverter converter2 = TypeDescriptor.GetConverter(type);
				if (converter2.CanConvertTo(destinationType))
				{
					return converter2.ConvertTo(null, culture, value, destinationType);
				}
				if (destinationType.IsEnum && type.IsValueType)
				{
					return Enum.ToObject(destinationType, value);
				}
				return value;
			}
			return converter.ConvertFrom(null, culture, value);
		}

		public static Enum ConvertEnum(object value, Enum defaultValue)
		{
			if (value == null)
			{
				return defaultValue;
			}
			Type type = value.GetType();
			Type type2 = defaultValue.GetType();
			if (type.IsEnum && type != type2)
			{
				return defaultValue;
			}
			object value2 = ((!(type == type2)) ? DisposableObject.ConvertValue(value, Enum.GetUnderlyingType(type2)) : value);
			if (Enum.IsDefined(type2, value2))
			{
				return (Enum)Enum.ToObject(type2, value2);
			}
			object[] customAttributes = type2.GetCustomAttributes(typeof(FlagsAttribute), inherit: false);
			if (customAttributes.Length != 0 && customAttributes[0] is FlagsAttribute)
			{
				return (Enum)Enum.ToObject(type2, value2);
			}
			return defaultValue;
		}

		public static bool IsSimpleType(object value)
		{
			if (value == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return IsSimpleType(value.GetType());
		}

		public static bool IsSimpleType(Type type)
		{
			if (type == typeof(string) || type == typeof(decimal) || type == typeof(DateTime) || type == typeof(DBNull))
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (type.IsPrimitive)
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public static bool IsSafelySerializable(object value)
		{
			if (value == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			return IsSafelySerializable(value.GetType());
		}

		public static bool IsSafelySerializable(Type type)
		{
			if (type == null)
			{
				return false;
			}
			if (type.IsSerializable)
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (IsSimpleType(type))
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (type == typeof(Size) || type == typeof(Rectangle) || type == typeof(Point))
			{
				return true;
			}
			if (type == typeof(Icon) || type == typeof(Image) || type.IsSubclassOf(typeof(Image)) || type.IsSubclassOf(typeof(Icon)))
			{
				return true;
			}
			return false;
		}

		public static bool IsSafelyInfragisticsSerializable(Type type)
		{
			if (!IsSafelySerializable(type))
			{
				return false;
			}
			object[] customAttributes = type.GetCustomAttributes(typeof(InfragisticsSerializable), inherit: true);
			if (customAttributes.Length == 0)
			{
				return false;
			}
			InfragisticsSerializable infragisticsSerializable = customAttributes[0] as InfragisticsSerializable;
			return infragisticsSerializable.Serializable;
		}

		public static void SerializeObjectProperty(SerializationInfo info, string name, object value)
		{
			if (value != null && info != null)
			{
				if (value is Type && (UseTypeWrapperForAllTypes || DisposableObject.HasSamePublicKey((Type)value)))
				{
					info.AddValue(name, new TypeWrapper((Type)value));
				}
				else if (IsSafelySerializable(value))
				{
					ObjectWrapper objectWrapper = new ObjectWrapper(value);
					info.AddValue(name, objectWrapper);
				}
			}
		}

		public static object DeserializeObjectProperty(SerializationInfo info, string name)
		{
			if (info == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException(SR.GetString("LE_ArgumentNullException_4"));
			}
			return DeserializeObjectHelper(info.GetValue(name, typeof(object)));
		}

		public static object DeserializeObjectProperty(SerializationEntry entry)
		{
			return DeserializeObjectHelper(entry.Value);
		}

		internal static object DeserializeObjectHelper(object value)
		{
			if (value == null)
			{
				return null;
			}
			if (value is ObjectWrapper objectWrapper)
			{
				if (objectWrapper.Value != null && !IsSafelySerializable(((ObjectWrapper)value).Value))
				{
					return null;
				}
				return (IObjectReference)value;
			}
			if (value != null && !IsSafelySerializable(value))
			{
				return null;
			}
			return value;
		}

		public static void SerializeProperty(SerializationInfo info, string name, object value)
		{
			if (info == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException(SR.GetString("LE_ArgumentNullException_4"));
			}
			if (value != null)
			{
				if (value is Enum)
				{
					Type underlyingType = Enum.GetUnderlyingType(value.GetType());
					info.AddValue(name, ((IConvertible)value).ToType(underlyingType, CultureInfo.InvariantCulture));
				}
				else if (value is Cursor)
				{
					info.AddValue(name, new CursorWrapper(value as Cursor));
				}
				else if (value is Type && (UseTypeWrapperForAllTypes || DisposableObject.HasSamePublicKey((Type)value)))
				{
					info.AddValue(name, new TypeWrapper((Type)value));
				}
				else
				{
					info.AddValue(name, value);
				}
			}
		}

		public static object DeserializeProperty(SerializationEntry entry, Type destinationType, object defaultValue)
		{
			bool succeeded;
			return DeserializeProperty(entry, destinationType, defaultValue, out succeeded);
		}

		public static object DeserializeProperty(SerializationEntry entry, Type destinationType, object defaultValue, out bool succeeded)
		{
			object result = defaultValue;
			if (destinationType == null)
			{
				throw new ArgumentNullException(SR.GetString("LE_ArgumentNullException_2"));
			}
			if (defaultValue == null)
			{
				if (destinationType.IsValueType)
				{
					throw new ArgumentException(SR.GetString("LE_ArgumentException_21"));
				}
			}
			else if (defaultValue.GetType() != destinationType && !defaultValue.GetType().IsSubclassOf(destinationType))
			{
				throw new ArgumentException(SR.GetString("LE_ArgumentException_21"));
			}
			try
			{
				result = (destinationType.IsEnum ? ConvertEnum(entry.Value, defaultValue as Enum) : ((!(entry.Value is CursorWrapper) || !(destinationType == typeof(Cursor))) ? ConvertValue(entry.Value, destinationType) : entry.Value));
				succeeded = true;
			}
			catch
			{
				succeeded = false;
			}
			return result;
		}

		public static bool DeserializeProperty<T>(SerializationEntry entry, ref T field)
		{
			Type typeFromHandle = typeof(T);
			try
			{
				if (typeof(T).IsEnum)
				{
					field = (T)(object)ConvertEnum(entry.Value, field as Enum);
				}
				else if (entry.Value is CursorWrapper && typeFromHandle == typeof(Cursor))
				{
					field = (T)entry.Value;
				}
				else
				{
					field = (T)ConvertValue(entry.Value, typeFromHandle);
				}
				return true;
			}
			catch
			{
				return false;
			}
		}

		public static T DeserializeProperty<T>(SerializationEntry entry, T defaultValue)
		{
			bool succeeded;
			return DeserializeProperty(entry, defaultValue, out succeeded);
		}

		public static T DeserializeProperty<T>(SerializationEntry entry, T defaultValue, out bool succeeded)
		{
			succeeded = DeserializeProperty(entry, ref defaultValue);
			return defaultValue;
		}

		private static object UnwrapObjectReference(object entryValue)
		{
			if (entryValue is IObjectReference objectReference)
			{
				entryValue = objectReference.GetRealObject(default(StreamingContext));
			}
			return entryValue;
		}
	}
}
